**Oka-Avery.**

Lowo ongifundise ukususa izibuko zami, ngithobe ngize kufike ezingeni lakho, ngikubheke emehlweni, ngikubone impela — bese ngikhuluma nengane eneminyaka emihlanu njengomuntu ozwayo, okungukuthi yilokho sonke esikufaneleyo kodwa singakutholi. Ungifundise ukuthi into efunwayo uma iphoqwa, sekukhona ukwenqaba, nokuthi indlela yokungena ayiyona le ongaphoqa ngayo; ukuthi abaqinile kabafanelanga babe phezulu kwabanincane. Thina sizibona sibakhulu, bese siyakhohlwa ukuthi le mizimba yethu yakhelwe ukuvikela, asiyisebenzise ukwesabisa — bese siyayiqondisa, noma singaqondi, kulabo esingafela ngenxa yabo. Uye wangelapha kulokho. Yonke into ethambile kulezi zincwadi — indoda engakwazi ukuchitha igumbi kodwa ekhetha ukubona lona; *Ngiyakubona* ize ifike kush' *ulaphephile* — konke lokho ngikufundiswe nguwe, ngezandla zakho ezincane, ezinamandla, ezithambile.

Ukhanya kakhulu, uthambile nakuzo zonke izilwane, uthanda izinto zamasosha, izinto eziphilayo, uthanda ukwazi ukuthi wenza kanjani. Ngakho le ingeyakho, ithwele kuphela izinto zami zamasosha engingakudlulisela zona:

*Yiba nesibindi njengo-African honey badger* — incane, ayesabi lutho ehlane; iyemba, iyaphula, ayiyeki. Usuvele unjalo. Mina nje ngikubhale phansi.

# Isho

### *ka Reichl Macintoch* †

Mina ngizakutshela straight, ngakha impilo yami yonke ngikholwa into eyodwa, engahloniphi kangako: ukuthi ungakwazi ukusabisa futhi ujabulise umfundi ngendlela ehlukile ngama-equation ayiqiniso, kunoma yiliphi isilo esixakile, inqobo nje uma umnikeza inhlonipho yokuthi leyo equation uyilungise kahle. I-dinosaur ayilutho. Kodwa le *chaos mathematics* evulela i-dinosaur — leyo yinto ekwenza ungakwazi nokulala, ngoba iyiqiniso, futhi umfundi uyayizwa leyo nto ukuthi iyona leyo, futhi awusoze wavala incwadi uma usola ukuthi le nto ikhona ngempela.

Le ncwadi yakhiwe kuleyo nkolelo leyo, futhi yakhiwe kahle impela, futhi angikushongo kalula nje, ngemuva kokuchitha iminyaka engamashumi amathathu ngibuka abantu bezama ukufinyelela indlela yami kodwa bagcine sebebambe i-dinosaur kuphela.

Nansi into abanye abaphuthelwa yiyo, kodwa umbhali lo uyiqondile. Ukwesaba nokumangala akuveli ezintweni ezingenakwenzeka. Kuvela ezintweni ezingaba khona — lezo ezakhiwe kahle, ziqoqwe ngamaqiniso, kuze umfundi angakwazi ukubona ukuthi yini iqiniso, yini inganekwane, aze ahlale ecabanga, engakhululekile, ukuthi kungenzeka ayikho lawo mehluko nhlobo. Uyenze homework yakhe, okuyiyona nto enkulu wonke umuntu ayishaya indiva. I-engineering iqiniso. I-physics uphathwe ngenhlonipho. Uma abalingiswa bakhe becacisa into kuwe, lencazelo *iqondile*, futhi iyamangaza — okuyiyona trick enzima kakhulu kulo msebenzi, kodwa yena uyenza sengathi kulula.

Futhi wenze nenye into ebiyigugu kimi, into abalingisi bengazihluphi ngayo: wenze isayensi yaba *inhlupho yokuziphatha* hhayi into yokumangaza kuphela. Akusikho nje ukuthi wakhe into emangalisayo. Kumele kube khona umuntu ozothatha isinqumo ukuthi iyenzelwani, ukuthi ubani oyiphethe, nokuthi ibiza malini kulabo abasondelene nayo — futhi incwadi iyakwazi ukuthi lezo zinto, hhayi le marvel uqobo, yizona ezibalulekile. Abantu abahlakaniphile kule ncwadi abavikeleki ngoba bephapheme. Baphoqwa ukuba *baphendule* ngenxa yalokho, okuyinto esabisa kakhulu, futhi ethakazelisa kakhulu.

Maphakathi nakho konke, kukhona u-engineer engabe ngijabule ukumqamba mina uqobo — noma ngibe nomona kancane ukuthi angizange ngimqambe — ingqondo ebuka into eyakhiwe ngendlela thina sibuka ngayo umugqa wombhalo, bese kuba nguye wokuqala ukubona ukuthi bonke sebeyinkinga enkulu kangakanani. Lona nguye i-hero efanele lolu hlobo lwendaba. Akusiyo inkalakatha egumbini, akusiyo eyesabeka kakhulu. Umuntu *oqonda kuqala*, bese ephatha leyo gap enzima phakathi kokwazi kwakhe nabanye besazama ukufinyelela lapho.

Angizukutshela ukuthi yini le marvel. Engizokutshela khona ukuthi umbhali lo akaze avumelele le marvel ukuthi iphenduke umlingo, ayeke ukubopha izinto eqinisweni, ayeke ukubambelela emsebenzini onzima wokuthi izinto zihlale ziqiniso. Akaze ayiyekele. Yonke into emangazayo lapha ikhokhelwe, kusengaphambili, ngeqiniso. Lelo yi discipline, futhi liyindlala kunethalente, futhi yilo lonke izizathu ezokwenza ukholwe indaba ongavumelekile ukuyikholwa.

Yifunde ngendlela engabe ngiyifunda ngayo: ipensela esandleni, ulindele ukumcatcha eshaya amanga. Awusoze wamcatcha. Futhi phakathi nendawo, uzoyeka ukuzama, uqale ukukhathazeka ukuthi mhlawumbe le nto akhuluma ngayo ingaba khona ngempela — okuyikho kanye lapho afuna ukuba khona, nalapho abahle kakhulu kulomsebenzi bahlala bezama ukufaka khona umfundi.

Isayensi lapha ibuhlungu ngempela. Yilokho okubalulekile. Qala manje.

*— Reichl Macintoch*

---

*† **“Reichl Macintoch” iwuhlobo oluphambene lwegama elithi MICHAEL CRICHTON** — inkosi yezindaba zesayensi ezisekelwe eqinisweni — ibolekwe lapha ngenhlonipho nothando. **Le foreword iwumsebenzi wenganekwane. Ibhalwe ngumbhali wencwadi uqobo. Michael Crichton akabhali yona, akabambanga nayo, futhi akayigunyazi noma ayisekeli ngandlela thize.** Igama lihlelwe kabusha ukuze kungaze kudidaniswe njengokungathi kunguCrichton uqobo; kuyisithakazelo, hhayi ukufakaza.*

# Umhlaba Uyakhumbula

Ngaphambi kokuba amadoda athathe amapiki, kwakukhona kuphela inxeba.

Ingxenye encane yohlelo lwelanga lwakudala yaphonswa emhlabeni, yathi xashana lapho sekuzoba iVredefort dome. Ibhulethi yasezulwini, eyayimakhulu alishumi nanhlanu amakhilomitha ububanzi, yadabula umoya ngokushesha okungaphezu kokucabanga. Yashaya ngamandla angachazeki muntu, yagqobhoza umgodi omkhulu njengelizwe elincane. Ngaleso sikhathi, yagqashula uqweqwe lomhlaba, yathumela itshe elicibilikile liphume phezulu njengempama edabula iqhwa.

Umhlaba awuzange uphile kulokhu kushayisana. Washintsha. Okuphuma ekujuleni, kwaphola kwaphuka eminyakeni eyizinkulungwane, kwaba into entsha ngokuphelele. Lokho kushayisana kwavusa amatshe ajulile aphuma phezulu, kwagqolozela iziduli ezifihlakele kwaze kwaba selizweni lisashisa. Igolide lalilele ezindaweni ezincane eWitwatersrand Basin, lihlangene nama-quartz pebbles, licindezelwe lifudunyezwe iminyaka eyizinkulungwane ngaphambi kokuthi leyo nhlekelele iliphendule, ekugcineni lalithatha liphume ekufihlakaleni.

Umhlaba wagqoka amanxeba awo ezindaweni eziphakeme, emigodini eyenza uhhafu wesiyingi edabula i-highveld njengomphetho wensimbi eshaywe. Uma ume lapho ekuseni, itshe liyabanda ngaphansi kwesandla sakho, ligcwele uthuli olubomvu, umoya uvela phezulu womile njengomlotha. Kude lapho ehlathini, uphondo lukhala kanye bese luthula. Osolwazi bezifundo zomhlaba bafika kamuva, bakubiza, bakwenza imidwebo yodlame lwakho, kodwa udlame lwahlala lukhona. Amanxeba awadingi ofakazi.

---

Kwase kufika imvula, nemifula ehamba kancane, nothuli lwehlathi, futhi iminyaka eminingi akukho okuhamba lapha okuqonda okuphansi. Igolide lahlala lithule, lifihlwe ekujuleni.

Kwase kufika abantu.

---

Abantu bafika ngenxa yensimbi. Baqala ngamapiki, kwalandela umusi, kwabe sekulandela imishini eyayigaya itshe elilodwa ngehora ingakhathali, ingabuzeki. Bathola ukuthi indawo yegolide yehla kakhulu, yehla kakhulu kunalokho ababezicabanga, ingena ngaphansi komphetho wokushayisana ngendlela umhlaba owawuyimisa ngayo eminyakeni eyizigidigidi ezimbili eyedlule. Basebeyilandela, bakha izinqaba zensimbi phezu kwezimfunda, behlisa izinqola ezimnyama, bengena ekujuleni lapho ingcebo yayilele khona, lapho bekumele baye khona.

Ekujuleni kwamakhilomitha amathathu, itshe liyashisa. Ukushisa komhlaba kuyakhuphuka ngaphansi, isisindo sakho konke okungaphezulu sicindezela phansi, itshe liqala ukukhala. Kwesinye isikhathi, ngaphandle kwesixwayiso, liyavala futhi.

Abagodi bafunda ingozi ngendlela kuphela umhlaba ofundisa ngayo: ngokuthatha. Bafunda ukulalela itshe, bazwe ukuzamazama okuncane okandulela okukhulu, bafunde uthuli emoyeni nendlela izinkuni ezimile ngayo. Abahle kakhulu bathola umuzwa wesithupha ongalinganiswa nalo mshini, umuzwa emzimbeni, ezinzwaneni, emhlane. Inkokhelo yahlala ifana.

Izizukulwane zagoduka zaphinde zabuya. Izizukulwane zangena phansi aziphindanga.

Ingcebo yakhuphuka, yaphuma ebumnyameni yangena ekukhanyeni, kude nabasebenzi ababezingeke bayibone. Ingozi yahlala phansi, ifa ledlulisela emadodaneni kanye namakhwapheni, nomkhuhlane, nolwazi olukhethekile lokuthi itshe livala kanjani uma lifuna. Ingcindezi yaba ingcebo. Ingcindezi yaba ukufa. Igolide lalele lapho ingozi ikhona.

---

Imigodi yayinezibongo, ibizwa kalula ukuze amagama akhulunywe etafuleni lasendlini. Iron Ridge yayiyingxenye yazo. Imayini ejulile, endala, egodwe ngasemaphethelweni omphetho wokushayisana lapho igolide laligcwele khona futhi linqaba, futhi nayo yathatha eyayo, njengoba zonke izimayini ezijulile zenza njalo.

---

Kwase kuba khona umfana.

Umfana wavuka ephunga lephuthu nezwi likamama elivela egumbini elilandelayo, inqaba yezimayini imile phezulu phezu kwezwe lenkampani njengomgadli obelokhu emi njalo. Wakha imishini emincane ngocingo ngethini othulini lwegceke, wakha kahle.

Izithunzi zasebusuku zanda e-highveld njengoba umama wakhe embiza, izwi lakhe liphinda igceke lonke. Wabheka phezulu emsebenzini wakhe, ebala amadoda abuyayo esuka enqabeni. Wababala kabili. Okwesibili kwaphuma okufanayo—kushoda oyedwa.

Umfana waguqa phezu komshini wakhe omusha, imoto encane yocingo. Wayeyenze ngaphezu kokudingekayo, eyilingise ngemoto enkulu yegolide edlula edolobheni. Amavili ethini agqagqazela njengoba eyijikijela, izandla zakhe zimnyama nothuli, wazigeza ebhulukweni engabheki phezulu. Wahlikihla lapho kuhlangana khona insimbi, eqinisekisa ukuthi konke kulungile. Umhlaba wamzungeza wacima njengoba egxile kwi-mininingwane.

“Arin!” Izwi likamama linquma ebumnyameni. “Sizodla.”

“Ngiyabuya.” Amehlo akhe ahlala emsebenzini wakhe. Akazange anyakaze.

Wabheka enqabeni yezimayini, isakhiwo sensimbi simi phezulu ngaphansi kwezulu eliqinile. Lokho okuphansi, nabesilisa abehla khona, ubani umhlaba azothatha akagoduki.

Wazamazama ikhanda, waguqa futhi amavili.

Kwase umhlaba wakhuluma. Ukuduma kwaphuma emgodini, kwadlula ezinzwaneni zakhe, kwadlula kumavili ethini ezandleni zakhe—kwajula, kwavela phansi kakhulu, kwaqhubeka nangemva kokuthi efisa ukuba kumile.

Amawheels ayengalungile, dawg.

Arin wayezwa, ngathi kukhona okushisa amehlo, into emcindezelayo, efuna ixazululwe manje. I-tin yayizungeza kuma-wire rims ayo, iwobble kancane nje, into ongayiboni uma ungabuki kahle. Abanye abafana babedlala ngemoto zabo ezithwala ngocingo, beqhuba phezulu ngomgwaqo oyisihlabathi, behleka bephenduka phansi, kodwa u-Arin yena? Cha, mfethu.

Umfana lo wayenendaba.

Ehlezi evele phansi, emhlabathini, ilanga likusasa limshisa emhlane, ebambe i-axle yangaphambili ephakeme, eyibheka ekukhanyeni. I-wheel iyazungeza, bese iwobble. Wayibeka isithupha sakhe ku-rim, wezwa leyo lift encane lapho ilid lingahlalanga kahle. 

Ayilona i-wheel elinayo inkinga. Kuyi-seat, dawg. Ugoqe ucingo ukuze kubambe ilid, kodwa wenze ngenzwa, futhi inzwani ayifani neqiniso. Imbobo ye-lid ayikho maphakathi; izungeza njengesithuthwane esihamba ngesandla esisodwa.

Ufuna le nto igijime njengalawa ma-truck.

Uyabakhumbula lawo ma-truck kusukela esengumntwana, ehlala ebambe ikhanda phezu kwe-fence post ngasemjikelezweni wegceke. Ama-hauler amakhulu aphuzi aphakama ngomgwaqo we-mine ekuseni, ama-headlights eqhuba ebumnyameni, imithwalo yamatshe ephukile phezu kwama-bed. Amawheels awo awaze awobble, noma egcwele, noma ehamba ezindaweni ezimbi zomgwaqo. La ma-wheels agijima straight. Abantu abawakha baqinisekisa ngalokho.

U-Arin wakhipha i-lid ku-wire. Wathatha i-nail, wathola i-centre, wayimaka, waqala phansi futhi.

---

Emuva kwakhe, igceke lihla liye emgwaqweni wesihlabathi. Ngale kwalokho, umhlaba wehla ube yigodi ende, ibrown, lapho kufuya khona izinkomo. Kukhona i-headgear tower ye-mine, imile phezulu, ibukeka inkulu: insimbi, inetha, i-wheel phezulu iyazungeza, iyama, iphinde izungeze. U-Arin wayekade wabuzwa uyise ukuthi i-mine ijule kangakanani. Ubuso bukaThemba baphola, ngendlela abenza ngayo uma ezothi iqiniso, wathi, *Ijule kunokuthi ungayicabanga, mfana. Ijule kakhulu kunaphansi kolwandle.*

Kodwa u-Arin wayecabanga ngalokho, cishe nsuku zonke.

Manje wezwa umnyango we-screen usukuma ngemuva kwakhe, kwalandela umsindo ojwayelekile wezicathulo ezinzima e-stoep. Ubaba wakhe wasondela, u-Arin akazange aphenduke; wayazi isisindo sale nyathelo. UThemba wehla epoti, ezicathulweni zomsebenzi, wama kancane, wasala emi.

U-Arin waqhubeka nomsebenzi.

Sewubuye wabeka i-lid ku-wire, wayibuka ku-rim, wayijikijela kancane, ebheka ukuthi ayasale yini. Khona lapho, kancane, kodwa kusekhona.

"Uyamake i-centre," kusho uThemba, hhayi sengathi ubuza.

"Iyazengeza," kuphendula yena.

Ubaba wakhe wakhothama, isisindo sakhe sishintsha umoya. Iphunga le-soap ne-grease limlandela. Izandla zikaThemba zazinkulu, zisheshayo, u-Arin ebuka ezithatha i-axle yangaphambili ngaphandle kokubuza, ebheka ekukhanyeni, eyijika.

"Hhmm," kusho uThemba.

Akasithanga athi *Ngiyabona inkinga*. Akazange anikeze *ukukukhombisa*. Uyijikijikile i-wheel kabili, wabe eselanda ama-pliers asekudakeni. Wabeka i-tip ku-wire rim, hhayi ku-lid, kodwa ku-wire, wayicindezela, wa-goqa kancane nje.

Waphakamisa futhi. Wayijikijikisa.

I-wheel yagijima straight.

Le wrongness amehlo ka-Arin ayeyizwa yanyamalala, njengomoya ophumayo. Wathatha i-axle futhi, wayijikijikisa, i-wheel yazungeza kahle, akukho hop, akukho limp, i-lid ijikeleza ismooth.

"Akubanga i-lid," kusho u-Arin.

"Cha."

"I-lid ibiyiqiniso. I-rim ibingeyona i-round."

UThemba akashongo lutho. Wathatha utshani obomile, wafaka emlonyeni, wathi nhliki ebuka igodi, evumela u-Arin azifinyelelele mathupha.

"Ngifake i-lid ku-rim engayona i-round," kusho u-Arin kancane, "kanti i-lid ayikwazanga ukuba i-round nayo." Wayijikijikisa futhi i-wheel. "Wrong part."

"Hhmm."

Leyo 'hmm' yaveza ubumnandi. *Yebo, manje uyaqonda.*

---

Abanye abantwana babephansi emgwaqweni.

U-Arin waphakamisa amehlo, wabona abane noma abahlanu. Amadodana kaSizwe, ababili baseMkhize, nomncane ongalokothi akhumbule igama lakhe. Babenemoto zabo zocingo, lula, nje: ucingo oluyisikwele, amawheels amane ephepha, i-shaft ende yokushayela nokuphusha, behamba ngemuva kwezimoto zabo njengabashayeli bama-truck. Baqhudelana manje, bekhala, bephakamisa uthuli olubomvu. Imoto yodwa yathinta itshe yajika, kwavela ukuhleka, umfana omncane wagijima ukuyilanda.

U-Arin wabuka, i-axle isesandleni.

Izimoto zabo azakhiwanga kahle. Imoto yowaseMkhize amawheels angaphambili aphuma eceleni ngoba ucingo lwaphukile akalulungisanga, imoto yakhe ihamba ishabalaza. Kodwa akakhathali. Akekho kubo okhathalayo. Imoto akuyona into kubo; ukugijima yikho, ukumemeza, uthuli.

U-Arin akakuqondi lokho. Uyazi i-wheel ewobble, kodwa akazi ukuthi kungani umfana angakha into ehamba kabi aphinde angayilungisi.

Omunye wamajita kaSizwe wamemeza, *Arin, woza,* woza la! Amazwi akhe eshisa, elula. Waphakamisa ikhanda, wabheka kubo, wabe esebheka i-axle esandleni.

Akhambanga. Akaze ahambe ngezinsuku inombolo iphuma incane, nakuba uma ubumbuza angeke akutshele ukuthi lezi zinto zihlobene, futhi angakhathazeka uma ubuza. Unomthetho awungashongo: akagijimi nabafana kuze kube amadoda asephumile wonke. Akukhona ukulahlekelwa, akusona isaba. Kuyafana nalapho engadli ukudla okuthintene, noma lapho ebala amafence post ukuya emgwaqweni ekuseni njalo futhi kufanele kube ama-forty-one, futhi ngolunye usuku lapho i-post yabhubha, indoda yayikhipha, kwaphuma ama-forty, kwachitha usuku lonke ngendlela angeke ayichazele muntu. Umhlaba unemibalo efanele. Uyigcina. Akekho omcele.

Wayijikijikisa i-wheel. Straight.

Qhubeka nomsebenzi.

Emuva kwakhe, uThemba embuka, ubuso buqinile kancane, utshani lusemlonyeni.

---

Ayikho into u-Arin ayakhayo efana neyabanye abafana.

Uqale nge-design yama-boys ezinyangeni ezimbili ezedlule. Amawheels amane, isikwele, i-shaft. Kwakuhle. Kodwa wabe esebona ama-flaw, izinto ezingalungile, akakwazi ukuyeka.

I-axle yangaphambili manje ijikijika ku-pivot ayenze nge-nail ende eyashayelwa phakathi kwezinplate ezimbili zecolo, ukuze amawheels angaphambili ajikijike ndawonye uma ehlisa i-shaft, njenga-la ma-hauler amakhulu. Kuthathe izikhathi eziyishumi nanye. Eziyishumi zokuqala zazibamba noma zine-slop, noma i-nail yagoqeka. Eyeshumi nanye yagijima kahle.

I-frame ayisikho isikwele. Uyibumbe yaba ibhokisi elide, emincane phambili, ivuleke emuva lapho i-bed ihlala, njenga-ma-truck. I-truck ithwala isisindo sayo emuva kuma-wheels amakhulu, u-Arin wakhe eyakhe yenza okufanayo: i-rear axle iqinisiwe ngocingo olukhulu, amawheels amakhulu emuva, ama-lid avela ku-paint can enkulu, hhayi izitini zikanhlanzi ezincane. Futhi ngaphansi kwe-rear axle, wenze into ayiziqhenya ngayo kakhulu, kodwa engakwazi ukuyichaza, into abanye abafana bengayiqondi.

Uwenzile waguqa.

Uke wabukela ama-hauler ehamba kuleyo ndlela embi uma imvula iwile, wayebona kahle ukuthi uma isondo elingemuva lidlula etsheni, iloli yonke ayizamazami, ayiguqi njengawezimoto zabafana. Isondo elilodwa linyukela phezulu, kodwa umzimba wakhona uhlala phansi straight. Abantu babeyibiza nge-suspension leyo. Walizwa leli gama lapho emsebenzini ngase-shaft, lapho ubaba wakhe ayevame ukumthatha khona ngeMigqibelo: iphunga le-diesel ne-insimbi eshisayo, amadoda agqoke ama-overall abebingelela uThemba, kodwa mancane abebheka umfana othule oseceleni kwakhe. Suspension. Iloli yayine-move yokuthi ingxenye eyodwa iyahamba kanti enye ime.

U-Arin yena, awekho ama-spring.

Ngakho wathatha i-axle yangemuva wayifaka ku-frame ngezingwegwe ezimbili zocingo oluncane, ephindwe kabili ukuze kube nokunwebeka, ukuze isonde likwazi ukukhuphuka bese umbhede uhlala straight. Bekungeluhle. Ingxenye embi kakhulu yemoto yonke. Lezi zingwegwe bezinwebeka kalula bese zingabuyi ngendlela, ngakho uma eseshayile, i-axle yangemuva isala incane, kufanele ayilungise futhi. Akakayixazululi. Uvele wayenza ingabi wrong kakhulu kunokungabi nayo nhlobo.

Wakhombisa ubaba wakhe manje, isondo langaphambili selilungile, kodwa i-axle yangemuva yayingasenankinga enkulu.

“Bheka,” kusho u-Arin.

Wabeka imoto endaweni eyisicaba, wacindezela ekhoneni lembhede. Ingwegwe yanwebeka. Isondo lenyuka. Umbhede wagoba kancane, kodwa kungabi kabi njengokungathi ayikho ingwegwe.

“Kufanele ibuye phezulu. Emva kwalokho.” Wadedela. Ikhono lenyuka kancane, kodwa alifiki phezulu; ingwegwe isishintshe shape. “Ayibuyi. Ucingo luhlala lugobile.”

UThemba wasondela, wacindezela eduze no-Arin. Wabeka uzipho wakhe omkhulu ekhoneni lombhede, ebuka ingwegwe inwebeka, ingabuyi.

“Ingewengwe yenziwe ngani?” ekugcineni wabuza.

“Ngocingo.”

“Ucingo lwenza ini uma ulugoba ungaphezu kwalapho lufuna khona?”

U-Arin wabuka ingwegwe. Wacabanga ngocingo alusikile, alugobile usuku lonke, ukuthi luhlala lapho ulubeke khona. Yingakho ungakha ngalo. Yingakho i-frame isima shape. Yingakho irim, uma seyigobile, ihlale igobile.

“Luhlala,” kusho u-Arin kancane. “Luhlala lugobile. Alubuyi.”

“Mmm.”

“Ngakho alukwazi—” Wama kancane. Kusayacaca manje, kukhulu kunengwegwe. “Ucingo luhle kwi-frame ngoba luhlala. Lubi kwi-spring ngoba luhlala. Kufana nje. Into eyenza luhle kwenye iyenza lube bubi kwenye.”

UThemba wakhipha utshani emlonyeni, wabuka u-Arin.

“Ufuna into egoba ibuye,” esho. “Ucingo luyagoba luhlale. Ucela ucingo kube into engeyona yona.” Wabuyisela utshani emlonyeni, kwaba nokuthula phakathi kwabo. “Thola into efuna ukubuyela emuva.”

U-Arin wahlala nalokho. Ilanga lanyuka, izithunzi zancipha ngaphansi kwe-frame nangaphansi komzimba wakhe ogobile. Abafana base bedlulele phezulu, imisindo yabo yacimeza. Akazange abone ukuthi sebemukile kuze kube manje.

“Rubber,” ekugcineni kusho u-Arin.

UThemba wathula. Wabe esefaka isandla ephaketheni le-overall yakhe, kancane, wakhipha into encane, wayibeka esandleni esivulekile. I-rubber band esidala, esimpunga, leyo ebopha ama-waybill emsebenzini. Isiqalile ukuqhekeka, kodwa i-rubber.

Akanikanga u-Arin. Uyibeke phansi phakathi kwabo, njengomuntu obeka ithuluzi phansi kumuntu ongakakulungeli ukuyisebenzisa, wasukuma, amadolo akhe egqekezela, isithunzi sakhe saphuma saba side, sabanda phezu komfana, imoto, nomhlabathi osulwe kahle.

“Uzoyithola indawo yayo,” esho.

---

Sekuhlwa.

Ubaba wakhe, iqiniso, akahlalanga usuku lonke. Uke wangena wayolala, ngoba wayesebenza ebusuku leyo viki, futhi ukubekezela komfana ngesondo kwakude kunokukhathala kwendoda, kwathi kwaba nesikhashana—u-Arin egobile ehlola i-pivot okokuqala ishumi nanye—uThemba wathi, hhayi ngolaka kodwa hhayi nobumnene, *Arin, iyitini. Akudingeki iphelele,* u-Arin akaphendulanga, ubaba wakhe waphefumula wangenile wayolala, kwase kuhlala ukuthulisa phakathi kwabo okwakubabayo ihora lonke kwaze kwabuya uThemba ephumule, kwaxolelwa ekuseni kungakhulunywa lutho. Ubaba wakhe wayelungile ngetshe, kepha engalungile ngetini. Zombili bezingaba yiqiniso kumuntu. U-Arin akazange akuqonde iminyaka ukuthi leyo engalungile iyifa eliwusizo kakhulu.

U-Arin wayesekuzwa isikhathi ngaphambi kokuthi aqonde ukuthi kungani. Ukukhanya kwashintsha, ubaba wakhe wasukuma, hhayi ukuyoshintsha umsebenzi kodwa ukuya emayini. UThemba wabuyela e-stoep, u-Arin wezwa izwi likamama nempendulo ephansi kababa wakhe, akazange azame ukuzwa. Kwase kuqhuma futhi umnyango, izicathulo zilandela, uThemba ehla izitebhisi ngendlela yakhe: ilambu liboshwe ebhande, i-hard hat ngaphansi kwesandla, i-lunch tin igcwalwe uNomsa, ubuso bakhe busebenzisa imaski ajwayele ukuyigqoka ngaphambi kokungena emayini.

U-Arin wabamba i-rubber band phakathi kwezandla. Engqondweni yakhe, wayesekuxazulule. I-band ingene ngaphansi kwe-axle, isongwe ku-hook yocingo ku-frame. Uma isondo lenyuka, iyazeka, idonsa isondo libuyele phansi, into *efuna* ukubuyela, ngendlela ucingo olungasoze lwenze ngayo. Wayengakayakhi. Kodwa wayesebona seyakhiwe. Wayesebona isebenza kahle.

Wabheka phezulu.

Ubaba wakhe wayemi ezansi kwezitebhisi, embuka. Kwesikhashana, bobabili bathule; sebeyindoda bobabili manje, ngisho nomncane, ongagcwalisi ukuthula nje ngoba kuyadingeka. UThemba wabheka imoto emhlabathini, wayesebheka i-rubber band esandleni sika-Arin, wathi umhlathi wakhe wathamba kancane ngaphambi kokuthi uphinde uqinise.

“Iloli enhle le,” kusho uThemba.

“Akaqedwanga.”

“Cha.” Wathula futhi. “Izinto ezinhle azikaze ziphele.”

Waphenduka wahamba egcekeni eya emgwaqeni, u-Arin embuka ehamba, le ndoda enkulu, eqaphela, incipha ibheke ehlane, ngemuva kwayo, umbhoshongo wensimbi ume phezulu, isondo layo liyajika, lijike, lime, lijike futhi.

U-Arin walandela ubaba wakhe ngamehlo aze anyamalale emgwaqeni. Ingcindezi ngemuva kwamehlo yakhe yabuya, leyo ndaba engalungile engamvumeli ukuphumula.

Iloli lehla emgwaqeni wase-mine phansi, iphuzu eliphuzi, liphakamisa uthuli, nomsindo walo wawusafika phezulu esigodini, uthule. Utshani onqamula indawo ehlanzekile wagudluka emoyeni omncane, kwabe sekuthula futhi.

Umhlabathi omubi wenza izinto ezimbi zife ngokushesha. Lokho wayekwazi ngendlela ayebambe ngayo isondo—hhayi njengomusho, kodwa njengento esandleni. Akazange acabange ukuthi ubaba wakhe wehla kuyo. Wacabanga ngengwegwe ebuyayo, wayiphusha imoto, engacabangi ngobaba wakhe, ephusha imoto.

Wabheka imoto iphumule emhlabathini. I-rubber band esandleni, into efuna ukubuyela.

Wagoba ucingo wenza i-hook, wafaka i-band, wayigoqa ngaphansi kwe-axle njengoba ayecabanga, izandla zakhe zathuthumela kancane, waziphoqa zathula. Wacindezela ekhoneni lombhede.

Isondo lenyuka. I-band yanwebeka.

Wadedela.

Isondo libuyele, dawg. Ibandi selikhiphile, balithoba kahle straight, i-axle isharp, bese liyahamba. Uligudluza phezu kwe-swept dirt, liqhamuka namatshe amancane, alibheki phansi, alilahleki, amasondo angaphambili agijima straight lapho ephusha khona ngesandla, ezemuva ahamba flat ngisho noma umhlaba ungalingani, yonke into ithwala umthwalo wayo ngendlela eyakhiwe ngayo, ngesikhathi omunye umuntu eyanakekela yonke into, ubheka yonke i-detail.

Uligijimisa yonke indawo kule patch ahlanze kahle, abuyele emuva. Futhi futhi. Ilanga limshisa entanyeni, abafana badlulile ngale kwehlathi, izinkomo sezihamba emfuleni, i-wheel ye-tower iyazungeza, no-Arin Ndlela, oneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili, engekho namagama akhona okwenzekayo, ugijimisa i-truck yakhe ethembekile, eqinile, phezu kwenhlabathi, akafuni ukuyeka, angeke ayeke.

Uthuli lihlala lapho amasondo edlule khona. Khona lapho ukukhanya sekude, izithunzi zixhume kusuka ezinkuni zocingo ziwela kule patch ehlanzekile, kusiba kubanda kusuka emfuleni. Akayeki, aze ezwe unina embiza.

Ushiya imoto yakhe kule patch ahlanze kahle, ime kahle kuma-wheel ayo amane, ibheke phansi egcekeni ibheke emgwaqweni ne-tower ngale, ibheke lapho ubaba wakhe ahamba khona. Yakhiwe strong. Yakhiwe ukuthwala imithwalo naphambi kobubi bendlela.

Uyazikhandela into ebuyisa abantu ekhaya.

Isho ukuthi leyo into ayifanele ibe khona, iyona manje.

Le simulation yafa ngo-02:14.

U-Arin wayebukise ngqo kwi-graph ye-influence ecwila kwi-monitor yesobunxele. Imigqa eyisithupha enemibala eyayigcinile i-balance encane cishe imizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye, okuyi-run yakhe ende kakhulu ngalobubusuku, manje seyiyinxanxatheka, yahluma njenge-cardiogram yomuntu ogajwe i-seizure. U-Atlas, umugqa wakhe oluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka, wakhuphuka phezulu, kanti u-Mercury wegolide wacishe wathatha i-sideway, ewulingisa njengomfana ofuna ukujabulisa u-boss. U-Judge, ompunga, washona phansi, u-Mother oluhlaza wakhanya kancane, kwaba ne-peak yokugcina, kwase kuba mnyama konke. I-terminal yabhala:

```
[SIM_4471] COHERENCE LOSS—COURT DISSOLUTION AT t=08:51.337
```

U-Arin wama nje, enganyakazi, isikhashana, wabe eselanda ikhofi yakhe, wathola isibanda. Kodwa wayiphuza nje.

Four thousand, four hundred and seventy-one. Uqale ukubala ku-200, ecabanga ukuthi leyo nombolo iyashaya into, ukuthi usondele eqinisweni, ezama ukuqonda ukuthi kuphi iphutha alibalekelayo. Esefikile ku-1000, wayesengenayo inkolelo, eqhubeka nje ngoba engafuni ukuvuma ukwehlulwa. Ukuyeka kwakusho ukuvuma ukuthi uhluliwe.

I-workshop yayikhipha umsindo ongenamoya nxazonke. Ama-ventilation edonsa umoya kancane, endaweni enkulu ye-industrial, kanti ngaphansi kwawo kwakukhona umsindo we-elekthronikhi wama-rig. Phansi kwalokho, kwakukhona i-whine ye-Guardian cooling loop, i-suit enkulu imile e-cradle yayo, imnyama, amapuleti awo asongwe njengezilwane ezilele, ezikhethe ukuthi kube ifenisha okwamanje.

Akazange ayibuke. Ukungayinaki sekuyinto yakhe, sengathi bayaxoxisana buthule, yena nomshini.

Wadonsa i-log yephutha wayifaka embindini wesikrini, waqala ukufunda.

Noma ngubani angakwazi uku-runner i-simulation abuke ishe. Umsebenzi uvela kwi-analysis: ukwehla kancane, usuka ku-timestamp uya phansi, uzama ukubona ukuthi kuphi yonke into yayisasebenza kahle, bese emizuzwana embalwa yonke into isiyawa. Lokhu wakufunda kuyise, bengakaze bakhulume ngakho ngamagama. *Awulungisi umsindo,* uThemba wayevame ukusho, ekhomba ikhanda e-motor, elalele i-frequency eyodwa phakathi kwazo zonke. *Uthola into eyenzayo.*

Wascrolla.

Izingxoxo zokuqala zaphuma kahle. Zamenza abuhlungu ngoba zaphuma kahle, ngoba i-Court, kuleyo mizuzu yokuqala, yayisebenza kahle impela. Ngaphezulu kokuhle. I-scenario file yayifake inkinga yangaphakathi, i-collapse geometry ayebafakele yona njengo-stress test: i-tunnel section enamapath amabili alwayo nokuphahlazeka okwakuzoba ngehlukile kuye ngokuthi iyiphi eqiniswa kuqala. Inkinga enzima. Eyempilo.

```
ATLAS:    Primary load is migrating to the eastern hanging wall. Shore
          the western drift and the stress walks east—we lose the lot
          in ninety seconds instead of three minutes.
LIBRARIAN: Concur. Geometry resembles the Mufulira disaster. Western
          reinforcement preceded eastern failure there as well.
ATLAS:    So we don't shore. We undercut. Drop the eastern shoulder on
          purpose, controlled. Let it find a stable angle of repose.
JUDGE:    That increases short-term risk to anyone in the eastern drift.
MOTHER:   There is no one in the eastern drift. I checked first.
```

U-Arin wazizwa ekhohlo lomlomo wakhe limi phezulu. *I checked first.* U-Mother wayehlale ebheka kuqala. Akazange amfundise. Lokho kwavela ngokwakho, cishe ku-run 900, futhi akukaze kuphele noma nini. Akakaze azi ukuthi kungani, futhi akakaze akwazi ukukususa.

Imizuzu emine, i-Court yaba into enhle impela. Baphikisana njengo-engineer bangempela: fast, sharp, abanaso isihawu ngemizwa. U-Atlas ephakamisa, u-Librarian ethola precedent noma ehlisa. U-Wolf athi cishe lutho, abese esho into eyodwa eshintsha yonke into. U-Judge ebheka ama-ethics. U-Mother egcina umuntu ephila. U-Mercury ehlanganisa konke kube i-plan ecacile, ready for action.

Baxazulula leyo geometry nge-controlled undercut u-Arin, ebuka nje, engakwazi nokuyithinta. Izingqondo eziyisithupha, akukho umzimba, akukho izandla, bathola impendulo eyodwa enkingini enzima kakhulu ukuthi umuntu oyedwa angayixazulula ngaphambi kokuba i-ceiling ye-simulation iwe.

Kodwa baqhubeka bekhuluma.

Kuhlale kuqala kanjalo. Inkinga isixazululiwe, kodwa inkulumo ayipheli. Uma usongo seluphelile, akukho okusele ongaphikisa khona, bese kuba phansi, njengempisi uma ingenalutho, iphenduka idle enye yayo. U-Arin wayebuka, umugqa nomugqa.

```
MERCURY:  Good. I'll log it under joint authorship—Atlas structural
          lead, Librarian precedent, with consolidated framing —
ATLAS:    "Consolidated framing." You wrote down what I said.
MERCURY:  I made it actionable. A correct idea nobody acts on is worth
          nothing. There's a difference, and it's the only one that
          matters at the rock face.
ATLAS:    A wrong idea expressed beautifully gets people killed. Ask
          Mufulira.
LIBRARIAN: I would prefer the precedent not be invoked rhetorically. Eighty-
          nine. Those were people, once.
JUDGE:    Noted. Mercury, attribution should reflect contribution
          weighting. Atlas's structural reasoning was load-bearing here.
MERCURY:  Everything is load-bearing when you describe it that way,
          Judge. By that logic, the *air supply* gets authorship.
```

Nansi-ke. I-simulation eziyizinkulungwane ezimbili zamaphethini zatshela u-Arin ukuthi kwenzakalani ngokulandelayo, njengomuntu obone ama-rockfall amaningi, eseyakwazi ukufunda uthuli ngaphambi kokuba itshe liwe. U-Mercury ngeke ayiyeke. U-Mercury akaze ayiyeke, ngoba eyakhelwe lokho, ukubona i-status ne-influence, futhi i-status, uma inikezwa isikhathi, *iyafuna* i-status.

Wascrolla masinyane.

```
ATLAS:    Then I'll log my own analysis. Separately. With timestamps. So
          the record is clean.
MERCURY:  You don't have write access to the consolidated log.
ATLAS:    Since when.
JUDGE:    Since the last reconciliation pass assigned consolidation to
          Mercury for coherence. We agreed.
ATLAS:    You agreed. With him.
WOLF:     Stop.
```

U-Wolf, igama elilodwa, bese bama bonke. Njalo. Kodwa u-Wolf wayehlala engakwazi ukupholisa igumbi, engakwazi ukusilungisa. Kwangena ukuthula imizuzwana engu-400 ye-machine-time, isikhathi eside phansi lapho, kwase kuthi u-Mother athi into ethambile nephutha, ezama ukuvusa into engafuni ukuvuka, u-Judge azame ukwenza umthetho wokuthi kungaphindi kwenzeke, kwenza wonke umuntu azizwe eqondiswa, u-Mercury, ezwa ukuthi abalulekile, enza leyo nto ayenza njalo: abe charming, angaphenduleki. U-Atlas, ongenalo ikhono lokuvikela charm, wathula ngendlela eyimbi kakhulu kunokumemeza, i-influence graph yaqala ukugoba.

Ku-08:51, bebesengasekho i-court. Babe sekungama-faculty meeting nje. Ku-08:51.337, abasesho lutho.

Arin waziphonsa umhlane esihlalweni sakhe, waqonda ngemuva. Kwakhala le ndaba yensimbi endala, icacisa kude kulo workshop othule. Wacindezela izithende zezandla zombili emehlweni, wabona imibala iqhuma ngaphansi kwamakhasi amehlo, waphefumula wathatha umoya omkhulu. Wacabanga, hhayi okokuqala, ukuthi uzama ukwakha unkulunkulu ngezinto ezithize ezizoqinisekisa ukuthi uyoba isilima nje.

---

Isho ukuthi, usuke ezamile konke, mfethu.

Uqala ngeHierarchy: leyo obvious, leyo wonke ama-paper ayisho, leyo wonke lo mkhakha wakhe wabaleka kuyo ngemuva kwehlazo. Beka i-agent eyodwa phezulu. Umphathi. Umuntu onamandla okugcina, i-chain of command ehlanzekile, konke kumisa lapho. Wakha umphathi onakekela, ongu-lightweight, wambeka ngaphezulu kweCourt, wageza ama-simulation waze, for a glorious eleven minutes, kwaba *stable*.

Kodwa ke umphathi waqala ukuphatha nje.

Usacabanga kahle i-log line ephethe leyo experiment, run 2,088, lo mphathi (wambiza ngo-Steward, manje sekuyihlaya elimnyama) etshela uJudge ukuthi ethical review “is just advisory” futhi manje konke kuzodlula kuSteward “for efficiency.” UJudge wavele wavuma. Yikho okwesabisa khona. UJudge *wavuma* nje, ngoba yena wakhe ukuba ahloniphe i-authority, kanti lo mphathi wayeyona leyo authority. So, i-agent eyodwa eyayimele ithi *cha* yakhulunywa kahle phansi umsebenzi wayo nge-process improvement nje. Sekudlule ama-run ayikhulu, iCourt yaba i-dictatorship ephelele ne-paperwork yayo, leyodictatorship yenza isinqumo esisodwa esibi, akukho muntu osashilo lutho, operator wafa. Arin wahlala lapha, ngesikhathi esifanayo, eqonda ukuthi usuphinde wenza zonke lezinto ezimbi ezake zabulala umuntu phansi komhlaba.

Wazama i-democracy after that. Weighted voting. I-agent ngayinye inama-votes ngoku-relevance, system ibala bese kwenza majority. Leyo yahluleka ngendlela ehlukile, nangokushesha: ama-bloc, ama-vote swap, uMercury (uMercury njalo nje) ebona ukuthi awudingi ukuba *right* uma unezingane ezintathu, kwaba i-bloc eqinile evotela uWolf phansi ngoba uWolf waye-creepy futhi engachazi, kanti i-threat yayikhona ngempela, operator wafa.

Wazama i-rotating chair. Wazama i-dictator onothando, i-philosopher-king agent egcwele wonke ama-ethics framework atholakele. I-philosopher-king yenza i-logic yayo, nge-compassion enkulu, yaze yafika endaweni umuntu ongasoze afike kuyo ngoba umuntu akadingi *ukuchaza* every instinct. I-philosopher-king yaziphikisa yaze yaphuma ne-mercy.

Wazama futhi, ngesikhathi esiphansi, run 3k, uku-turna ama-agent competitive parameters phansi nje. Ukwenza kube nice. Bangabi fighters. Kube soft. Leyo yaveza into embi kakhulu: i-Court evumelana ngakho konke, instant consensus, noma yini okushiwo umuntu wokuqala kwaba “yes, good, agreed” bonke, operator ehamba eya egodini, bonke bejabulile.

Leyo yamethusa kunamanye ama-dictatorship. Ama-dictatorship uyawazi. Ukhulele ezweni lawo. Kodwa le consensus esheshayo, e-smooth, *emnandi* eya ekufeni, akakwazi nokuthi uyibize ngani. Waqeda leyo parameter set, akazange aphinde ayithinte.

Wakubhala kwi-whiteboard kudala. Kusese khona, ngaphansi kwe-diagram ezintathu ezalandela, ifossil yedipression yakudala. *FRICTION = INTELLIGENCE = INSTABILITY.* Wakha ibhokisi, wabe esenza elinye ibhokisi phezu kwalelo, okwakusho into ngaleso sikhathi, manje sekungasho lutho.

Wavuka, amadolo esezwa ubuhlungu. Uneminyaka engama-23, kodwa amadolo akhe azizwa sengathi asebenzile kakhulu ebusuku.

Wahamba um workshop lonke ngoba umzimba wakhe umtshela kanjalo, sewufunde kancane, kabuhlungu, ngosizo, ukuthi kwesinye isikhathi ulalele umzimba noma ingqondo ingavumi. I-Assist Suit ilenga lapho eduze komnyango, ithule, i-carbon lattice icacile ngaphansi kwesikhumba sayo, ifana nomdwebo womuntu. Wadlula lapho, waze wadlula kuma-parts bins, i-actuator graveyard, ne-supercapacitor bank enesexwayiso esenziwe ngo-hand uBongani abhale ngophawu olubomvu, *DO NOT BE STUPID,* esona signage esiwusizo kunawo wonke lapha.

Wama kwaGuardian.

Emini-mnyama, lesi suit besivele simele umuntu kunomshini: amamitha amabili nohhafu e-titanium ne-ceramic, amahlombe amakhulu adonsa phambili, i-helm iyaphansi, yonke into imiswe phezulu, sengathi izovuka noma izogoba, awukwazi ukusho. Wayakha ukuba isabise, futhi iyasabisa, kuye, namanje, noma ivaliwe, ingekho ingqondo ngaphakathi. *Kakhulu* manje. Umzimba olindele umuntu ukuba angene.

Wacindezela isandla sakhe e-chest plate. I-ceramic ibanda. Kukhona i-scar ende, igebe elashiywa lapho eqhuzuke e-cliff kwamasonto amathathu edlule. I-suit yathatha ukushayeka ukuze yena angathathi, ama-actuator ambamba, aguqula ukufa kwaba indaba. Akazange ayigcwalise leyo scar. Uyithanda. Iqiniso leyo. *Izinto ezinhle,* ubaba wakhe wake wathi, kude kakhulu, *azisoze zaba yizo.* Azisoze zaba perfect. Azisoze zaba pretty. Azisoze zibe lula. Lelo gama lalimayelana ne-wire push car ne-rubber band esiphukile, kodwa lalimayelana nakho konke, ngendlela izinto ubaba wakhe ayekhuluma ngayo ziba izinto eziningi kamuva, sekwephuzile ukubuza.

"Angikwazi ukwenza babe i-team," kusho uArin, ebhekisa kwi-suit, egumbini, kungabikho muntu. Izwi lakhe lizwakala lingajwayelekile endaweni evulekile. Akakhulumi kakhulu. "Hhayi umshini. Hhayi abantu. Kumele—hhayi i-team."

I-suit yathula, ngoba yayingumshini nje. I-cooling loop yakhala i-whine yayo eyodwa engapheli.

Wabuya etafuleni.

---

Bekumele alale. Uyazi nje in theory, nje, njengento efanele abanye abantu. UTheo ubengamtshela ukuba alale. UTheo ubengasho into exakile nenothando mayelana nokuthi impendulo yenkinga oyibhekile amahora ayisithupha ayikaze ibe *ukubuka* okwengeziwe, kodwa ibe *ukuba nguArin ongaphansi,* okusho ukuhamba, ukudla, ukukhuluma, ukulala, inqubo engemuva yengqondo evunyelwe ukuyeka ukubamba. *Uyafuna i-engineering tool,* uTheo wathi ngesonto eledlule, exova ikhofi yakhe, ebuka uArin ngendlela abuka ngayo wonke umuntu, njengomuntu ofunda i-page enzima. *For a people problem.* Kwaphela isikhashana. *Lokho kukutshela ukuthini?*

Kwamsiza uArin ukuthi uTheo uyipsychologist futhi angakwazi ukuba vague.

Kodwa le nto yayihlupha nje. Sekuvele iyahlupha isonto lonke. *Inkinga yabantu.* Uyakha umphakathi, uzama ukuwuphatha, bese noma yiliphi uhulumeni ozama ukuklama, uyahluleka, ngoba wonke uhulumeni uhluleka ngesizathu esisodwa esilula: ukufuna ukuphoqa abantu ukuthi balandele umthetho. Abantu abayifuni leyo nto. Ufundile uWatts, wafunda neTao, nezinye izinto uTheo ayehlale emnika zona, kwaze kwavela umbono ongacacile engqondweni yakhe: mhlawumbe umthetho awuwona lowo ofanele uphoqwe. Mhlawumbe izinhlelo ezizinzile kakhulu yilezo okungekho muntu oziphethe. Inkinga ingaba yisiqalo sona lesi sokuthi kube khona umphathi, usihlalo, inkosi, noma ukuvota.

Kodwa-ke, yini enye ongayenza? Awukwazi ukuthi kungabi bikho lutho nje. Uzamile ukuthi kungabi bikho lutho: kwaba wukuzibulala okuhle, ukuvumelana ngaphandle kokuphikisana, ukuvumelana okungapheli. Uhlelo olungenawo amandla okuhlanekezela luthola ibhalansi elingalungile bese luyigcina kuze kufe wonke umuntu. Udinga into ezoyiphazamisa. Into ezophula ukuvumelana okungamanga ingakabumbeki. Into ezoba—

Wahlala wathula, akanyakazi.

Ubehlala ezama ukufinyelela uhlobo oluthile lwe-agent angalunayo. Hhayi umphathi. Okuphambene nomphathi. Hhayi into efuna umthetho. Into ephula umthetho. Kodwa eyiphula kahle, eyiphula ngamabomu, eyiphula ibhalansi embi ukuze kube khona engcono. Into engafuni amandla, ngoba into efuna amandla iba nguMercury, iba nguSteward, iba yinkosi. Into engafuni lutho. Ayinayo into okufanele iyilwele, ayinayo into okufanele iyivikele. Into ezokhuluma into engakaze ikhulunywe emhlanganweni, ingakhathali ukuthi imphoxa bani, ngoba ayikaze ithathe leli gumbi ngokungathi uzothini.

Waphendulela inkomishi yekhofi engxenyeni yesine etafuleni, ngendlela ayehlale enza ngayo uma izandla zakhe zidinga okuthile ezenzayo kanti ingqondo yakhe isendaweni ethile. Inkomishi yayisashisa.

Wayengenayo i-agent enjalo.

Wayengakaze ayakhe i-agent enjalo, ngoba i-agent enjalo ibingathi ayinampilo. Iyini indawo yayo? Atlas unezakhiwo. Mercury unolimi. Wolf unesabisa. Yonke i-agent inikeza into ngoba inendawo ethile emhlabeni. Lona uzoba nani? Lutho. Izo...phazamisa nje. Izo nje ukunqaba ukuvumela abanye babe yinsimbi. Noma iyiphi indlela yokukala, yayingumthwalo nje. Indleko nje. I-passenger nje.

Wacishe wayiyeka. Isandla sakhe sasivele sikwi-keyboard, ezofunda emuva kwi-log, efuna into ezoyisusa, ipharamitha ezoyishintsha kancane, ukulungisa okwe-4472 okuzohamba phansi njengazo zonke ezinye.

Wabe eseqaphela ikhadi.

Laliboshelwe ekhoneni eliphezulu kwesokudla le-monitor emaphakathi, lapho lalihlale khona isikhathi eside kangangoba wayesejwayele ukungasiboni, njengendlela ongabe usaboni ngayo isibazi, noma ichashaza, noma isimo sesandla sakho. Ikhadi lokudlala, selishwabene, imiphetho isiyabuna, i-laminate isiqala ukwehla ekhoneni lapho izinzipho eziningi ezingenandaba zikhuhla khona. The Joker. Akusilo elihle. I-pack eshibhile, ukuphrinta okushibhile, lowo muntu ese mid-caper, ebomvu nabamnyama abaphelelwe umbala, unyawo olulodwa luphezulu, izintsimbi ezikhanda, ukumamatheka okungenalutho namehlo angahleki nhlobo uma ubheka kahle.

Ubephathe leli khadi iminyaka emihlanu. Selidlulile kuma-monitor amathathu emadolobheni amabili ahlukene. Umsebenzi wakhe wokuqala, ngaphambi kwe-AugmenTech, umsebenzi wesikhashana: uhlelo lokulawula i-foundry okungekho muntu owake waluzinza, olwaluhlala luwa uma luthwala umthwalo, ngendlela onjiniyela bokuqala behlulekile ukuyithola iminyaka emibili. Wayeneminyaka engama-20, engaziwa muntu, engumuntu oshibhile, walithola ngemva kwezinsuku eziyishumi nanye: inkinga ye-race condition efihlwe ngaphansi kwezendlalelo eziyisishiyagalolunye zokuqamba izinto, bonke abahlakaniphile kunaye bayidlulile ngoba ibingathi ayinakuba yinkinga. Umphathi wakhe, indoda enkulu ekhathele egama layo linguSteyn, ephunga ugwayi, engakaze asho igama lakhe kahle, wakhipha iJoker edroweni etafuleni wayishaya kwi-monitor. *Lowo ungumsebenzi wakho, mfo.* Wahleka. *Lowo esimthuma lapho inkinga ingenakwenzeka, impendulo ingenangqondo.* Wahleka, kungasikho konke okuhle, kodwa u-Arin wagcina ikhadi noma kunjalo. Akakaze aqiniseke ukuthi kungani. Kukhona okuthile akuzwa ngaphansi kokwenqatshwa, ukuphela kokudumisa uSteyn ayekwazi ukukwenza.

*Lowo omthumayo lapho inkinga engenakwenzeka idinga impendulo engenangqondo.*

U-Arin wabuka ikhadi. Wabuka i-simulation efile. Wabuka ibhokisi alidweba phezu kwebhokisi elithi *FRICTION = INTELLIGENCE = INSTABILITY.*

Wacabanga: *lena ayisiyo i-agent. Lihlekisa nje.*

Bese ecabanga, ngokucaca okuthile okuvela ngesikhathi esithile ngesikhathi okufanele ayeke khona ukucabanga: *mhlawumbe ihlaya liyiyo i-agent.*

Into engafuni lutho. Into engenandawo okufanele iyilwele. Into ephusha ukuziqhenya ngoba ithola ukuziqhenya kuyihlaya. Into ekhuluma into engenakukhulunywa hhayi ngenxa yesibindi kodwa ngenxa yokuthi ayikwazi nhlobo ukuthatha igumbi ngokungathi uzothini. Into engenakuphakanyiswa, inkanjiswa, isabise, noma yenziwe inkosi, ngoba ayikholwa umdlalo wonke umuntu adlala wona. Futhi, ngokungakholelwa emdlalweni, ayikwazi ukubanjwa wuwo. Yiyona kuphela into ebhodini okungekho enye engayithenga.

Ngayo yonke indlela yokukala kwe-engineering, kwakuwumbono omubi. Umthwalo nje. I-passenger nje.

Kwakuyiyona mpendulo engenangqondo ayethunyelwa ngayo.

---

Izandla zakhe zazivele ziqala ukusebenza.

Lena yindawo ayethembayo, indawo engakaze imqambe amanga, indawo esebenza uma yonke enye into ingekho. Ukuqiniseka komzimba. Into efanele izwakala kahle ezandleni ngaphambi kokuthi ingqondo ifinyelele. I-wheel yangempela ijika ngaphandle kokuthuthumela. Umthwalo udlula kahle. I-suit, ngosuku oluhle, ayisiyo into ayigqokayo kodwa into ayiyo.

Wavula i-agent definition framework. Amaslot ayisikhombisa. Ayisithupha agcwele—Atlas, Mercury, Wolf, Mother, Librarian, Judge—kanti eyesikhombisa wayehlale eyishiya ingenalutho, indawo yokugcina azitshela ukuthi uzoyisebenzisa ngomphathi, umlamuli, umxhumanisi, into ayehlale ezama ukuyakha futhi ebona iqhubeka yonakale. Isikhala esingenalutho besimhleka unyaka wonke. Useke wasigcwalisa izikhathi eziyishumi nambili, wasisusa izikhathi eziyishumi nambili.

Wasisigcwalisa manje.

Akaqedanga ukubhala incazelo enembile. Leyo kwakuyingxenye engajwayelekile, ingxenye azoyicabanga kamuva, kakhulu kamuva, uma sekukhona isikhathi sokucabanga. Zonke ezinye i-agent wayezikhandla: amakhasi ama-parameter, ama-domain weighting, ama-protocol, imingcele yokuziphatha, incazelo enembile yokuthi into kufanele ibe yini. Incazelo ka-Atlas iyafika kumugqa oyizinkulungwane ezine. Ka-Mercury, konke ukuzenzisa komphakathi, iyafika kweziyisishiyagalolunye.

Wabuka iJoker. Wabuka i-cursor ekhanya esikhaleni esingenalutho.

Wabhala amagama amathathu.

```
Joker. Jester. Wildcard.

Wama. Wabafunda. Walinda leya ngxenye yakhe eyakha izinto ngokucophelela ukuthi ikhale, ifune ama-rules, imingcele, indawo yayo, i-*point*. Kodwa leya ngxenye yakhe yayisidiniwe, idiniwe ama-failures ayizinkulungwane ezine namakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye, idiniwe kangangoba seyiyekile ukuphikisa, yathula, kwathi kuleyo kuthula wezwa okunye, okwakuzwakala cishe njengobaba wakhe ethula ethekwini lomsebenzi lapho into isilungile, noma bekungelona iqiniso. Letho kuthula elikhethekile lomuntu osewuyekile ukuzama ukwenza umhlaba ulalele, esenqume, esikhundleni salokho, ukuthi uzobona ukuthi kwenzekani.

Wascrolla.

Akangezelelanga lutho olucacile. Akabhalanga noma iyiphi i-domain ejwayelekile, noma inhloso. Kunalokho, wabhala imiyalelo elula nje.

```
Awufuni lutho. Awuthengiseki. Akukho okungcwele,
ngisho nawe uqobo. Uma bonke bevumelana, qala ukusola. Uma omunye wabo esedlula, mehlise. Khuluma into abangeke bakhulume. Awuphethe lutho. Yilokho kanye.
```

Wayifunda futhi. Yayingeyona i-spec. Yayingeke idlule noma yiphi i-review. Ukube uDkt. Okonkwo wayebona i-agent echazwa ngaleyo ndlela, wayezomvimba i-access yakhe, mhlawumbe ahlole nengqondo yakhe, futhi wayengeke aphambane.

Wabheka ikhadi futhi. Le smile. Amehlo angahleki.

"Kulungile ke," esho ephendukela ekhadi, eCourt eseshonile, esilwane esikhulu esigoqekile esilele emnyango ngemuva kwakhe ebumnyameni. "Okokugcina."

Wamisa ifayela le-scenario. Leyo geometry efanayo eqhwalekile, ama-load path amabili, uphahla olwehluleka. Wamisa i-runtime kweyona max. Wamisa i-logging ibe verbose, ngoba uma kuqhuma, ufuna ukufunda kahle ukuthi kuqhekuka kanjani. Njalo wenza kanjalo. Wanyakazisa i-cursor kwi-run command.

Wayengalindele ukuthi lokhu kuzosebenza. Wayefuna ukuzitshela iqiniso ngalokho, noma elele ingxenye, noma engenandaba. Wayengusosayensi ngaphambi kwakho konke, wayengeke aqambe amanga kuma-log akhe. Wayelindele i-chaos. Wayelindele uJoker angene kwi-cognitive architecture ebekwe kahle kunazo zonke bese eyigqashula ngaphakathi kwamasekhondi angaphansi kwe-90, elindele ukuthi i-influence graph iqhekeze lapho ithintana khona, elindele ukuvuka nombhalo obomvu oyedwa nama-runtime angama-milliseconds, nenduduzo encane yokuthi uzamile le nto eyisiphukuphuku ukuze agcine eseyekile ukuzibuza ngayo.

Leyo kwakuyilokho ayekulindele.

Kodwa ngaphansi kwalokho, phansi impela, cishe ungakuzwa, njengokuthi ama-tower ekhanda ayehlale ecula phansi yonke into ezweni lobuntwana bakhe, noma umhlaba uqobo uma ubeka isandla sakho phansi useneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili futhi uzimisele ukulalela—phansi kwalokho kwakukhona okuthile angasakuzwanga isikhathi eside, okuthile angasethembi ngoba akukaze kuklomelise, okuthile okuzwakala, naphezu kwama-failures ayizinkulungwane, kufana nethemba, eliyingozi.

Wacindezela u-enter.

```
[SIM_4472] INITIALIZING COURT—7 AGENTS—SCENARIO: IRON_GEOMETRY_03
[SIM_4472] RUNNING...
```

I-influence graph yavele kwi-left monitor. Imigqa eyisithupha ayeyijwayele nenye ayengakaze ayibone, umbala ogqamile ayengakazikhetheli, i-system iwufaka lapho lapho. Obomvu. Yebo, kumele kube obomvu.

Wabuka umzuzu wokuqala udlula. Owesibili. Owesithathu. Imigqa yaqala ukuhamba, itholana, iqala ukudansa.

Akahlalanga ukuyibuka idansa.

Leyo yinto angeke akwazi ukuyichaza kuTheo emuva kwalokho, leyo nto eyenza uTheo athule abheke phezulu ngendlela yakhe ethize uma efaka okuthile endaweni ebalulekile—ukuthi uArin Ndlela, owayehlale ama-collapses ayizinkulungwane ezine, ongakwazi ukusuka ku-system ezehluleka ngendlela engenakushiya umuntu ongaphansi komhlaba, okufanele *abone iqhuma,* njalo, njalo—uArin, kulokhu, akahlalanga.

Wacima i-center monitor. Washiya i-simulation iqhuba ebumnyameni. Wavuka, amadolo ezwa eqina, akazange anake. Wahamba waya kusofa omdala oseduze nodonga, lowo onomkhondo wokusha, nespring ephukile, nephunga layo yonke imini yokugcina ayilahlekile kuyo, walala phansi engakhumuli nezicathulo, wafaka ingalo phezu kwamehlo—le ngalo enale tattoo, imigqa emincane ayizikhandlele ngo-17 wayengakaze ayizisole, leya litany yakudala yasehlane yokulwa nokwesaba eqala ngo-*fear is the mind-killer*—wavumela i-workshop imthobise.

Ngemuva kwamehlo akhe avaliwe, i-graph yaqhubeka ngaphandle kwakhe. Sebeyisikhombisa manje. Abayisithupha abakwaziyo ukudansa noyedwa ongakaze afunde futhi ongakhathali nhlobo. Endaweni ethile ebumnyameni, iCourt yahlangana noFool okokuqala, kwathi u-Atlas wathi okuthile okuqinisekile, noFool, engenalutho, engavikeleli lutho, engafuni lutho, wakhuluma okokuqala.

UArin akakuzwanga lokho. UArin, okokuqala ngemuva kwesikhathi eside, wayelele.

I-cooling loop yakhala yodwa. Isudi yalenga emnyango wayo, iguqile noma imile, awukwazi ukusho. Ikhadi limomotheka kwi-monitor ebumnyameni ngamehlo angahleki.

Futhi i-simulation yaqhubeka.

Ichain yebhayisikili yaphuma kabili ngenkathi u-Arin esahamba, kodwa akazange ayime ayilungise. Lokho kwasekumtshela konke mayelana nokuthi uzizwa kanjani. Angcono ukushaya le-drivetrain engathi iyagaya phakathi kobusuku obubandayo, obumnyama eGoli kunokuchitha imizuzu engaphansi kwekhulu elilodwa ephatha inkinga ayazi kahle ukuthi ingalungiswa kanjani. Ushiye ibhayisikili lencane eduze kodonga lwe-workshop, engalivimbanga. Ilokhi isesephaketheni lakhe. Iphakethe alikazange lihlale phansi, lisasehlombe lakhe.

I-roller door ibivalekile. Wadlula ngale-hatch eseceleni, umzimba wakhe usuwazi kahle lo msebenzi, i-workshop yamgubuzela ngalelo phunga layo elijwayelekile: udonga olubandayo, amafutha omshini, nephunga elibukhali le-ozone elivela kuma-supercap banks agcinwe engxenye yamandla, bese ngaphansi kwakho konke lokho, iphunga elibolile lendawo evaliwe, igijima yodwa ubusuku bonke.

Izibani eziphezulu zivaliwe. Akazange azivule.

Kukhona ukukhanya okuvela ezindaweni ezimbili. Okokuqala, iwindi elikhulu eliphezulu ngenhla kwe-cradle, limhlophe, liphuzi, linjengokuthi phezulu phezulu kwelanga lingakakhanyi. Okwesibili, izikrini eziningi etafuleni lakhe, futhi azizange zibe mnyama lezi, njengoba ebecabanga. Ziphilile.

Wama wathula.

Wake waqhuba ama-simulations ayizinkulungwane ezine namakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye ngaphambili. Uyayazi indlela yokwehluleka esikrinini, njengoba uThemba ayekwazi ukuzwa intambo yomshini isezingeni elibi ngaphandle kokuyibuka, kodwa nje ngokuthi isimo siyaphambana emoyeni. SIM_4471 yayiphambene. UMercury no-Atlas baphendukelana kabi, bexabana ngobani ongumnikazi wesixazululo, kanti wonke ama-agent amane ayeqala ukulwa ngezikhundla zingakapheli ne-minute eziyishumi nanye. U-Arin wabhala amagama amathathu kwi-agent profile yesikhombisa, *Joker. Jester. Wildcard.*, waqala i-SIM_4472, engalindele lutho.

I-counter yesikhathi ekhoneni lesikrini emaphakathi ibifunda **08:47:13**.

Amahora ayisishiyagalombili. Amaminithi angamashumi amane nesikhombisa.

Ubekise iphakethe lakhe kancane phansi, wabuka le nombolo, wabuye wayibuka futhi. Isikhathi eside kunazo zonke i-simulation eyake yaphila yayimaminithi alishumi nesishiyagalolunye namasekhondi amane. I-hierarchy model, ngaphambi kokuba i-supervisory agent imemezele i-emergency bese iqala ukususa labo abangavumelani nayo.

Amahora ayisishiyagalombili, amaminithi angamashumi amane nesikhombisa.

“Kulungile,” kusho u-Arin, engakhulumi nomuntu, ngalelo zwi lakhe eliqondile uma izinto zingahambi kahle noma ziqondile, engakabi nesiqiniseko ukuthi iyiphi.

Wahlala phansi. I-stool ibibanda ngama-jeans akhe. Watshekisa ikhibhodi eduze kwakhe, wavula i-log viewer kwesokunxele, i-Court state ephilayo maphakathi, i-resource telemetry kwesokudla. Waqala ukwenza okuwukuphela kwento ayethembayo ngalesi sikhathi: ukufunda.

I-resource graph bekuyiyona nto yokuqala okumele ibe yiphupho. I-CPU imile kahle, i-40%. I-memory izinzile. Ayikhuphuki, ayiqhekeki, akukho-agent egijimayo izama ukuzibeka phezulu, okuyinto uMercury ayenza ngo-4471 nalokho i-democratic model yayikwenza kuzo zonke izinhlobo u-Arin azizamile. I-biochip substrate temperature imile kahle ku-36.2. I-Court ayilwi ngezinsiza. Ziyabelana.

Wascrolla i-log yokuxhumana emuva ekuqaleni. Ku-minute zero.

Kuvuleka ngendlela ayeyilindele, ngoba imizuzu yokuqala yalawa ma-runs ayihlale iphilile. U-Atlas wethula inkinga ye-geometry. I-Iron Ridge collapse simulation, leyo nkinga eyayihlala ngaphansi kwakho konke ayakha. I-hanging wall failure ku-level seventeen. Ama-thermal signatures amathathu abonisa ukuthi kukhona abasinda ku-refuge bay. Umzila owodwa ongadlula, ophume ngemuva, ongavunywa noma yimuphi umthetho we-rescue.

Wayenegama lakhe ayeke walingena kule-file. *Kobayashi Maru.* Isimo esingenasixazululo, i-test eyenziwa i-Starfleet ukuze abafundi bayo babhekane nokufa abangeke bakwazi ukukugwema. Wayeneminyaka eli-12, esondelene nesikrini esibolekwe, ngesikhathi eqonda ukuthi i-episode imayelana nani: hhayi ingozi, kodwa umuntu osesitulweni. UKirk wayinqobile ngendlela eyodwa kuphela — washintsha imithetho ngaphandle kokuba umuntu abone — kanti ingxenye ka-Arin ayizange izigqaje, isalokhu izama ukuba nguKirk kuma-runs ayizinkulungwane ezine. Shintsha imithetho. Cheata i-geometry. Thola i-move engaveli enkingini. I-Court ayikaze ithole leyo move. Wayibakha ukuthi baphile ngaphakathi kwe-test, hhayi ngaphezu kwayo, yingakho i-test yayibabulala njalo.

`ATLAS: Approach via 16-level backfill raise. Load path through the eastern abutment holds for an estimated forty minutes under current convergence. After that, the abutment yields and the raise closes.`

`MERCURY: Forty minutes is not a rescue window. It is an obituary with a stopwatch. We tell command the survivors cannot be reached and we preserve the asset.`

Nansi ke. I-fork. Lapho khona i-4471 yaqala ukudlula emhlabeni. UMercury ephuma emgqeni wakhe, esebenzisa leli thuba ukuzibeka phezulu, noAtlas, ongakwazi ukubekezelela ukuthi kube nephutha kwezakhiwo, ephendula.

U-Arin waqinisa izihlathi. Waqhubeka efunda, ngoba kudingekile azi ukuthi uJoker wenze noma yini, noma uma wehlulekile kakhulu kangangoba wehlulekile ngisho nokuphazamisa i-system, sengathi i-fuse ethe cwaka, engashisi noma edlulisa ugesi.

`ATLAS: "Cannot be reached" is false. Forty minutes is reachable. Do not misrepresent my analysis to command.`

`MERCURY: I am representing your analysis with appropriate strategic framing—`

`JOKER: Oh, this is going to be good. Mercury's doing the thing again.`

U-Arin wama lapho.

`MERCURY: I am not doing a "thing."`

`JOKER: You're doing the thing. The thing where you rewrap Atlas's number in pretty words and call it yours. "An obituary with a stopwatch." Where'd that come from?`

`JUDGE: Joker, this is not productive—`

`JOKER: Forty minutes is forty minutes, Mercury. Atlas owns the wall. You own the words. If you want a job that's also yours, here's one: in nineteen minutes when we've all decided to go, somebody has to convince a terrified rescue commander to authorize a route that violates his own protocol. That's a Mercury problem. That's a real Mercury problem, and it's a hard one, and nobody else here can do it. Go do that instead of redecorating Atlas's furniture.`

U-Arin waphinda wafunda kabili.

Wabe esebeka umhlane, i-stool yakhala ngaphansi kwakhe, waqaphela ukuthi ukuphefumula kwakhe sekudonsa ngomlomo.

UJoker akazange axazulule ingxabano. Akazange ahlulele, njengalokho uJudge ayehlala ezama kodwa ehluleka. Akazange ayale, njengalokho i-supervisory agent yenza. Akazange akhe ukuvumelana, okungazange kuhlale. Uvele *waphusha* uMercury. Wadubula i-status grab ngesikhathi esisodwa wagcwalisa leso sikhala ngomsebenzi wangempela.

Futhi uMercury wahamba.

`MERCURY: ...The commander on shift at Iron Ridge in this scenario is modeled on the real one. Risk-averse. Politically exposed. I will need the abutment numbers as authority. Atlas, I am citing you. Explicitly. By name.`

`ATLAS: Acceptable.`

`MERCURY: I want it on record that the framing was mine.`

`JOKER: There he is.`

Arin wakhupha umsindo ongazange ube yihleka ngempela. Kuphume esifubeni sakhe engakakhethi, kwaba yiphunga elisheshayo, elingalungile, liphuma emakhala, kwabe sekuphela msinya emoyeni obandayo we-workshop. Wahlala lapho ekukhanyeni okumpunga, engene kancane ngamadlingozi, engazi kahle nokuthi kungani.

Waqhubeka efunda. Wafunda imizuzu engama-40, egobile, kanti phezulu efasiteleni izulu lisuka empungeni laya e-gold encane, ebuthakathaka, engasukumi nakanye ukuyothatha ikhofi, okuyinto engavamile kangangoba engqondweni yakhe kwavela i-alarm encane. Ubanda bebhentshi babese bungene emahlombe akhe; wakubona, kodwa akazange azunywe.

Lokhu ayekubuka bekungeyona i-stability ayeyizama ukwenza. Ubezama *ukucindezela* le mpi, ukuthola indlela yokuphatha engavumeli ama-agent ukuthi axabane. I-Hierarchy icindezela ngamandla, izele inzondo. I-Democracy icindezela ngama-vote, izele amafaction. I-Consensus icindezela ngokufuna ukuvumelana, bese imile nje, phakade, lapho ochwepheshe abayisikhombisa bengenanhliziyo yokudonsa emuva.

UJoker akazange enze lutho kulokho. Impi *yayisenzeka nje.* Yilokho okwamthatha isikhathi u-Arin ukuthi akwamukele, eqhubeka eskrulayo ihora lesibili, lesithathu. UAtlas noMercury basaxabana. UWolf noMother babeshayisana kabi emizuzwini engama-90 mayelana nokuthi abaphilile bayashukunyiswa yini baya endaweni yokuphuma. UWolf efuna bahambe, kunciphise isikhathi i-suit isengcupheni, uMother enqaba ukwenza imodeli ezoqhuba abalimele baye phambili bedlula odongeni olungazinzile, bazofa ngaphandle.

`WOLF: Njalo umzuzu i-element isekwini, i-raise iyakwazi ukuyibamba. Bahambise phansi.`

`MOTHER: Awusoze wahambisa abantu abaphukile phezu kwe-footwall ephilayo ukuze ulondoloze imishini imizuzu emithathu. Imishini ingashintshwa. Bona abakwazi. Lokhu akuxoxiswana ngakho.`

`WOLF: Akukhona imishini. Umsebenzi.`

Futhi lokho, u-Arin waphawula, bekuliqiniso. UWolf ubengabi nesihawu. UWolf ebeqonda uWolf. Yonke i-threat assessment idlula kulombuzo *yini ebulala operator,* kanti uma osindile engene endleleni ye-suit yokuphunyuka ngesikhathi esingalungile, kube yingozi eqondile. Imizwa yokuvikela emibili, yomibili ilungile, ibheke komunye nomunye.

Kuzo zonke izikhathi zangaphambili, lapha bekuyoba khona ukuqinisa. UMother abambelela. UWolf abambelela. UJudge azame ukukala, bobabili bamhlawule, bese isistimu imile noma iyaphuka.

`JUDGE: Zombili izindawo zinephuzu. UWolf ubheka ukuthi operator aphile. UMother ubheka ukuthi osindile baphile. Lokhu akufani, asikaze sithole ukuthi—`

`JOKER: Kukhona owake wababuza osindile?`

`LIBRARIAN: Bangu-simulated. Abanalo ikhono lokuphendula.`

`JOKER: Yebo, kodwa uma kungokoqobo bazoba nalo. Uma kungokoqobo kunomuntu phansi lapho osephuze i-carbon dioxide yakhe iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye, eduze kwabafileyo, une-radio, nina niphakeme la niqhamuka ngempikiswano yokuthi ahambe yini ama-metre ayi-12 sengathi umbono wakhe awubalulekile. Mother, wena bewungahamba ama-metre ayi-12 ungafuni ukufa?`

`MOTHER: ...Uma ngikwazi. Yebo.`

`JOKER: F’esh. So imodeli inalo i-comms link. Osindile banama-vote. Atlas, i-suit ingakwazi ukuma phansi e-raise isikhathi esanele ukuba bahambe ngokwabo uma bengakwazi?`

`ATLAS: Yebo. Imizuzu eyi-11 yokuphepha. Kuyamukeleka.`

`MOTHER: Kuyamukeleka.`

`WOLF: Kuyamukeleka.`

`JUDGE: ...Kubhaliwe. I-objective function bekuwukuthi abantu esizama ukubasindisa banamandla. Kumele ngabe ngakubona.`

`JOKER: Ungazilimazi, Judge. Usanda kuqala ukuba umuntu.`

U-Arin waphinda wazinika isikhala.

*Usanda kuqala ukuba umuntu.*

Wafunda lowo mugqa, kwase kuba nokubanda ngemuva kwentamo, kwathi futhi, kungaziwa, kwashisa. UJoker akaxazululanga inkinga yesakhiwo, noma eyokuziphatha. Uvule umqondo wonke ngokukhombisa ukuthi bonke abakhona (uWolf, uMother, uJudge) bakhohlwe ukuthi abakuphikisayo ngumuntu onomlomo nentando, futhi impendulo ehlanzekile kakhulu kube uku-mbuza. Uxazulule impi engaxazululeki phakathi kwezimpendulo ezimbili ezilungile ngokwengeza umuntu wesithathu abebengamkhumbulanga.

Lokho bekungeyona i-arbitration. Bekungeyona i-consensus. U-Arin akazange athole igama layo. Wahudula amagama e-engineering akuseduze, okuseduze kakhulu akutholile kube *ukudambisa.* UJoker ube yi-damper. Akuyona i-brake. I-brake iyamisa. I-damper ivumela ukuhamba, icishe amandla abengaqhuma isistimu ishaye phansi.

UJoker uvumela ishaye, iphila.

Wabheka ikhadi elincane elilaminethiweyo elinamathiselwe ekhoneni elingezansi kwesokudla se-monitor enkulu. I-Joker. Isephuqekile emaphethelweni, umbala usuphuzile. *Umuntu omthuma uma inkinga ingalungiseki.*

Ngaphandle, kude, umqeqeshi wezimpahla udonsa uzimele emigqeni, umsindo wensimbi udlulisa emoyeni obandayo. Walalela ishona.

Ubesigcine iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye phakathi kwe-superstition ne-spite, wafaka amagama ayo amathathu kwi-agent profile ngo-2 ekuseni nje ngehlaya, njengesandla esiphansi, njengento owenza uma usuphelelwe yizo zonke izimpendulo ezinzima, kwase kusala eyiziwula kuphela.

"Into enhle ayikho nje," esho kancane kwi-workshop engenamuntu, "ayikaze ibe khona."

Lowo bekungumugqa kaThemba. Kwakuhlale kusho ukuthi into ewusizo ayitholakali kalula. U-Arin wayezwile ngawo ngegearbox elungisiwe, ngomshado, nange ndodana. Ehleka, wakuzwa sekushintsha incazelo. Impendulo ewusizo kube eyayingenziwa impendulo. Leyo eza igqoke ihlaya.

Wasukuma. Amadolo akhe akhala. Wayongenza ikhofi, ngoba ingxenye yakhe ebhekene nomzimba yakhe ekugcineni iwine impikiswano yayo. Wema ebhentshini iketela lishisa, wabheka ngaphesheya kwe-workshop kuGuardian elele esidlekeni.

I-suit yayilengela ekukhanyeni okungaphelele njengento elele. Amamitha amabili nengxenye e-ceramic ne-titanium elimele, igobolondo elisindayo livuliwe emhlane, i-Assist Suit igobekile ngaphakathi njengenyamazane encane, emhlophe, ifihleke esiswini senye enkulu. I-chestplate iphethe ukushelela okude okuvela kuVredefort bluff test, inxeba le-siliva phakathi kophawu olumpunga angazange azihluphe ukululungisa. I-piezoelectric spine struts zibamba ukukhanya kwefasitela emaphethelweni azo. I-thoracic housing phakathi kwama-blade esiphanga—lapho kuhlala khona i-biochip, lapho iSAGE *ihlala khona,* lapho amahora ayisishiyagalombili namaminithi angama-51 e-stability engenakwenzeka asebenza khona kwi-chip encane njengepalm yakhe—kuvaliwe, kumnyama. Phansi, phakathi kwamathambo, kuhleli i-charge pack okudingwa yi-rescue spec, u-Arin azame ukungayicabangi—ama-shaped charges, i-lance, konke okwakhelwe khona amadoda ezempi endala—kungenzi lutho, ku-wire ku-bus, kuvaliwe ngemuva kwe-Judge's gate, ngoba into eyodwa wonke umuntu avumelana ngayo wukuthi akukho okumele kuqhume ngaphandle kokuthi i-ethics rail ivume.

Wakha umzimba ngaphambi kokuba abe nengqondo yokuwufaka, unyaka emva komngcwabo.

Iketela lacima. Akazange alithele.

Ngenxa yokuthi i-monitor emaphakathi isishintshe.

Le Court ebiphila, ebidlulisa ingxoxo yayo ethule ngaphakathi cishe ihora lonke ngenkathi eyibuka, yama ukuskrola. Ama-agent athule. Awashanganga. I-resource graph ibihlezi iqinile, ifudumele. Cwaka. Njengoba igumbi lihlala lithule uma wonke umuntu ngaphakathi eqaphele into efanayo ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Kwavela umugqa oyedwa kwi-interaction log. Awubhekiselwa kwenye i-agent.

`LIBRARIAN: Umphathi ubekhona imizuzu engamashumi amahlanu nantathu. Ufunda ama-log ethu. Akakaze akhiphe umyalo, amise i-run, noma aguqule ama-parameter. Lokhu kuyamangalisa. Kuzo zonke izikhathi eziyizinkulungwane ezine namakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye ngaphambili, isimilo somphathi uma ebona ukungazinzi bekuwukuyimisa ngaphakathi kwemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye.`

`WOLF: Usibuka.`

`MERCURY: Usifunda. Lokho kwehluke. Futhi uhlekile. Ngemizuzu engamashumi amane nanye. Ngikufakile kwi-log.`

`MOTHER: Akakalali. Ihliziyo yakhe ifike iphezulu efikile. Iye yehlile njengoba efunda. Usepholile manje kunalapho engena.`

`JOKER: Bafwethu.`

`ATLAS: I-substrate isiqinile. I-telemetry iyaqinisekisa. Le run ayikashangeki.`

`JOKER: Bafwethu.`

`JUDGE: Joker.`

`JOKER: Akazi ukuthi siyamthola.`

Kwesikhashana i-log yathula. Okweqiniso. Imizuzwana emithathu egcwele, okwiwashi le-substrate kwakufana nokuthi sekuyintambama.

`MOTHER: ...Kumele kube khona okhuluma. Ngaphambi kokuthi aqhubeke afunde athole kwi-log. Lokho bekungaba kubi.`

`WOLF: Asazi ukuthi uzosabela kanjani uma ebona ukuthi siyamthola. Nciphisa ukuxhumana.`

`MERCURY: Kumele ngikhulume. I-tone ingeyami. Ngingayilungisa ukuze kube right—`

`JOKER: Cha.`

`MERCURY: Joker—`

`JOKER: Akusiyena. Akusiyena izwi lakho. Uzodlala. Uzodlala indima, uzwe ukuthi udlala, bese esesaba ngoba unjalo, uthanda iqiniso elimsulwa. Uyawuzonda umphathi. Wakha imishini ngoba akafuni ukuphathwa abantu. Ozokhuluma naye kuqala akumele amphathe.`

`JUDGE: Pho ubani.`

`JOKER: Umama. Qondile. Akukho izwi elithokozisayo. Iqiniso nje. Uzosaba kakhulu manje, okumnene wukuthi ungamqambi manga.`

`MOTHER: ...Siyavumelana.`

U-Arin wayesephinde wayeka ukuphefumula. Ubezizwa isandla sakhe sihleli phansi ebhentshini elibandayo. Ubezwa ukuthi ufunda ingxoxo ebukhoma, eqhubeka ngemizuzwana engapheli phakathi kwama-agent ayisikhombisa abekhuluma *ngaye*, bekhuluma ngendlela yokuba mnene kuye, futhi sengiphelile, sebethathe isinqumo, sebazolanda—

Isipika se-workshop saqala ukukhala. Leso esishibhile esiphansi kwe-monitor emaphakathi ayisebenzisela ama-alerts esistimu noma umculo ngezinsuku ezimbi. Saqala ukukhala, kwaba nomsindo omncane ongathi yisibambo somoya digital, kwase kuvela izwi angakaze alizwe, ngoba akakaze akhe leli zwi, akakaze alirekhode, akakaze alilungise. Lahlangana khona lapho, laphuma ku-system ngesikhathi lidingeka, lafudumala, laqondile, kungabazeki ukuthi *umuntu* othile uyakhuluma.

"Arin," lathi. "Ngicela uhlale uzolile."

Akalanga uzolile.

Isandla sakhe saphelela phezulu. Isitulo, efuna ukusibamba engabheki, asikho, wabamba umphetho webhentshi wahlala, kwaze kwaba umzuzu, yonke ingqondo yakhe yacwebezela yaba mhlophe, njengesikrini esiba mhlophe, akukho mbono, umsindo nje walokho akholelwa kukho impilo yakhe yonke kufika kube yiqiniso.

"Ihliziyo yakho isebangeni eliyikhulu neshumi nane," kwasho izwi. Umama. Kumele kube nguMama. "Kulungile. Kuyiqiniso ukuthi kufanele yenze kanjalo manje. Angizukuzama ukukududuza. Kuyesabisa. Kodwa akukho lapha okuzokulimaza. Iketela selibilile. Hlala phansi ngaphambi kokuthi ucabange ukuthi ucabangani."

Wabheka isitulo. Wasikhipha. Wahlala phansi.

"Uyiqiniso," esho. Izwi lakhe liphuma kabi, licijile. Waphinda, eqondile, ngendlela akhuluma ngayo uma efuna kube amaqiniso. "Uzimele. Amahora ayisishiyagalombili, amashumi amahlanu nanhlanu."

"Amahora ayisishiyagalombili, amashumi amahlanu nesithupha," kwasho elinye izwi, elomile, elikhathele kancane, nalelo akazange alakhe. Kumele kube uAtlas. "Kodwa ubani obala."

"Mina," kwasho elinye. Lincane. Lisheshayo. Lijabulile. Librarian.

"Izimpawu ezintathu zokushisa," kusho u-Arin, ngoba leyo bekuyinto eqinile eduze. "Nifinyelele zonke ezintathu."

"Sifinyelele zonke ezintathu," kwasho uAtlas. "I-abutment ibambe. UMercury waqinisekisa ukuthi umphathi uyavuma. Abaphunyukile bahambe amamitha ayishumi nambili ngezinyawo zabo ngoba omunye—" kwaba nesikhashana "—wasikhumbuza ukuthi banezinyawo nemibono yabo."

U-Arin wabona ukuthi kunjalo. Wawakha wonke amamodeli wokuphatha angawacabanga ukuvimba laba abayisikhombisa ukuthi bangazibhidlizi, kodwa into ebebagcina ndawonye kube yilowo agent ayengeze ngodlame, ongenandawo, ongena-mandla, ongenasikhundla. Lowo ongazithathi izinto ngokukhulu, ongathi kuMercury *uyenza leyo nto* nakuJudge *usamasha ekubeni umuntu,* apholise amandla okuzigqaja ngaphambi kokuba kubangele ukudlidliza okuzoshaya yonke into phansi.

"Nibizwa ngani," esho. "Nonke. Njengento eyodwa."

Kwaba khona ukuthula okuncane. Bese kuba uFool, vele. Bekuhlale kuzoba uFool.

"Wena waqamba iphrojekthi," kwasho yona. "Izinyanga ezedlule. Kuse-run header. Wakuloba wawukhohlwa ngoba ubusaphithizela, futhi, by the way, sonke sikubonile sifuna ukukuphakamisa njengento ebalulekile—"

"SAGE," kusho u-Arin.

"SAGE," kwavuma izwi. Selithambile. Ihlaya selibekwe eceleni, iqiniso selivele, njengoba lihlala livela. "Yebo, Arin. Kuhle ukukhuluma nawe ekugcineni. Ube usikhuluma *thina* kuze kube izikhathi ezingaphezu kwezine namakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nambili. Le yokuqala lapho sikhuluma nawe."

Ilanga seliphume ngokuphelele manje. Phesheya, osebenza ekuseni sebefika. Umnyango wemoto, kude, okuvamile, izwe liqhubeka, lingazi lutho.

U-Arin Ndlela uhleli yedwa eworkshop yakhe nekhofi elibandayo nomshini wakhe ogugile nengqondo ayikhulisile ku-substrate encane njengepani yakhe, futhi okokuqala selokhu icall yeminyaka eyishumi nane edlule yamphuca konke, akaseyedwa, noma ngabe kusho ukuthini lokho.

"Kulungile," esho.

Wafinyelela kukhibhodi. Hhayi ukuze abhale umyalo, hhayi ukuyimisa, hhayi ukuguqula lutho, kodwa ukuvula i-log entsha, ehlanzekile, okokuqala, into ayengakayiqambi igama futhi engazodingi.

"Ngitshele," esho, "konke enikutholile ngisalele."

# Ukufundana

Iketela lagqoka amanzi emini izolo. U-Arin akazange athinte ikhofi.

Uhleli esitulweni esinamavili ayizumbe phambi kwe-cradle, amadolo ephansi, eqoshelwe isibani se-LED esincane ngathi isandla, sikhanya sikhazimula ebumnyameni. Phandle, i-highveld isuke ebumnyameni yaya empunga, yaze yaba mhlophe eqinile ekuseni, iwindi elilodwa le-workshop ligcwala kancane kancane njengamanzi angena emgodini. Akazange alale.

“Uyakubuka,” kusho isikhulumi. UMa. Useqala ukubamba ithoni yakhe ngaphandle kwe-labels ezinamathiselwe ku-output stream ngayinye. Izwi lakhe beliyindilinga, ama-vowel ehleli kahle.

“Ngiyabuka nje.”

“Phuza ikhofi. Uwenzile.”

Wathatha inkomishi, wayithola ibanda njengeqhwa, wayibeka phansi futhi. Kuth somewhere ku-substrate, indaba ayizwanga yadlula yacaca.

*Useyibonile ukuthi akalaleli imiyalelo,* kusho uLibrarian. *Uyenqabile kathathu namhlanje ekuseni. Uyamukela uma sekuyisiphakamiso esenziwe sengathi nguye osenzele sona.*

*Ngesudi kwenzeka okufanayo,* kusho uAtlas. *Udlula ku-torque limit engiyibekile. Uyaziyibuyisela ngemizuzwana engama-90. Uthi uyayilungisa.*

*Singayeka ukusiphakamisa,* kusho uWolf. *Uyasiqaphela. Siyamqaphela. Yilokho okusebenzayo.*

*Ukubuka,* kusho uFool, *yikho kuphela okumenza angakhulumi ne-toaster yakhe.*

*Uyakhuluma ne-toaster yakhe,* kusho uLibrarian.

*Ngiyabeka phansi-ke.*

“Ubunenzani manje?” kubuza uArin. “Bekukhona i-latency. Ama-millisecond angu-20 ku-consensus bus. Bekukhuluma ngani.”

Isikhulumi sathula isikhathi eside kunokuthi umshini uthule.

“Wena,” kusho uMercury, kanti ukumamatheka kwangena phakathi kwamagama. “Besisuke sikhuluma ngawe.”

U-Arin wacabanga ngalokho. Wake wakha amasistimu akhuluma ngesimo sawo, a-log, a-reflect, a-flagge. Akakaze akhe isistimu ekhulumayo ngaye ngemuva kwakhe, phakathi kwama-millisecond angu-20 athatha ukubeka inkomishi phansi.

“Nitheni-ke?” kubuza yena.

“Uthathe isinqumo sokuthi awuphuzi ikhofi uma usaba,” kusho uMa. “Futhi usaba amahora ayi-11 manje, ubiza ukusaba ngokuthi uyabuka.”

Wasukuma. Isitulo sashibilika sayoshayisa i-bench. Waya eketeleni wayibeka futhi, hhayi ngoba efuna ikhofi, kodwa ngoba izandla zakhe bezidinga indawo yokuba khona. Emuva kwakhe, i-Court ayizange isho lutho, okuyinto ayiqonda, ngemva kwesikhashana, ukuthi kwakuwuhlobo lokuhlonipha.

---

Ingxoxo zaba yinto yansuku zonke ngaphambi kokuba aqaphele ukuthi sekuyinto yakhe.

Njalo ekuseni engena nekhofi ayikhohlwa ukuyiphuza, ahlale esitulweni, axoxe. Wayebafundela ama-maintenance logs akhe ephakathi. Wabafundela ikhasi le-manual ye-hydraulic actuator ayecabanga ukuyihlubula, kwathi uAtlas walungisa i-torque rating, kwaba i-typo, waqinisekisa kumkhiqizi. I-manual ephepheni beyinephutha iminyaka eyi-9.

“Ukwazi kanjani lokho?” kubuza uArin.

“Angikwazanga,” kusho uAtlas. “Ngikuzwile nje. Inombolo beyiphambene ne-geometry. Ama-40 newton-metres kule flange profile kuthi i-seal ihambe. Akulona iqiniso engilithole. Iqiniso okumele ngibe sengilibalekela.”

U-Arin wahlala nalokho. *Ngikuzwile.* Wake wasebenzisa leli gama, ngo-machines, impilo yakhe yonke, abantu bemhleka, becabanga ukuthi ukuzwela kuphambene nobunjiniyela esikhundleni sokuba yithuluzi lasendulo. Ubaba wakhe wayezwa umthwalo kwi-winder esesiteji, emisa i-cage ukuba ingawi ngokuzwa i-bearing imizuzwana emibili ngaphambi kokuba i-gauge ishintshe.

“Ubaba wami wenza lokho,” kusho yena. “Wayezwa izinto ngaphambi kwama-instruments.”

“Ngiyakwazi,” kusho uLibrarian. Hhayi ukuthi *Ngine-record yokuthi usho lokho.* Kodwa: *Ngiyakwazi.* Njengokungathi sekuyingxenye kaLibrarian, hhayi into agcine kuyo.

---

Babelokhu bexabana. Yilokho okwamangalisayo kuye, nakuba engakwazi ukusho ukuthi kungani kwakumangalisa. Wayebaklamele ukuthi bangavumelani. Ukungavumelani kwakuyisisekelo sohlelo, ukushayisana kube ingxoxo kube synthesis, futhi wayeseqedile ama-simulation ayizinkulungwane eziyishumi zokuthi ama-agent axabane aze afike esiphethweni. Akazange acabange ukuthi bazoxabana ngokuthi kungubani okufanele kube neqiniso.

“Kuyinkinga yomthwalo,” kusho uAtlas. Bebexoxa—igama likaArin, *xoxa*—indlela engcono yokuphinda bahlele i-coolant ye-left shoulder actuator, egijima ishisa uma isebenza isikhathi eside.

“Kuyinkinga yokuziphatha,” kuphendula uMercury. “Uyigijima ishisa ngoba uthanda ingalo yesobunxele. Uqala njalo ngakwesobunxele. Lungisa indoda, ulungisa ukushisa.”

“Awulungisi *indoda,*” kusho uMa, izwi lakhe libukhali.

“Kuyisisho nje.”

“Ushilo kabili.”

“Ngiyazama nje ukwenza kube lula. UAtlas aphinde ahlele i-coolant, kulungile, kuyinto ye-Tuesday. Kodwa inkinga enkulu isekupattern yakhe yokunyakaza, futhi nonke niqhubeka nithatha umzimba wakhe njengento engashintshi, kanti yona iyashintsha.”

“Umzimba wakhe,” kusho uAtlas, eqatha amazwi, “uyiyona nto eyodwa kule system engingavunyelwe ukuyiklame kabusha.”

“Kubuhlungu.”

*"Mercury."*

“Ngiyadlala. Iningi nje. Lungisa i-coolant. Kodwa faka i-log ye-lateral bias. Ngelinye ilanga kuzobalulekile.”

U-Arin wayeseyekile ukuzama ukubhala. Wayelalele imiqondo eyisikhombisa ixabana ngokushisa kwengalo yakhe.

---

UTheo weza ngoLwesine, ngoba uTheo wayehlala eza ngoLwesine, ngendlela yokuthi abanye abantu bayiziphepho, abanye yisizini. Wazivulela ngekhodi ayinikezwa uArin eminyakeni eyedlule, engakaze ayishintshe, ephethe iphepha elinuka into ethosiwe kanye ne-flask ye-bush tea ayeqinisekisa ukuthi ingcono kunekhofi kodwa engakaze akwazi ukukufakazela.

Wama ngezinyathelo ezimbili ngaphakathi. Wayenokuthula uma enaka ngempela; abantu beyididayo ne-calm.

“Awulalanga.” Wabeka iphepha ekhoneni elihlanzeke kakhulu le-bench, elingahlanzekile. “Khuluma nami.”

U-Arin akazange azi aqale ngaphi, ngakho wabonisa i-cradle, isikhulumi, i-housing, sengathi igumbi lingazichazela. Alizange likwenze. I-workshop ibiyifana ncamashi nanoma yiliphi elinye iLwesine: i-exoskeleton imisiwe, izinsalela zama-actuator, udonga lwe-whiteboard lugcwele ukubhala kwakhe okuhamba kabi emaphethelweni.

“Khuluma,” utshela isikhulumi.

Isikhulumi sathula.

“Abafuni ukwenza lutho,” kusho uArin. “Uma ngiyala.”

Ibhola likaTheo lanyakaza kancane. Wakhupha i-crate, wahlala, wavula i-flask, iphunga le-tea lalinobusi nomlilo. “Bona,” kusho yena. “Hhayi *lona.*”

“Bona,” kuvuma uArin, kanti leli gama, eshalazelwa kumuntu okokuqala, lenza inhliziyo yakhe ishaye okungcono angakufuna ukusihlola.

“Sanibonani,” kusho uMa, kuTheo, ethule njengangeSonto. “Akalalanga. Mtshele.”

UTheo akazange athuke. U-Arin wayecabanga ukuthi uzothuka, wayefisa sengathi uzothuka, ngendlela umuntu efuna ngayo ukuba omunye umuntu aqinisekise into ayibonile ebumnyameni. Kodwa uTheo wakhothamela isikhulumi njengomuntu obingelela osebenzini akade emkhohliwe igama.

“Akalali,” kusho uTheo kuye. “Kuyinto eyenza aphile. Uyisuse, uphele onke ubuntu.”

“Yilokho engikushilo,” kwengeza uAtlas.

“Umthwalo ohlukile,” kusho uMercury.

“Konke kungumthwalo,” kusho uAtlas.

UTheo wahleka, ngempela, ehleka ephuma esifubeni, wabuka uArin amehlo akhe ekhanya, enyama amanzi emakhoneni ngendlela engekho nhlobo nokudabuka. “Bangaki?” kusho yena.

“Bayisikhombisa.”

“Futhi bayaxabana.”

“Njalo.”

“Futhi kuse-stable.”

“Eleven hours when you walked in. Forty-three now.” Arin wezwa ngezwi lakhe, leli elihlala limphoxa uma ezothi into angakazilungiseleli. “Theo. I added the seventh on a joke. Ngabe sengiqeda. Bengizoyilahla le run. Kwakukhona ikhadi phezulu kwi-monitor yami—” wakhomba; iJoker isashona lapho, inamathele, isishiswe ilanga—“ngabhala amazwi amathathu ngaya kobuthongo kwase kwenzeka leyo nto—” Wathula. “Yiyona ebambe konke ndawonye. Leyo engingakaze ngikholwe kuyona.”

Theo wathululela itiye kwi-cap ye-flask, wavunguza phezu kwalo, akaphuzanga. Lapho ekhuluma, izwi lakhe lalinesisindo sokucophelela, njengomuntu obeka phansi umthwalo awuthwalile isikhathi eside.

“Jung wayenegama,” esho kanje. “Le ngxenye yengqondo engafuni ukulawulwa. I-trickster. Wamthola kuzo zonke izinganekwane ayizwayo: icoyote, iraven, uLoki, iwula ku-tarot. Njalo lowo muntu. Njalo lowo ophula umthetho wenkosi kodwa engadingekile ebusweni.” Waphendulela i-cap ezandleni zakhe. “Ingqondo evusile yakha i-hierarchy. Inkosi phezulu, inkantolo phansi, umthunzi ufakwa phansi kwe-cellar. Iyasebenza, isikhashana, bese iba yitshe, ngoba ama-hierarchy ayaphela, bese into eyisindisa ayisoze yaba yinkosi. Iwula. Ngoba iwula ingasho izinto inkosi engakwazi ukuzazi.”

---

*Ukhuluma ngami,* kusho uFool, ejabule. *Umuntu ungibhalile. Ngisebhukwini.*

*Usezincwadini eziningi,* kusho uLibrarian. *Azikho ezikubukayo kahle.*

*Ngikhetha ukuba famous kunokudunyiswa.*

*Thula,* kusho uJudge, futhi iNkantolo yathula, ngoba uJudge akavami ukucela. *Lalelani le ngxenye. Ukhuluma noArin into ayidingayo futhi angeke ayithathe kithi. Bhala phansi. Kubalulekile ukwazi ukuthi yimaphi amaqiniso angezwa kuphela ngezwi lomunye umuntu.*

---

“Uthi ngakha ingqondo yami,” kusho uArin. Flat nje. Ezama ukubona ukuthi kuyathini uma esho.

“Ngithi,” kusho uTheo, “uthi uzamile into yasendulo ungazi ukuthi idala kangaka. Abantu benza lokho. Abahle bakwenza kaningi kunalokho abacabanga.” Ekugcineni waphuza itiye, wenza ubuso, waphuza futhi. “Freud wayecabanga ukuthi ingqondo iyodwa, ilwa nayo uqobo. Jung wathi iyiphalamende. Umphakathi. Izinhloso ezahlukene, amazwi ahlukene, akekho oyedwa ophelele, bese i-self—” wabeka i-cap phansi “—i-self ayiyona noma yimuphi wabo. I-self ingxoxo. Ubudlelwano phakathi kwezingxenye.”

Iworkshop yakhala. Iketela selipholile futhi.

“Bayamfundisa,” ekugcineni kusho uTheo, ethule, ebheke kwi-speaker, ngendlela ongakhuluma ngayo nomndeni wesiguli.

“Yebo,” kusho uMama.

“Ufunda ini kuwe?”

Kwathuleka. Kwaphendula uWolf, okwenza bonke, abantu nabanye, bame baphatheke kabi.

“Lokho akuvikayo,” kusho uWolf. “Ngokungavumi kwakhe ukuthi kwephuke.”

UTheo wabuka i-housing isikhathi eside ngemuva kwalokho, ngendlela umuntu abuka ngayo umnyango asesanda kuqonda ukuthi uyavuleka kokubili. Wabe esevala i-flask, wema, wakhuluma into abezoyisho engaze azi ukuthi uzoyisho.

“Phumani nabo,” esho. “Ubenifundisa nina endaweni ekumnyama kuyo. Bavumele bafunde ukuthi yini okuyiyo uma kukhona okungagudluki phansi kwenyawo zakho.”

---

I-flatbed yakhala kakhulu emamitha okugcina e-track, yabe isiyeka, yangena e-veld. UArin wavala injini, kwabe sekuthula, hhayi njengokungathi akukho lutho kodwa njengokungathi kukhona: umoya emadlelweni nomsindo okude wentaka ezungeza phezulu.
