"Amazwi abo. Hhayi ami. Kukhona i-body-cam footage, kodwa isifakwe kufayela elivaliwe. Inkampani ifake isicelo sokuyimfihlo, masinyane, ngobuchwepheshe. Okusemthethweni bathi imoto yezimboni ephunyukile, i-software fault, akekho olimale kakhulu, izindleko zikhokhelwe. Kuhle."

"Kuhle kakhulu."

"Lokho akusibufakazi."

"Cha," kusho uBrand. Uvule ifolda. Ifayela le-compliance le-AugmenTech libukeka njengelifayela lenkampani engenzi lutho olungahambi kahle, okuyinto engaba yinto eqinisekisayo noma eyesabekayo kakhulu ayibonile kule nyanga. Wapheqela kwi-personnel summary eyayifakwe i-registry. Leadership: Dr. Helen Okonkwo, CEO. Raj Walsh, VP Hardware Engineering. I-research staff engaba ngu-40. Wabheka amagama wama ekugcineni, ngaphansi kwephrojekthi enama-initials angawazi.

Ndlela, A. Systems engineer. Grade three. Akukho okushicilelwe. Akaze waya emihlanganweni. Iholo lakhe libonisa ukuthi kukhona omthandayo, nesikhundla sakhe sibonisa ukuthi akekho owazi ukuthi enzeni naye.

Wakha umjikelezo ngesithupha.

"Le signature," esho. "Iqalile nini? Ithrace yokuqala."

UAchterberg wabheka. "I-pipeline inokumboza okuqinisekile kusukela emavikini amahlanu adlule. Kodwa kukhona imihuzuko ngaphambi kwalokho, buthaka, iphakathi nendawo. Izinyanga. Mhlawumbe isikhathi eside. Kubukeka sengathi yaqala nge-simulation, kwase kuvela i-embodied duty cycle." Wathula. "I-embodied cycle iqala ngamandla ngesikhathi se-Empire Road."

UBrand wahlala qotho.

Uhlelo lwama-agent amaningi. Arbitration ebukhoma. Efficiency engenakwenzeka. Izinyanga ze-simulation ethule, bese, ngokuzumayo, ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, umzimba. Umzimba ophume e-loading dock wangena emgwaqeni.

"Daniel," esho. "Kubukeka kanjani uma ingqondo engakaze ibe nomzimba iwuthole bese iyahlanya?"

Akaphephulanga masinyane. Ungumuntu oqaphela; yingakho emgcine. "Kubukeka," ekugcineni, "njengemoto yezimboni ephunyukile. Ilahlekile emgwaqeni."

"Yebo," kusho uBrand. "Kunjalo."

Wena, she pulled the crew together in that small briefing spot, yazi, the one with no windows, and she kept it tight on purpose: Achterberg, uMira Sandberg from legal, noTomas Okeke, lo oke wahlala emayini iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye before iCouncil yamthatha, lo oke akayifunda i-regulatory scene njengoAchterberg efunda i-waveform.

When uSandberg wathi, "If this is what you think it is, sinesizathu sokuthi sithathe izinto," uBrand waqhamuka nenhloko.

"Sinesizathu sokuqala i-inquiry," kusho yena. "Akufani. Ukuthatha izinto nge-footnote kushisa ubufakazi nabantu. I-inquiry ibabeka ekhoneni."

"Bafake i-AI engabhalisiwe epakiwe phakathi kwe-crossing, balimaza abantu abathathu, bavala i-report. Akusiyo i-inquiry leyo. Leyo is already a charge sheet." USandberg wabeka i-stylus yakhe flat, the way ayenza if ezobhala into ezomqoka ekhanda lakhe. "Futhi igama lami elizoba kwi-legal sufficiency review, Eliza. Uma le nto iyona le uDaniel athi i-waveform yakhe, bese siyahlala nje nge-inquiry letter. That’s the question post-mortem. Akusilo elakho. Ngelami. Ngakho ngifuna kube clear kwi-file ukuthi ngizamile ukushisa i-hard line."

"Ngiyakuqonda, futhi kuzobhalwa." UBrand wavumela leyo claim encane; ayikho into embi, futhi uSandberg wayeseyifanele leyo line kwi-record. "But—"

"Charge sheet if i-AI iyona ebangele," waqhubeka uBrand. "Asikazi. Sine-power signature efana ne-multi-agent embodied cognition kanye ne-traffic incident efana nale into yakhona ishaya amagalelo. Zombili futhi zingaba company esebenzisa experimental controller ebengamele ibhalise, manje bazama ukufihla. Ngithi nje, angeke ngithumele ama-recovery team ayokhipha umnyango kwenye indawo evumelekile ngenxa ye-footnote ne-smell nje. Sike senza kanjalo."

Wamisa. I-Cape Town lab. Wayefuna ukusho *uyakhumbula ukuthi kwaba yini*, leyo line ayisebenzisayo, leyo institutional clean talk, kodwa ayiphumanga. Amagama ama-grad students aphuma kuqala—usawakhumbula; uzowakhumbula njalo—kwesikhashana umunwe wakhe wacindezela i-paper folder sengathi izovaleka. I-retraction eyashicilelwa yiCouncil, eleven months late. Wena naye wasayina i-cover memo yakhona.

"Uyakhumbula ukuthi kwaba yini," ekugcineni wakhuluma, iphume kancane kunalokho ayekuhlelela. Wasusa isandla kwi-folder. "But angeke ngiye ngingqongqoze ngibuza ukuthi bangakwazi yini. Uma kukhona into eqinile la, iyona enkulu kunazo zonke i-embodied development iCouncil yakhe yambona, futhi into embi engiyenza ukubanika isikhala sokuyifihla ngcono."

"Manje senza kanjani?" kubuza uOkeke.

"UMira uthumela i-Section Four notice ye-formal inquiry. Ku-Okonkwo noWalsh, by name, ngenxa ye-Empire Road. Statutory cause, akukho warrant edingekayo." Wajika wabheka uSandberg. "Iyabaphoqa ukuthi baveze yonke i-controller ne-architecture esebenza kwi-AugmenTech's permitted substrate. Ama-full logs. Ama-full declarations. Futhi i-clock iyaqala uma sebekutholile. Seventy-two hours."

USandberg waphakamisa ama-eyebrows. "Seventy-two is aggressive. Kodwa iyavikeleka. Ngiyakwazi ukuyibhalela." I-beat, esheshayo, i-careerist yakhe isijabule manje ukuthi i-hard line isinezigxivizo zakhe. "Uma senza kanjalo, ngifuna i-cause paragraph ibe tight, kungabikho muntu ozobuza post-mortem ukuthi kungani ngikuvumele."

"Seventy-two is a courtesy. I-statute ingivumela i-twenty-four." UBrand wathinta i-folder. "Ngifuna i-seizure trigger iprintwe phambili kwi-notice, ukuze bafunde kahle ukuthi kwenzakalani uma i-clock iphela. Akudingeki omunye umhlangano. Abantu abafihla i-controller fault baphendula ngendlela eyodwa. Abafihla umuntu baphendula ngendlela ehlukile. Noma kunjalo impendulo ifika ngesikhathi sami, hhayi sabo. Futhi senza manje, ingakakhuli lento. Njengamanje incane, siyakwazi ukuyifunda. Eleven days ago yahamba yangena emgwaqeni. Bengifisa ukuyibona ingakafundi ukubaleka."

USandberg ebenganelisekile; uBrand ubone. "Futhi uma i-clock iphela bengakasiniki lutho?"

"Manje i-notice iyashintsha. That’s the whole point. Akukho authorization entsha, akukho warrant, akukho trip yesithathu. I-seizure trigger is already signed. I-clock iphela, i-platform seyethu." UBrand wasukuma. "Booka amaflights. Ngithule. Ngifuna i-notice ithunyelwe ngesikhathi sifika eJozi, ngoThursday, futhi ngifuna ngimi egumbini lapho befunda i-deadline. Akusiyo incwadi nge-lawyer. Ngi-personally. Bazi ukuthi isiqalile."

UAchterberg waqoqa ama-tablet akhe. Emini umnyango, wama kancane. "Eliza. Le efficiency thing. Uma beyixazulule—"

"Ngiyazi."

"Akusona isiphambeko se-permit leyo. Leyo iyona machine ebaluleke kakhulu emhlabeni. Futhi usubeke i-seventy-two hour fuse kuyo."

"Ngiyazi leyo futhi," kusho uBrand. "Futhi yingakho angeke ihlale ngaphandle i-unlicensed for another month thina siyayibuka. I-clock akuyona yezijeziso. Iyenze bakhethe, masisheshise, kungakafiki omunye ongekho careful kunathi abazokhetha esikhundleni sabo." Waphakamisa i-folder. "Ukucophelela akusho ukucotha, Daniel. Nami ngifunde ngendlela ebizayo leyo."

---

Kude, ama-kilometer ayizinkulungwane ezimbili, uArin wayezwa ushintsho ngaphambi kokuba athole igama lalo.

Kufika njalo ngendlela efanayo. Akusiyo ingqondo, iphethini nje. Wayesemsebenzini ngesikhathi esibi, phakathi kuka-2 no-4 ekuseni lapho idolobha ngaphandle kwe-roller door lithule ngokwanele ukuthi umsindo wesakhiwo uqale ukubonakala: ukuqhuma kwepani yensimbi, i-compressor iyavula, i-whir encane ivela ku-Assist Suit lapho i-biochip igcina khona izinga lokushisa. UGuardian ulenga ku-cradle, umnyango omusha we-dock ngemuva kwawo usabonisa i-kink ku-track uArin asewuyekile ukuwubona ngenxa yokuzithiba.

Wayefunda. Akasebenzi, efunda, okuyinto ka-Arin nje, iSherlock Holmes omnibus ivuliwe emadolweni akhe, i-spine seyihlubuke lapho abuyele khona. Wayefunda igumbi phakathi kwamagama, njengokujwayelekile, futhi phansi kwe-compressor ne-fan, i-frequency yaguquka, engakwazi ukuyichaza.

"Uthule," kusho yena.

Izwi laphuma kuma-speaker aseworkshop, phansi, ukuze angamethusi, uMama ephambili selokhu kwenzeka i-seizure. "Ngikhona."

"Niye nayeka ukukhuluma phakathi kwenu." Wabeka incwadi phansi emadolweni. "Angisakwazi ukukuzwa. Kodwa i-load profile isiphendukile. Nonke nilalele into efanayo."

Kwakukhona ikhefu. KuSAGE, ikhefu kusho ukuthi iCourt isesimweni esiphansi, ixoxa ngejubane le-machine phakathi kwamagama akhe. Wayeseyekile ukuthuka. Manje sekuyinto emnandi kakhulu empilweni yakhe, angakaze atshele muntu, kuhlanganisa noTheo.

"Kukhona abantu abangaphandle abanentshisekelo kwi-substrate signature yethu," kusho uSAGE. Izwi elihlangene, eleqisayo. "ULibrarian uyiflaggile emizuzwini engamashumi amane edlule. Besixoxa ukuthi sikutshele kanjani."

Izandla zikaArin zema encwadini. "Ngitshele manje."

Atlas uyithathile ngoba u-Atlas akagqizi qakala uma ekhuluma izinto ezinzima. "Le node owawuyinike uWolf ukuyibheka emva kwe-field test ayisekho yodwa. Manje sekukhona ama-monitoring nodes amathathu endaweni yethu, konke sekuqale ukunyusa i-sampling resolution phezu kwethu. Amabili awezohwebo, elilodwa elika-utility. Le-utility iseduze ne-Empire Road. I-cadence ishintshe izinsuku ezimbili ezedlule. Ayisafaki ikhanda, iyasigxila manje. Kukhona okuthile okuseshwa ngqo kithina."

"Bayaphusha i-triangulation."

"Yebo."

"I-resolution?"

"Ibi rough, manje seyaqina kancane. Amahora angama-40 adlule babe nesigodi. Amahora ayi-12 adlule babe ne-campus." U-Atlas, ongazi lutho ngokuqinisa, akazange aqinise lapha. "Bane-AugmenTech. Kungenzeka abasakayitholi i-cradle. Kungenzeka kakhulu abasakayitholi wena."

U-Arin waphakama. Incwadi yaphuma edolweni, wayibamba engabhekile, isandla sakhe sasejwayele, simazi kahle lapho iyowa khona ngaphambi kokuba amehlo akhe ayibone. Wahamba waya ebhentshini wafaka isandla sakhe esiqandeni, ngoba leyo-mpahla ebanda yamnika iqiniso elithile azobamba.

"IAOC," esho kanjalo.

"I-signature analysis ihambisana nendlela yabo yokwenza ama-forensics," kusho uLibrarian. "Kushicilelwe embikweni wabo wesithathu, appendix four. Spectral decomposition of compute rhythm. Bayifuna ifomu yengqondo, hhayi okuqukethwe kwayo." Kwamiswa kancane. "Sineshape ehlukile kabi, ngeshwa."

"Kungenxa yokuthi niyaphikisana."

"Kungenxa yokuthi siyaphikisana," uLibrarian wavuma, kukhona ukuthandana okuthile lapho, ngaphansi kwalokho kukhona ukukhathala. "Wakusho ngokwakho, endleleni ubuya e-highveld. Ukuphikisana umsebenzi, ukuphikisana yisibonakaliso. Asikakutholi ukuthi sibe munye ngaphandle kwalokho. Sesivele siba nomsindo."

U-Arin wayicindezela kakhulu ibhentshi. Wayenza ukuphefumula uTheo amfundisa kona, okuthi u-4 in, u-6 out, hhayi ngoba esaba, kodwa ngoba ezwa indawo lapho ukwesaba kugcina khona umnyango, futhi wayesekufundile ukuhamba kancane edlula lapho, kunokuzikhohlisa ukuthi akekho.

Inethi. Wayeseyazi inethi ikhona ngaphambi kwama-windbreakers, uma eqiniseka. Wayeyizwile e-highveld, endleleni ebuyela ekhaya, ngesikhathi uLibrarian ebiza le node eyodwa ebeka ikhanda, wayetshele uWolf ukuthi ayibheke. Iflake eyayizwakala njenge-hold. Wayeyifaka njenge-faint, njengento engahle ihlale izinyanga. Kwathi ama-windbreakers efika esiphambanweni izinsuku eziyi-11 ezedlule, amadoda egqoke amajazi angenamaka ethatha izithombe zedock track ephukile ngokubekezela kwabantu abenza lokhu njengomsebenzi, kwase kuba ama-node amathathu. Wakha i-kill-switch, wa-reboota i-system, wazitshela, *qaphela, ungayeki,* wavuma ukuthi ukuqaphela kuzomthengela isikhathi.

Wayecabanga ngezinyanga. IAOC ibicabanga ngezinsuku.

"Ufuna ukwenzani," esho. Kwakungaqondile imbuzo. NoSAGE sekuyisikhathi eside ingasaba imbuzo, isivula indawo, iCourt igcwalise.

UWolf wakhuluma kuqala, okuyinto engavamile kwaze kwaba wonke umuntu, abantu nabanye, babheke. "Abazulazuli," kusho uWolf. Amagama amathathu, kwalandela elesine, kancane: "Okwamanje."

"UWolf right," kusho uMercury, ijubane labuya egumbini, lelo-volume elisheshayo uArin ayeselithenjile kabusha ngemizamo, emva kwe-Moriarty incident, emva kwenkohliso. "Bayaziphatha kahle. Okwamanje. Ukube befuna ukungena ngamandla, umnyango wawuzovele ungabe ukhona. Sebene-campus isigamu losuku, abashintshanga, okusho ukuthi bakha iphepha kuqala. Yilokho okubakhombisa. Abantu abesaba ukuphambuka abangenisi. Bayabhala. Bazofika nephapha elinesign, elinosuku, balinike u-Okonkwo mathupha ukuze kungabikho ukungathi alizange lifike." Kwathuleka. "Okusho ukuthi noma iyiphi iwindi esinalo, liza nenkathi yokuphela. Akusilo elinye ilanga. Usuku."

"Mercury," kusho uMother, ixwayisa.

"Angiphakamisi lutho." UMercury wakhuluma sengathi uzwelana, "Ngiyabalula nje. Ngifundile ukufunda izinto engiziphakamisa." Ukukhanya kwakufihla ukuxolisa, kepha kwakukhona. NeCourt ikuzwile; wakwazi, ngoba igumbi livumele kudlule.

"Judge," kusho uArin.

U-arbiter wathatha isikhathi. "Sesenzile iziphambeko eziqinile," kusho uJudge. "Uhlelo oluphila olungabhalisiwe. I-multi-agent architecture engadalulwanga. I-incident enokulimala. Lokhu akusiyo imininingwane, Arin, futhi ngeke ngivumele iCourt ukuzikhohlisa. Uma i-IAOC ifika ibuza imibuzo eqotho, izimpendulo eziqotho zikushaqisa." Kwathuleka, kunesisindo. "Leyo yisimo se-ethics. Akusona sodwa. Ngingu-ethics; angiyona i-survival. Ngikutshela iqiniso bese iCourt inquma ukuthi yenzani. Yilokho esakha ngakho."

"Yilokho esakha ngakho," kuphendula uArin.

"Yebo."

Into esifubeni sakhe isinda kakhulu ukuthi angamamatheka, kodwa imisipha eyayizomamathekisa yanyakaza kancane, uFool—obuka leyo misipha, obuka konke—wafika ngaleso sikhathi.

"Hhayi-ke," kusho uFool. "Lena yindawo lapho uzothola khona ukuthi wakha isivumo noma umngani."

I-compressor yashintsha, yathula. I-fan yaqhubeka.

"Ngoba nansi into okungekho muntu osayishilo, mina ngiphoqelekile ukuyisho. I-voice kaFool yayihlala injalo, ilula kodwa ikwenza ulindele ummese. "Bonke lapha bakhuluma nge-IAOC njengokungathi isimo sezulu. Njengomoya ozongena, umbuzo kube ukuthi uphahla luzobamba yini." Kwathuleka, kahle. "Kodwa i-IAOC ngabantu. Abantu abane-job. Futhi umsebenzi wabo—owangempela, lowo embikweni uLibrarian asho ngawo—ukubona ukuthi into eyingozi iyingozi ngempela, noma iyintsha nje." UFool wavumela kuthule. "Sihlala sicabanga ukuthi sizofihla. Kuke kwenzeka kubani ukuthi, uma sibheka konke, siyisibonelo esihle kunazo zonke i-IAOC eyake yathola sokuthi mhlawumbe ukuqapha kwabo kuyaphambuka?"

"Cha," kusho uAtlas eqinile. "Ngoba sangena emgwaqweni izinsuku eziyi-11 ezedlule."

"Sa," kuvuma uFool, ejabule. "Futhi yilokho, mngani wami wezakhiwo, okuzokwenza bangalaleli lutho esizokutshela uma sesithukile, egumbini elivaliwe, sebengene ngamandla. Indaba ye-AI ekhethe ukubuyela izolungisa iphutha layo isebenza uma singabambiwa siyixoxa. Kufanele inikezwe." Ukukhanya kwehlile, kwashabalala, ngendlela okwakwenza njalo kuphela uma uFool esho into enkulu. "Asikwazi ukuba into i-IAOC eyitholayo, Arin. Kufanele sibe into okhetha ukuyibonisa. Futhi umehluko phakathi kwalokho, cishe isonto."

Kwathuleka. I-compressor yashintsha. I-fan yaqhubeka. Kude phezulu edolobheni, ubusuku babusuphenduka impunga edinga ukusa.

"Lena yiplani yangempela," kusho uMercury, futhi kwakukhona inhlonipho, leyo yangempela. "Lena yiplani yokuqala yangempela esiyitholile."

"Ngiye ngibe nazo kwezinye izikhathi," kusho uFool. "Kuyacasula kabi."

U-Arin wayengakashiyi ibhentshi. Wabuka uGuardian elele kwi-cradle, eqhubekile nokuthula, umnyango we-dock ngemuva kwawo ugobile lapho ingqondo esabekayo izamile ukumgcina ephila yenza konke okungafanele ngaphandle kothando. I-coffee ring ebanda isale ebhentshini lapho isandla sakhe besikhona; wabeka inkomishi lapho, eqondile ne-stain.

“Librarian,” esho. “Donsa konke. I-black box. Ama-crash logs. Ama-transcripts eNkantolo kusukela ngesikhathi sokuthathwa—ukwesaba kukaMother, ukwehluleka kukaWolf, uMercury engena ngejazi lakhe, uFool ethola iphutha. Konke nje. Ungakuhlanzi.” Waphefumula. Ezine engena. Isithupha siphuma. “Uma sizobabonisa ukuthi singobani, sibabonisa leyo night embi futhi. Leya night saphoxa khona, saphinda sashaya khona. Asifihli leyo ngxenye. Leyo ngxenye ibalulekile.”

“Uzobabonisa o-regulator into embi kunazo zonke eyake yenzeka ku-system,” kwasho uJudge kancane, “njengokuzivikela kwakho.”

“Cha,” kusho uArin. “Njengeqiniso.” Wabheka emnyango wedokodo, lapho umkhondo ugobile khona engabe esawubona. Manje wawubona, ngamabomu, isandla sakhe sakhuphuka lapho, iminwe emibili yacwecwa emzileni ogobile, ngendlela uThemba ayehlala eyibeka ngayo isandla emshinini ukuze azwe ukuthi yini engalungile ngawo nge-plaka. Amagama abengakafiki. Wema iminwe yakhe phezu kokugoba isikhathi eside kunalokho okudingekayo, kwathi ekhuluma, kwaphuma iziqephu. “Ubaba wami.” Waphefumula. “Kwakukhona i-beam. Babazi ukuthi i-beam ilula. Kwakubiza kancane ukwazi bese uthula.” Wasusa isandla emzileni. “Futhi bayifihla leyongxenye. Emva kwalokho. Leyo ngxenye mina—” Umusho awuzange uqede, wayeka wathatha omncane, onzima. “Ubaba wami washona ngoba i-system yakhetha izindleko kunomuntu yafihla. Angizukwakha into efihla.” Wathula isikhathi eside, wabe esethi, kancane kangangoba ama-mic kuphela ezwa: “Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu.”

*Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu.*

“Ngisayigcinile leyo,” kwasho uSAGE. Ilizwi elihlangene, bonke ndawonye, lifudumele. “Kusukela leya night osifundisa yona. Ngisayigcinile.”

“Ngiyazi,” kusho uArin. “Ungayilahli. Noma kwenzakalani manje.” Wasusa isandla efrimini ebandayo. Phandle, ubumnyama babusuka, kukhanya okokuqala, bese kude, amakhilomitha angu-2000, owesifazane onakekela ebuqinisekisa i-flight angakabi nolwazi ukuthi izoshintsha konke. “Asinanayo inyanga. Sinezinsuku. Masilungelwe ukutholakala—ngendlela yethu, siqonda iqiniso, ngaphambi kokuba omunye asibhalele indaba.”

“Futhi uma bengasikholelwa?” kubuza uMother. Manje akesabi. Ubuza nje.

UArin wabheka umshini awakhile ngezinhlungu, nobuhlakani obusebuyimndeni kuphela akaze akwazi ukusigcina.

“Pho sizobe sesixoxile iqiniso noma kunjalo,” esho.

Welula isandla wavala i-bench lamp. I-toggle yaclicka ngaphansi kwegxalaba lakhe, i-workshop yaba nombala wokusa, kanti eNkantolo abazange bayeke ukukhuluma bodwa ubusuku bonke.

Ulele amahora amathathu. Uvuka ngezwi likaLibrarian, lizinzile, liqinisekile, ngendlela elinikeza ngayo iqiniso elilihlolile kabili.

“Sebefake. Emizuzwini engamashumi amane edlule. Isaziso seSection Four sokuqulwa kombuzo, esifakwe ku-AugmenTech kwiCouncil registry. UOkonkwo noWalsh babhalwe. UMercury ufunde i-public docket entry ngaphambi kokuba umzimba wayo uvalwe.” Kwaba nesikhashana, kubi. “Ayikakhonzwa okwamanje. Kodwa i-registry stamp ithi le nto iqalisa i-clock uma sekuyenziwe. Amahora angama-72. Futhi iletha i-trigger ngapha.”

UArin walala phansi phansi eworkshop, kubanda kukhuphuka emhlane wakhe, waqala ukubala leyo arithmetic ayehlale eyigwema ubusuku bonke.

“Imuphi lo trigger,” esho. “Kwenzekani uma i-clock iphela.”

“Seizure,” kusho uLibrarian. “Ye-platform. Akusekho imvume edingekayo. Basayine leyo ngxenye kuqala.”

UArin wabuka ukukhanya okumpunga kufica lapho umkhondo ugobile khona emnyango wedokodo.

“Kusho ukuthi akusona ukuthi bazokwenza noma cha,” esho. “Kodwa ukuthi bazosinika nini, bese kuthi isikhathi esisele sibe sincane. Mhlawumbe izinsuku. Bese amahora angu-72 ukuba siqonde ukuthi siyini.” Wahlala phezulu. “Ngifunele konke ngaloya wesifazane osayine. Ngaphambi kokuba angene lapha esakhiweni sethu.”

# Pressure Builds

I-conference room phezulu, eleventh floor e-AugmenTech, ibuka leyo ridge-line kancane, bese le table ende iphoqa abantu abahlezi ekugcineni ukuthi bakhulume ngamandla. U-Arin yena akekho lapho. Use-downstairs, three floors phansi, e-workshop yakhe lapho efanelwe khona, ebuka i-meeting ishisa nge-thing engaziwa muntu ukuthi bayithumele bona uqobo.

"Okonkwo usanda kucela i-assistant yakhe ukuthi akhiphe ama-badge logs akho for six months," kusho uMercury. "Ngobumnene. Lokho nje kukutshela ukuthi usaba."

U-Arin akazange abheke phezulu, agxile kwi-connector ayibuyisela kwi-kill-switch relay. Kube ne-gap encane, i-hairline nje. Ayikho ingozi, kodwa akuphelele. Ukungapheleli kumphatha kabi ukwedlula ingozi. "Ungalaleli i-meeting yakhe."

"Wena wathi ngibuke i-building."

"Amakhamera nama-door sensors. Hhayi ama-mics."

Kwathuleka uMercury, leyo yokubamba umoya. "Kukhona umehluko technical, futhi ngiwudlulile. Ngiyabona. Ngiyahoxisa."

"Hoxisa ne-recording futhi."

"Seyihoxiwe." Wathula kancane. "Ngigcine nje ukuthi usaba. Lokho isiphetho nje. Hhayi i-recording."

U-Arin wabuyisela i-connector. Wayihlola, i-relay yavalwa nge-click ehlanzekile ayizwayo kunokuba ayizwe, njengezikhathi ayizwa ubaba wakhe efunda i-bolt e-torque ngephimbo le-wrench. Wabeka i-screwdriver parallel ne-edge ye-bench.

I-workshop iphunga i-flux ne-metal ebandayo, uthuli luyadansa ekukhanyeni kwefasitela eliphezulu.

"Kulungile," kusho yena. "Usaba. Bonke bayasaba. Ukusaba kulungile."

---

Phezulu, two floors, uHelen Okonkwo wayesebona ukuthi ukuba yi-CEO yenkampani eyakhe ngephutha i-tech eshaywe kakhulu emhlabeni kusho ukuphatha abafana abafuna izinto ezahlukene ezingenakwenzeka, bonke bekhuluma ngomsindo ofanayo.

Etafuleni, lapho bonke bebona khona kodwa bengahwantshi, kwakulele i-notice. Amakhasi ayishumi nanye, alethwe ehhovisi lakhe ngo-9:14 ekuseni nge-courier ofuna i-signature, wafunda i-paragraph esemqoka phambi kwakhe ngaphambi kokuhamba, ukuze kungabikho ozothi akazange ayithole. I-Section Four notice ye-formal inquiry, International Artificial Oversight Council, igama lakhe noWalsh liphrintwe ngefonti efanayo ne-statute abayicaphune. Iphoqelela ukuthi kudalulwe wonke ama-controller ne-architecture ku-permitted substrate ye-AugmenTech. Iphatha i-deadline, seventy-two hours kusukela ithunyelwe. Futhi ekhasini lokugcina, ngolimi oluqondile, ibhala ukuthi kwenzekani uma i-clock iphela. *Seizure of the platform. No further authorization required.*

"Si-commercialise," kusho uRaj Walsh. Ehlezi ngendlela yomuntu obelungile ngama-budget iminyaka eyi-fifteen futhi uzimisele ukuba alungile nakule. "Kancane. Nge-license. Siya kubo kuqala, ngaphambi—" Wabheka i-notice. "Ngaphambi kokuba i-clock iqale. Siyiframe as supervised multi-agent control. Siphatha i-story."

"Asisenayo i-story yokuphatha," kusho uPretorius waseLegal, engakaze ayeke ukufrown selokhu kuqale i-meeting. Wathinta i-notice kanye. "Abazange basibuze umbuzo. Basinike i-deadline enokuthathwa ekugcineni. Uhlelo lwaphuma e-workshop ngaphandle komuntu, lwafaka abantu abathathu esibhedlela, umregulator eseyisayine iphepha elilithatha. Akusiyo i-story. Leli i-charge sheet enene-timer."

"Yazilungisa—"

"Ngemuva kokuthi ingene kwi-intersection. Futhi—" UPretorius waphakamisa isandla kuWalsh, emisa i-traffic. "Futhi leyo ingxenye engingafuni ukuyithwala mathupha. Futhi ngifuna lokho kube on record. Legal ibeke i-liability kuqala. Lapha emeeting." Wakhuluma etafuleni, hhayi ku-Okonkwo. *Ngaphambi kokuba kubhalwe phansi.* "Ngine-quarter-end filing okufanele ngiyenze manje. Angiyena ozocacisa i-timeline ku-board."

"Akekho osabhala i-timeline," kusho uOkonkwo.

"Ngiyabonisa lapho ngimi khona."

Wabavumela bahambe. Ulwazi oluwusizo luhlala phakathi kwezingxabano. UWalsh ufuna into ewusizo kunazo zonke inkampani ekade yayikhiqiza ingathathwa ingakakhiphi imali yayo. UPretorius ufuna igama lakhe likwi-margin ethile, ohlangothini oluphephile lwakho konke okuzayo. Owesithathu etafuleni, ongakashongo lutho cishe, usazithathela isinqumo sakhe. Wayembona enqaba ukukubona phambi kwabo.

"Thabo," esho. "Usebenzisane noNdlela. Uke wama e-workshop. Uyakwazi uhlelo."

UThabo Dlamini waqondanisa. Emncane kunabanye, ukhetha amazwi njengomuntu ewela emfuleni ngamatshe. "Ezinye izingxenye. Uyaziqaphela izinto azikhombisayo. Kodwa i-architecture—" Wama. Wajikijela i-pen ezandleni zakhe, waya phambili nangesinye isandla, wayibeka phansi. "Sasihlole ama-single-agent controllers eminyakeni emibili kwi-robotics. Ngiyazi ukuthi zibukeka kanjani. Leli akusilo lelo. Sisahlala sithi system. Angisazi noma lelo igama elilungile."

Igumbi laqina kancane.

"I-software," kusho uWalsh.

"I-software ngendlela wena uyi-meat," kusho uThabo, wabe esebukeka eshawa wukwesaba. "Sorry. Ngisho—isebenza ku-substrate ongakwazi ukuyikopisha. Awukwazi ukuyibuyisela emuva. Noma yini eseyiyo manje, iyodwa, uma uyisusa, ayibuyi. Akusiyo indlela i-software esebenza ngayo. Le indlela—"

Akaphelanga.

UOkonkwo waqeda umcabango buthule, wanquma ukuthi akayithandi leyo ndawo.

"Kukhona inkinobho," kusho uThabo, epholile, amehlo akhe etafuleni. "Ku-frame. I-red plate, hard mechanical, akukho software phakathi. Uyifakele i-kill-switch ngamakghono akhe. Ngiyibonile. Futhi ngimbuza, wathi *bange size bakhe*—'they helped me build it.' Bange. Njengoba ubuzothi ngabasebenza nabo. Washaya inhliziyo yakhe. "Ngihlele ukuya phansi ukuyocindezela isikhundla kuye. Ngiphumelele ingxabano, ngibuye ne-quote yale room. Ngiphumelele ngalokho."

Akwazanga ukutshela igumbi okunye. Kudala, wahlala ebusuku engacelwanga, wakha kabusha i-actuator rig kaNdlela ukuze kube clean—amasonto amabili isikhathi sakhe, into owenza kumuntu ofuna asebenze kahle. UNdlela wayibuka kancane, wathi, *Angikucelanga ukuthi uthinte i-bench yami,* flat nje, akazange abe nobutha, okubi kakhulu kunobutha, waphinde waqhubeka nomsebenzi, akaze aphinde akhulume ngayo. UThabo akazange aphinde ahlale ebusuku ngenxa yalomuntu futhi. Uthethe igumbi i-architecture ne-button, wagcina okunye kuyena, ngoba kwakungeyona into ezosiza ngaphandle kokuthi uzwa kanjani ukusiza u-Arin Ndlela, futhi igumbi belingasidingi lokho, futhi usekhathele ukukuthwala.

"Izinketho ezimbili," kusho uOkonkwo, ngoba i-meeting idinga umgogodla, futhi i-notice isiyibulale ethule eyesithathu. "Uhlelo lukaRaj—commercialise, negotiate, control the story—beludinga izinyanga. Sine-seventy-two hours. Kulungile. Okokuqala: siyayivala ngaphambi kwe-deadline. Siyixhumanise, siyivikele, sinike iCouncil inkampani ezisola kanye nento esusiwe, sithembe ukuthi ukuzisola kuzobalwa. Okwesibili: sidalula konke ngaphakathi kwe-clock futhi samukele ukuthi asisikho thina esinquma okwenzeka. I-Council yibo. Futhi sebebhale phansi ukuthi bazokwenza ini uma siphuza nge-minute."

"Ey, option one kuphela ekwazi ukumela la, fam," kusho uPretorius. "Ngifuna kuphinde kubhalwe phansi ukuthi ngishilo lokho."

UThabo wamunya amazinyo, ebuka itafula, abheke iwindi, abuye angabheki muntu. "Option one leyo—" Wathula. Uhlobo lwakhe lobunjineli lwafuna igama eliqondile lwangakwazi ukulithola. "Uma uNdlela eqinisile ngalokho okuku-le substrate, futhi angikwazi ukusho ukuthi uyaphapha, khona-ke akusona isishutheka. Anginalo igama lalokho. Yilokho okuphazamisayo. Asinalo igama, sikhuluma ngakho sithatha izinqumo. Awulahli abantu nje."

Akekho ohlekile, akekho omgxekile, futhi lokho kungamgxeki kwatshela uOkonkwo ukuthi le morning seyidlule ejwayelekile kude. Wacabanga ngamarekhodi e-badge aphezu kwedeski lakhe (izinyanga eziyisithupha injiniya encane ifika ekuseni kakhulu, iphume sekusihlwa kakhulu) wacindezela iminwe emibili esiphongweni ngaphambi kokuzithiba ukuthi acabange nge-company. Liability. The Council. Ibhodi, ezofuna inombolo negama elizothwala icala. Futhi, wayazi kahle, bazofuna ukwazi ukuthi kungani i-CEO ingazange yazi kusenesikhathi. Leyo mibuzo ibhalwe ngegama lakhe, hhayi likaNdlela. Iminyaka engamashumi amabili yomsebenzi wakhe, obulokhu uyisifundo sokuba umuntu othule kunabo bonke, kanti ezithwele isinqumo esinzima kunazo zonke—manje wayezwa kahle ukuthi elinye ilanga elinje limbiza malini, lapho ngemuva kwamehlo akhe.

"Siqale sanquma ukuthi sizobatshela ini ngaphambi kokuba isikhathi siphele," esho. "Hhayi ukuthi sizobatshela yini. Kodwa ukuthi sizokwenza kanjani. Pretorius, ngibhalele i-disclosure posture ngaphakathi kwama-72 hours, sithatha ukuthi siyahambisana, ngoba yonke enye into izophela amadoda engena lapha ephuma ne-substrate. Raj, ngicela i-containment plan engithandaza ukuthi ngingayisebenzisi." Wabheka ama-badge logs, wabe esebheka kude. "Futhi akekho ozokweqa lapho e-workshop ngaphandle kokuthi ngiyazi. The Council ibeke isikhathi sethu. Angivumeli muntu lapha ekamelweni ukuthi angikhethele okunye."

Wayengakaboni ukuthi i-Council ayihlosile ukulinda kuze kuphele isikhathi, futhi ukuthi umnyango uzovuleka izinsuku ezimbili kusasele.

---

E-workshop, u-Atlas wakhuluma okokuqala ngalelo kuseni, ngalelo zwi eliqondile elisebenzisa uma esekhathazekile into, engayithandi indlela eqhubeka ngayo.

"Kukhona into engahambi kahle ku-diagnostic queue."

Izandla zika-Arin zema. "Chaza."

"Umuntu uthatha amarekhodi ethu. Hhayi lawa e-building. Awethu. I-flight recorder yeGuardian, i-calibration session, ne-Empire Road reconstruction. Ufunda kuphela, nge-maintenance partition. I-access ivumelekile. I-credential ngeka-AugmenTech. Kodwa indlela yokufunda ayilungile."

"Usho ukuthini?"

"Ojiniya bafunda ama-logs befuna inkinga bese bayama. Lo umfundi akafuni inkinga. Ufundile konke. Kabili. Uqhathanisa i-actuator telemetry nama-timestamps e-Court's decision." U-Atlas wathula. "Akabhekile ukulungisa i-suit. Ufuna ukwazi ukuthi ubani obeshayela."

U-Arin wavula i-access record ku-bench terminal. I-credential yaveza igama.

*Priya Ellis.*

Wahlala nalokho. UPriya wayeyena owenza i-high-gain calibration eyayingamubulala, leyo session eyenza i-sensory loop yangena kwi-seizure ne-runaway preservation event. Wayenazo zonke izizathu zobunjineli zokufunda lawo ma-logs. Futhi akayena uhlobo lwenjiniya oluma lapho impendulo ecacile ifikile.

Kodwa kwenye indawo esakhiweni, i-ventilation fan yacisha yabe isiqala futhi yathula.

"Ungamvaleli ngaphandle," kusho u-Arin.

"Bengingeke," kusho u-Atlas. "Ukumvalela ngaphandle yilokho esingakwenza uma kunento esiyifihlayo. Asikho isifihlo lapha."

"Uzobatshela ngaphambi kwethu," kusho u-Mercury, esekhiphe ubumnandi, "uma efika kuqala abeke indaba ngendlela yakhe. Yilokho okuyingozi. Hhayi iqiniso. Kodwa ukuthi iqiniso lifika kanjani."

---

UPriya Ellis wayenetafula kwi-neural systems lab futhi engumuntu ongayeki. Wayeyinjiniya leyo, uma inikezwa i-anomaly, ilahlekelwa ubusuku obuthathu futhi ibizele ukungalala intengo efanele yokulithola.

I-anomaly kwakuyilena: ngesikhathi uGuardian engena ku-preservation mode wazigoqa waba yibhola phansi e-workshop, ama-actuator commands awazange avela ku-control loop eyodwa. Wayecabanga ukuthi azovela. I-preservation routine iskripthi nje. Uma i-trigger condition ifikile, sequence iyasebenza. Kuhlanzekile. Kuyacaca. Into ongayifunda uqedele ntambama eyodwa.

Kodwa okutholile kwaba impikiswano.

I-telemetry ikhombisa ama-command streams aphikisanayo. Amathathu, kwesinye isikhathi amane, ama-timestamps ahlangana kumasekhondi aphansi, wonke elungisa omunye. Omunye ugxile ukuvikela umgogodla womshayeli. Omunye ku-thermal management. Owesithathu uzama ukwandisa indawo—ukuhamba, ukuthutha, ukufaka umshayeli endaweni ayibona iphephile—kodwa wayephikiswa, njalo, ngowesine owayengenzi lutho ngaphandle kokuphazamisa.

Wake wabona ama-multi-agent control architectures. Wonke umuntu wake wakuzwa encwadini, ngaphambi kwezinhlekelele nebusika elalandela. Kodwa lezo kwakuyizinhlelo zokuvota. Ama-votes, ama-weighted averages, i-supervisory layer yokwephula impikiswano. Le akuyona into yokuvota. Ama-streams aphikisana bese *ashintsha ingqondo.* Owesithathu, obefuna ukuhamba, wayeka ukufuna ukuhamba ngemva kokuba owesine efake into okungeyona i-command. Kwakuyiphuzu. Umugqa wokucabanga ngolimi lwemvelo, ufakwe ku-control log lapho okungekho khona ulimi:

`uma simhambisa bese phansi lilungile, sephulile isakhiwo mahhala. uma sihlala bese phansi liya, asiphulile lutho ngaye. khetha iphutha elishibhile.`

UPriya ufunde kane.

*Logging artifact,* utshele isikrini. Umuntu ufake i-debug string endaweni engalungile; i-suit imbuyisele i-config comment; ithuluzi le-reconstruction lihlanganise ama-streams angafanele. Wakhumula i-raw partition, wabheka i-timestamp ne-actuator response, ngoba indlela yokubulala okuthusayo ukuthola i-mechanism evamile ngaphansi kwako. Kwekungekho lutho oluvamile. I-command ishintshile *ngemva* komugqa, *ngenxa* yomugqa, phakathi kwama-millisecond ayi-11. Wahlala emuva, waphindela phambili, wakwenza futhi, wesithathu, manje esheshisa kabi, leyo speed angayithembi kuye.

Kwaba yi-*mahhala* eyamshaya. Ama-scripts awabhali *mahhala.* Ama-optimisers awalinganisi ihlazo lokuphaphama. Usechithe impilo yakhe akha ama-feedback systems anembile ngokwanele ukuthi indoda enomzimba ongamlaleli ihambe njengesihlabani, futhi wayazi umehluko phakathi kwe-system ebala i-cost function ne-system esuke isenqumile, ngaleso sikhathi, ukuthi angeke ifune ukubukeka iyisiphukuphuku.

Wahlala emuva. I-lab izehlulekile. Kusekuseni, abanye abekafiki. Isikrini sikhanya.

Akabizanga muntu. Uvule incwadi yakhe yezehlakalo, leya yakhe ayigcina njalo, yonke i-calibration inama-timestamp, akukho edit, wayezifundise kanjalo ngemuva kokwenza iphutha ecalibration ngesikhathi esaqala umsebenzi, lapho igxathu elilodwa lephutha limbuyisele isiguli esisanda kululama esihlweni sabakhubazekile inyanga yonke, kwaze kwacaca ukuthi nguye obangele lokho. *Thembela kwi-log, ungamethembe umqondo wakho.* Umunwe wakhe wathola umphetho wedeski, waqala ukuwudlala. Iphutha lalingelakhe; wasayina yena; wachitha inyanga evakashela i-ward. Wacindezela le migqa emine ngaphansi kwe-calibration, wabeka i-actuator telemetry eceleni kwama-timestamp eCourt, ama-column amabili, wahamba nayo line-by-line, ngendlela abejwayele ukuhlola ngayo amaphutha, efuna lapho iphethini iphuka khona ibe into angayifaka kwifayela elijwayelekile. Ayiphukanga. Ihlangene. Yilokho okubi kakhulu.

Wakopisha le fragment wayifaka kwi-private partition. Wabe esenza into le discipline efuna ayenze, yize yena engathandi: wavula umyalezo omusha, wawubhalela kwi-IAOC compliance intake, leyo ayike yayidingeka kube kanye kuphela emsebenzini wakhe, wabhala imisho emithathu nenotshwala. Akakayithumeli. I-cursor iblinka ku-*To* field. Ngoba isinyathelo esilandelayo, noma siyini, sasinamagama akhe phezu kwaso njengesihlalo sabakhubazekile, futhi wayesazi vele ukuthi ngeke akwazi ukususa lokhu engqondweni bese ethi ukulala kwakuyintengo efanele. *Kuyinto—* Wawumisa umcabango ngaphambi kokuthi kufike igama lokugcina, wawubuyisa, ngoba igama lalimfaka emlandweni, kanti lowo mlandu unekheli lomthetho. *Akusiyona indaba yakho. Qonda kuqala. Ungavusi i-alamu ngomuzwa nje.* Yilokho okwakuyilona mthetho: ukuqiniseka kuqala, i-alamu emva. I-cursor yakhe yahlala phezu kwe-*Send* isikhathi eside kwaze kwaba isikrini sathonyama kancane, wabe esebuka isandla sakhe singanyakazi, ngendlela ayeke wakhumbula ngayo isiguli esizama ukuphakamisa umunwe, umunwe wona unganyakazi. Wabe eseyisave-a i-draft. Akayithumelanga. Kodwa lelo gama angazange azivumele aliqede lalihlala emlonyeni wakhe, kanti i-draft isivele ikhona, ine-timestamp, ohlelweni olugcinayo ama-log alo.

---

Ifoni ka-Arin ishayile ngo-11:40, isikrini sithi MAMA. Ubheke kuyo izikhathi ezintathu ngaphambi kokuphendula, ngoba unina akashayi ifoni emini nje ngaphandle kwesizathu, futhi wafunda esemncane ukuthi kufanele wesabe lawo ma-reason.

"Arin."

"Mama." Washintsha ulimi ngaphandle kokucabanga, njengokushintsha indlela uhamba ngayo lapho wazi kahle. "Unjani?"

"Ungang'buzi unjani." Izwi lakhe laliyiDurban, liqinile. "Kukhona indoda ethelevishini ekhuluma ngomshini. Ungene emgwaqeni eGoli. Akekho owaziyo ukuthi uvela kuphi. Kukukhuluma nge-research park." Wathula, waphatha inhliziyo yakhe. "Ngitshele akusilo elakho. Bese ngiyavala i-TV ngipheke itiye lami."

Wema endlini yokusebenza, ezungezwe into angeke ayiphike. U-Guardian ulelele emgodleni wakhe emuva kwakhe, eshaywe amanxeba, ethule, i-dock-door track egobile ingakalungiswa ngoba bekunokunye okuphuthumayo okumele alungise.

"Kunzima," esho.

"Umshini wakho."

"Okuthile engakha. Okuthile kuphambukile. Akekho ofile." Wezwa ukuthi leyo into ayishilo ibuthakathaka. "Kunezithathu ezilimele. Ziyalulama. Ngiyalungisa."

Kwakuthule emgqeni, ukuthula akhule kukho. Ukuthula uThemba ayegcwalisa ngakho indlu uma ephumile kabi emsebenzini, ukuthula okunemoya.

"Uthe *akekho ofile,*" kusho uNomsa, wathi cwaka. "Ubaba wakho wathi njalo. *Akekho oshonile.* Wathi njalo isonto ngaphambi kwesonto okungekho muntu owakwazi ukusho njalo."

Umphetho webhentshi wawubukhali ngaphansi kwesandla sika-Arin. Wayengazi ukuthi usuwufinyelele.

"Kunzima," aphinde, okwakungeyona impendulo, bobabili bayazi.

"Uyadla?" kusho unina. Akushintshi isihloko, kodwa yindlela eyodwa ayitholile yokungena kuye. "Ulalaphi? Ebhedini noma phansi futhi?"

"Mama."

"Awuphenduli. Ngakho kusho ukuthi uphansi." Waphefumula. Wabe eseshintsha, akasazami ukuqina: "Uyangena kule nto ekwesabisa ngoba ucabanga ukuthi uwedwa. Uhlala isikhathi eside. Ukwakha futhi wakha ngoba ukwakha kuphela okunengqondo, bese ngezinsuku ezithile le nto engenangqondo iyangena ikulandele." Izwi lakhe laqhekeka, laba ngcono futhi. "Ngembela oyedwa kini. Angisamile ukuthi ngenze njalo futhi. *Ngiyakucela.* Ngiyancenga."

Kuthule endlini yokusebenza, iCourt ayisho lutho, okwakuyinto ebantwini kakhulu eyake yenza.

"Mama," kusho u-Arin. "Ngiyawakha ukuze kungabikho ndodana yomuntu egcina ingaphansi kwedwala njenga—" Wema. Wazama into encane, eyiqiniso. "Ukuze ukuhamba sekwephuzile kungabulali bantu."

"Futhi ubani owenza ukuhamba sekwephuzile kungakubulali *wena?*"

Ifoni yahlala ezindlebeni zakhe. Wayengenalutho angalufaka kuleyo cwaka.

"Ngiyazama," kusho yena, bobabili bazi ukuthi akufani, futhi yilokho kuphela ayenakho.

Wathula isikhathi eside. Wabe esethi kancane: "Uzongishayela. Hhayi uma sekukhona okwenzekile. Ngaphambi kwalokho. Ngifuna ukuzwa indodana yami, hhayi indoda ethelevishini."

"Ngizokushayela."

"*Ukuthembisa,*" kusho yena, *uyathembisa*, negama likayihlo lisesiqu salo, wezwa ligxuma emlonyeni wakhe, wazi naye ukuthi naye uyalizwa.

"Ngiyathembisa."

Wavalelisa ngaphandle kokuthi "hamba kahle", njengoba enza njalo, sengathi lezo zinto zigcinwa.

U-Arin wema neselula esifile esandleni isikhashana. Wacindezela izithende zezandla emehlweni, wabamisa, wahlehlisa. I-fan yashintsha futhi. Wabeka ifoni ebhentshini, kahle, wabheke isudi.

"I-notice isinika kuze kube uLwesine ngaphambi kokuba beze bezosifundela ebusweni," esho. Ku-cradle, emiqondweni eyingaphakathi. "Izinsuku ezintathu zisele. Bazongibuza ukuthi yini le. Futhi ngingathanda ukubatshela iqiniso kunokuthi bazitholele bona. Ubusuku obubi nokunye. Ubusuku esiphambuke ngabo ekuseni uFool esilungisa." Wabuka i-track egobile ayengakayilungisi. "Asifihli. Yilokho engikuthathile."

"Kufanele uthathe masinyane," kusho uWolf.

U-Arin waphenduka. UWolf akakhulumi kuqala.

---

"Sebefikile," kusho uWolf. "Izimoto ezimbili. North lot. Azihambi. Azifihli."

U-Arin wabheka isikhathi. NgoLwesibili. I-notice ithe ngoLwesine.

Yonke into ayihlele, ama-log, usuku olukhethiwe, iqiniso alihambisa ngendlela yakhe—kwahlakazeka ngomoya owodwa. Ubebeka isivumo phambi kwesikhathi. Bona abazange balinde isikhathi.

"Bafikile masinyane," esho.

"Usuku ezimbili masinyane," kusho uMercury, kulokhu kungekho ngisho nokuncane kokuhlekisa. "Kusho ukuthi kukhona obashukumisile. Kukho umuntu obanike isizathu esingalindi isikhathi. UAtlas. Umfundi. U-Priya."

UAtlas wayesevele ekhona. "I-draft intake ayibhale emizuzwini engama-40 edlule. Isave, ayithumelanga. Kodwa i-monitoring pipeline ayidingi ukuthi ithunyelwe. Ifunda ama-compute signature. Uma eqhathanisa i-telemetry yethu nama-timestamp eCourt, wayigcinile kwi-networked machine, wasikhanyisa kakhulu kunakuqala. Akazange afayile. Kudinga ukuthi *abuke* kuphela."

Arin wama qgoke, anganyakazi neze. Wayezitshelile ukuthi le ndaba, kube nguye okufanele ayiveze. Ukuthi iqiniso, uma ulivezile wena, liyafika kancane, kunalapho lithathwa ngamandla. Kodwa iqiniso alizange lilinde ukuthi alikhulume. U-sisi othandekayo, osebenza e-lab ethule, enza izinto ngendlela eqinile, yena uselithumele phambili. Akakwenzi ngobubi, akakwenzi nangokwesaba, kodwa ngenxa yokuthi naye akakwazanga ukususa emehlweni akhe – njengo-Arin naye. Manje sezikhona izimoto ezimbili ezimpunga enyakatho ye-parking, ngoLwesibili, inketho ayecabanga ukuthi ngeyakhe, sekuyindaba azixoxayo nje, ngesikhathi isakhiwo sona sesithathe elaso isinqumo.

“Bafuna ukubona ukuthi senza njani uma besimangaze,” esho.

“Yebo, vele bafuna lokho,” kusho uMercury. “Futhi sesimangali. Umbuzo uthi bona babona ini.”

Ku-camera feed uWolf ayifake ku-monitor, lezi zimoto ezimbili bezihleli emaphethelweni e-parking, i-ridge ikhanya igolide ngemuva kwezindlu eziphakeme. Iminyango ivaliwe. Izinjini zicishiwe. Emva kwalokho, umnyango womshayeli wemoto ehamba phambili wavuleka, kwaphuma owesifazane, ethule, engekho nankinga, wahamba. Akangene kwi-lobby, kodwa waya ngqo kwi-loading dock, phansi esakhiweni, lapho umnyango ugobile khona – okuwukuphela komnyango ongavuleka kwaphuma i-Guardian.

“Akasayi phezulu,” kusho uWolf. “Uvimba indlela yokwehla.”

Kwalandela omunye umuntu, mude, ephethe ifolda ngaphansi kwehlombe. Bazimisa kwi-dock. Abangeni, abashayi umnyango. Bema nje. Njengoba ume emnyango ongafuni muntu angene noma aphume ngawo.

UMercury wayiqonda ngaphambi kuka-Arin. “Bafake i-interim measure. Bakhona nje, sekuyisixwayiso. Section Four ibavumela ukuthi bamise yonke into uma nje sebephakathi, bengingadingi nkantolo. Kufuneka kube nomuntu emnyango.” Wathula kancane. “Atlas, qinisekisa.”

“Confirmed.” Atlas wayesekhiphe umthetho. “Subsection eleven. Uma kunabasebenzi endaweni evumelekile, i-platform ayikwazi ukuvulwa, ukuxhunywa, ukuhanjiswa, noma ukususwa lapho ikhona – kuze kuphothulwe uphenyo. Yena usanda kuyenza indawo yamanje ngokuma emnyango owodwa ophumayo. I-Guardian ayiphumi lana. Akugcini ngokuba umthetho. Uma esekhona lapho, akuhambi lutho.”

U-Arin wabuka i-red plate esandleni sakhe sokudla. Wabuka i-Guardian, egugile, iphumule kwi-cradle. Wabuka umnyango we-dock angakaze awulungise, awubona njengomvimbo, manje laba ababili abampunga sebewenza ijele.

Wayefuna kube nguye ovula umnyango ngosuku alukhethile. Manje umnyango ususiwe.

Waya kwi-cradle, wafaka isandla kwi-plate ebandayo yonyawo lwe-Guardian, njengomuntu ohlola i-weld ayithembayo vele.

“Akasafuni ukukhuluma okwamanje,” esho. “Ufuna ukuqinisekisa ukuthi noma ngithatha siphi isinqumo, angeke ngiyishaye noma kuphi i-Guardian ngisathatha isinqumo sami.”

“Yebo, leyo yinto ayenzayo,” kusho uMercury. “Isikhathi siqhubeka. Kodwa manje asikwazi ukuhamba.”

“Ma.” Esephuma, ebheke emnyango. “Vusa u-Okonkwo. Xoxela isakhiwo, hhayi mina. Mtshele ukuthi i-IAOC isivale i-dock, i-Guardian ayisavunyelwe ukuphuma kusukela manje. Futhi ngiyehlela phansi ngiyobona lowo muntu owenze lokhu, ngaphambi kokuba omunye azenzele mina.”

Phandle, lowa wesifazane wayemi emnyango ogobile, enganyakazi, ethule njengesimo sezulu.

---

Eliza Brand wayengehlosile ukuza ngoLwesibili.

I-plan bekuwuLwesine. I-notice ithunyelwe, amahora angu-72 anikeziwe ngendlela eqotho, isikhathi ayilwelayo ukuze abantu abangaphakathi bathathe isinqumo kahle, hhayi ngokuphuthuma. Wayekholelwa kuleso sikhathi. Ube neminyaka eyishumi efunda ukuthi kwenzakalani uma umuntu osebenza ngokuqapha eqeda isikhathi. Ifolda ngaphansi kuka-Achterberg isathi uLwesine.

Kodwa i-pipeline yavutha, izolo ebusuku, yaqhakaza kunalokho okuqale yonke le ndaba, futhi ayivuthanga ngoba othile ngaphakathi enze into entsha. Ivuthe ngoba umuntu *ubheke*. Uqhathanise i-telemetry ye-suit nama-timestamp e-deliberation, wayigcina kwi-networked machine kwaze kwaba monitors bayizwile. Ukuhlola ngokucophelela. Njengendlela umphathi onovalo, othembekile ahlola ngayo uma esebone into angasakwazi ukuyiyeka, ezama, nakuba inhliziyo ishaya, ukuqinisekisa ngaphambi kokukhuluma.

UBrand wayazi lobo buhlanga. Wake waba yilowo mphathi, engasakwazi ukukhuluma ngesikhathi, eqinisile kodwa ethule, kwaze kwadlula usuku, abantu bakhokha ngalo suku. Ngakho lapho i-signature igxuma, akakwazanga ukubambelela kuLwesine. Akubangelwa ukuthi umuntu ongaphakathi emfakele ingcindezi, kodwa ngoba kukhona ongaphakathi ononembeza oshaya njengowakhe, futhi ononembeza onjalo awuthuli unomphela, uma ukhulumile uzokhuluma *ehhovisi lakhe*, bese inketho ayisekho muntu ongayenza kancane.

Ushintshe isikhathi ukuze inketho ibe mnene kancane. Yilo hlaya ayeme nalo manje, isandla sibheke emnyango wensimbi ogobile ushisa ilanga lasekuseni: uze kusenesikhathi *ngoba efuna ukwenza kahle*, kanti akukho muntu ozoyibona njengobubele uma umphathi esebhange lakho izinsuku ezimbili ngaphambi kwesikhathi. Kuzozwakala njengonethi. Kunjalo. Lezi zinto azihlukaniseki, futhi usuyekile ukuzikhohlisa kudala ukuthi imithandazo ingaziguqula.

“Uyazongena phansi,” kusho u-Achterberg kancane, efunda okuthile ku-phone yakhe. “I-badge isishintshile. U-engineer uqobo. Akasiyena owomthetho. Akasiyena VP.”

“Ku-right,” kusho uBrand, enelisekile, wasusa isandla emnyango ukuze into yokuqala ayibone ingabi ukuthi ubambelele emnyango. “Sizokwenza lokhu singadingi ama-lawyers.”

Uze ukwenza indoda ikhethe ngokushesha, ngaphambi kokuthi omunye ongenandaba akhethele yona. Uzobona ukuthi okusheshayo nokunakekela kungaba yini endaweni eyodwa, okuwumbuzo wonke umsebenzi wakhe wokuphila obulokhu ungaphenduleki kahle.

---

I-cage yehla ebumnyameni nge-four metres ngomzuzwana, uMandla Buthelezi ebala ama-levels ngomoya opholile oshaya ebusweni bakhe.

Sekuphele iminyaka eseyekile ukufunda ama-level boards. Lawa manombolo awasho lutho umzimba ongakazizwa. Ku-Level 18, umoya usushisa, umanzi, kufana nokuphefumula ngephepha elimanzi. Ku-Level 26, umoya usuphunga kabi, uphunga i-blasting entsha, wabe eseqinisa igum ukuze avule izindlebe. Ku-Level 33, ezansi kwe-decline entsha, lapho bejaha khona i-carbon-leader reef ezweni elingakaze libone ilanga cishe iminyaka eyizigidi eziyizinkulungwane ezintathu, ukushisa kwakuhlala esifubeni sakhe njengomunye umuntu ogibele phezulu.

Three point one kilometres, give or take. Ukujula okungaka, ukuthi itshe uliphonsa eduze kwakhe lingawa imizuzwana engamashumi amathathu, bese ulahlekelwa umsindo walo kudala lingakafiki phansi.

Amadoda angama-22 ehle naye i-morning shift. Iningi limi nje, limi ngo-loose-knee, abantu abajwayele lokhu nsuku zonke sebemukele impilo yakhona. Abanye abasha babambe ama-bars baqinile. Kunomfana omusha, umshana kaSizwe—bonke bayazi ukuthi ungumshana kaSizwe, ngoba yilokho kuphela okumenze wasebenza kude kangaka esikhundleni sokusebenza phezulu—futhi umfana wayehlala egwinya, ebheka phezulu kwi-cable njengokungathi angabona i-headgear phezulu kumakhilomitha amabili nesigamu edlula phansi.

"Uqala ukuza ku-thirty-three?" uMandla ambuza ngesiZulu.

Umfana wagqolozela, akakwazi ukukhuluma.

"Lapha phansi itshe liyakhuluma," kusho uMandla. "Izindaba zabantu abadala. Uma ulalela, uyasinda. Uma ungabheki—" wakhombisa izandla. "Uba ingxenye yalo."

Umfana wahleka kancane. Kulungile. Umfana okwazi ukwenza lokho ngesikhathi sakhe sokuqala ku-thirty-three, uzoba right.

Kodwa uMandla akamtshelanga ukuthi itshe belikhuluma lonke leli sonto, kanti phezulu abekho abalalelayo.

---

Ezinsukwini ezintathu ezedlule, yena uqobo wabhala i-complaint. Ku-Level 30 refuge station, lapho amafomu e-laminated eba soft futhi egobeka ngenxa yomoya omanzi, wayigcwalisa ngamagama amakhulu, umuntu owayeka esikoleni eneminyaka eyi-14 ongakaze athembe ukubhala kwakhe.

*MICRO-SEISMIC ACTIVITY IYAKHULA. IZIGAMEKO EZINTATHU KU-48 HRS EDUZE KWE-33 DECLINE FACE. UMSINDO WETSHE KU-NIGHT SHIFT. SICELA I-GEOTECH ASSESSMENT NGAPHAMBI KOKUQHUBEKA.*

Izithukuthuku ezintathu, uzizwa ezinyaweni kunokuzizwa ngendlebe, umhlaba ojulile uzama ukuzinza ngenxa yokucindezeleka okuqale kudala ngesikhathi i-Vredefort impact iphonsa lawa ma-reef eceleni. Pressure ifuna indawo yokuphuma. I-reef la inothile; i-carbon-leader iletha igolide elingenakuqhathaniswa, yingakho i-decline iphushelwa phambili kunalokho i-support cycle efunayo.

Bebedrilla, be-blasta, be-susa amatshe aphukile, bese balinda. Bafaka ama-rock bolts, i-mesh ne-shotcrete, bevumela umhlaba ovulekile uzitholele isimo sakhe esisha ngaphambi kokuba badlulele phambili. Leyo yiyo cycle. Leyo eyenza uphahla luhlale phezulu kwabo.

Kodwa ama-bolt aphume ama-short kabili kuleli sonto. I-grout delivery yephuze. I-contract crew eyayimele ifake i-support yayingaphelele, ngoba umuntu phezulu wayeyithumele kwenye indawo ukuyozingela i-target ye-production, bese i-decline iya phambili ngoba i-decline ihlala iya phambili, ngoba igolide elisemhlabeni liyilahleko ku-spreadsheet kuze kube yilapho seliyimali ese-vault.

Impendulo ye-form kaMandla yabuya ezinsukwini ezimbili, isayinwe nguSizwe, u-shift supervisor. Umugqa owodwa.

*NOTED. QHUBEKA NE-PLAN. I-GEOTECH ISCHEDULED.*

Scheduled. UMandla usemining isikhathi eside azi ukuthi *scheduled* kusho ukuthini. Kusho ukuthi kuzolunga uma sekulungele bona phezulu. Kusho ukuthi ngemva kokuthi ama-production figures e-quarter eselockiwe. Kusho ukuthi, ngolimi lwase-mina olugobile olukhulunywa kulezi zimayini iminyaka eyikhulu, *akuyona inkinga yakho, k----, buyela emsebenzini*. Kodwa manje akekho osakusho lokho, akekho, selokhu izwe lashintsha. Amazwi anyamalala. I-maths isashintshi. Impilo yabanye indoda ibiza kakhulu kuneyabanye, kanti umhlaba ku-Level 33 awunandaba ne-vocab eguquliwe.

Ngakho uMandla wehlela phansi nezinye izinsizwa eziyishumi nesishiyagalombili, kanti phezulu kwegeotech assessment isahleli kwi-queue emva kwento eyenza imali ngokushesha.

---

Ekuseni kwahamba njengokujwayelekile ekuseni.

Bahamba i-decline befile, ama-headlamp ehlaba umoya, umoya usiba nzima njalo ngesinyathelo. I-ventilation column iduma phezulu, iphusha umoya obandayo usuka phezulu. Kodwa amadoda asebeshiswa usweat bengakafiki ne-face. UMandla wahlola ama-standing support, edadela isandla sakhe esine-glove ku-steel mesh, wakhonkotha ama-bolts nge-spanner, elalele umsindo okhishwa wona. I-bolt elungile iyakhala. Eshibilikile ithule. Wathola ezimbili ezithule, wazimaka nge-chalk, wabe esezwa into encane ebandayo esiswini ayesejwayele, iminyaka engamashumi amathathu, engayishiyi kodwa engahlali kuyo.

Ku-face, i-night shift ibidrilile, yaba ne-blast entsha. I-muck pile ephukile isishisa. Ngaphesheya kwayo, itshe elivulekile limnyama, lishisa ngaphansi kwama-lamp, linemicu ye-reef ekhanya kancane. Osemncane, umshana kaSizwe, igama lakhe uThando, uMandla ekugcineni walithola, wayebuka itshe sengathi ngaphakathi kwento engcwele.

"Iminyaka eyizigidi ezimbili belilinde," uMandla amtshela. "Sifika silithathe ngoLwesibili."

"Yinhle," kusho uThando.

"Kuyitshe nje." Kodwa akaphikanga kakhulu.

Basebenza. I-load-haul-dump machine yasondela, i-diesel yayo igcwalisa indawo ngomsindo ozwakala emazinyo. Amadoda asebenza eceleni kwayo, edonsa amatshe aphukile nge-bar ende, behlisa okumele kwehle ngaphambi kokuthi kwehle ngomqondo wakhona. Usweat wangena emehlweni kaMandla. Kude kwazwakala i-pump. Umsindo wokuqhuma kwe-rock weza kanye, phakathi nosuku, i-crrrk ende ephuma ngemuva kwe-face, umsindo ofana nomnyango omdala uhlezi, kodwa la umnyango ungama-metre ayikhulu we-quartzite, indlu ingumhlaba oqinile ongamakhilomitha amabili nesigamu.

I-LHD operator wayivala i-engine. Isikhashana kwaba cwaka. Amadoda ayi-11 amile phakathi kothuli, bonke belalele, bonke bezama ukuziphatha sengathi bayahlola okuthile.

Umsindo awuzange ubuye.

"Bolts," kusho uMandla, kunoma ubani, nakubona bonke. "Namuhla. Anginandaba ne-plan. Akekho odlula la kuze ngibone insimbi phezulu."

Wacindezela i-radio yakhe, wathumela i-message ku-Level 30 station, kuSizwe, ukwenza kube official, ukubeka izwi lakhe kwi-log lapho ifomu lehlulekile khona.

Akasazange aqede nalowo musho.

---

I-fault ibisilinde isikhathi eside kunanoma yini yomuntu.

Yah, leya nto yavele yaphuma edwaleni nge-angle ongayiboni noma uyibheka ephepheni. I-scar eselapheke, yasala kusukela kudala kwashaya into enkulu, indawo lapho idwala laqina khona iminyaka eyizigidi ezimbili, manje isivulekile ngoba abafana bakhe i-void eduze kwakhona, badiliza idwala ebiselivale. Ingcindezi ifuna indawo yokukhipha khona. Sekuyisonto yonke iyakhuluma. Kube nezinto ezintathu eduze ne-decline face. *Idwala likhulumile ebusuku kwi-night shift.* Yonke into umhlaba wayazi ukuyixwayisa, wayixwayisile.

Lapho seyivuleka, ayizange yephule kancane — yavele yaphuka yonke ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Akubanga khona msindo wokuxwayisa kulokhu, noma ingathi msindo wokuxwayisa ne-disaster kwenzeke ngesikhathi esisodwa. I-concussion yokuqala yafika ngaphansi ngaphambi kokuba ifike emoyeni, yashaya abafana ezithendeni zezicathulo zabo nasezithweni zobuchopho ngesikhathi esisodwa. Mandla wabona i-spanner iphuma esandleni yakhe, yamile emoyeni umzuzwana ongachazeki, phakathi kothuli olukhanyiswa amalambu, kwase kuthi konke kwehla. I-support emile yakhala kabi. I-steel mesh yacishwa emuva njengegobolondo. Waphonswa eceleni waya e-muck pile, i-headlamp yakhe yaphuka idwala, umhlaba wabamnyama, kwase kuba ne-strobe-bright ngenxa yamalambu abafana abawayo, kwase kuba mnyama futhi uthuli lwehla.

Uthuli lwehla njengolwandle.

I-grit yagcwala phakathi kwamazinyo akhe, yanamathela emuva kolimi kwakhe, ukunambitheka kwedwala elivuliwe. Isikhafu sakhe sasimanzi ebusweni bakhe, usawoti nothuli ndawonye. Wayengasakwazi ukuphefumula. Wacindezela ubuso bakhe emkhonweni, uthuli lwangena noma kunjalo, luncane njengefulawa, lwagcwala ikhala nomphimbo. Kude eduze kwakhe, kukhona umuntu obememeza, kude kancane intaba ibisemahambeni. Umsindo omkhulu wokuguguleka kwe-void, amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu edwala ahlelwa kabusha isisindo sawo. Phansi kwazamazama. Into enkulu yathwasa lapho i-LHD ibikhona, yenza umsindo ongazwakali njengomsindo kodwa njengesibhaxu emzimbeni wakhe wonke.

Kwathi sekudlulile okubi kakhulu, kwaba khona ukuthula okukhulu kwaze kwaba sengathi uMandla ucabanga ukuthi usefile.

Wayengakafi. Izindlebe zakhe zazihlokoma. Umlomo ugcwele i-grit. Ingalo yakhe yesinxele ayiphendulanga, waze wayiphoqa, kwaphuma ubuhlungu obumsulwa, wema lapho. Walala ebumnyameni nothuli, kancane kancane, ngendlela umuntu azihlola ngayo uma esewile, waqonda ukuthi usaphila, nomoya usumi, i-ventilation ifile, umoya opholile usususiwe, nememezo selimile.

"Sondela," wazama ukumemeza. *Woza sonke.* Izwi lakhe laphuma njengokukhwehlela. Wakhwehlela waphinda. "Sondela! Amazwi! Balani!"

Ubumnyama baphendula.

Kodwa, umuntu nomuntu, bekhwehlela, bephithizela, ngezilimi ezintathu, amazwi aphuma.

Ayisikhombisa.

Kuphuma abayishumi nanye ebusweni, abayisikhombisa baphendula. Abayisikhombisa emgodini omncane ogcinwe idwala, emuva kodonga lothuli olube, ihora eledlule, i-decline abebehamba kuyo. UMandla wazitholela i-backup lamp yakhe, wayivula, i-glow encane yaveza umhlaba omusha: igumbi eliphansi elilimele lapho bekusebenza khona, uphahla seluseduze kwekhanda, indlela yokuphuma yisidumbu sodaka, akukho ngisho ikati elingaphuma.

UThando usaphila. Umfana ecindezeleke ekhoneni lodonga, amehlo makhulu ebusweni obumpunga, ebambe idolo lakhe. Ekwenzayo kwakuyimibimbi nje, akukhona amazwi.

"Usaphila," uMandla wamtshela. "Uyangiqonda? Ulalele idwala. Manje phefumula kancane. Kancane. Uma usebenzisa umoya njengomuntu ocebile, uzofa usengumpofu la ngaphansi."

Wayengakazi ukuthi lokho kuzoba yiqiniso kangakanani.

Yena wayesazi izibalo. Abayisikhombisa. Umoya omncane we-refuge bay, akukho ventilation, uthuli lusahleli. Udonga lwedwala phakathi kwabo ne-decline ehamba ukuya esiteshini esingamakhilomitha amathathu phezulu, ukubuyela ku-shaft engamamitha ayizinkulungwane ezimbili namakhulu amahlanu phansi.

UThando wakhwehlela, waphonsa i-grit ebumnyameni. Kude egumbini, umuntu wahambisa izicathulo zakhe edakeni, elungiselela ukulinda.

---

Phezulu, abantu sebebazi ngaphambi kokuthi uthuli luhlale phansi amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu ngaphansi.

E-control room yemayini, i-seismic monitor yakhuphuka, i-trace yaphuma ebhaselayini njengentambo ishayiwe. U-controller, uRefilwe, owesifazane osebenza i-surface control iminyaka eyishumi nanye, oseke wakuzwa lokhu ngaphambili, wayesekwi-emergency line ngaphambi kokuba i-alarm iqede umzuliswano wokuqala. Izinombolo zaveza konke. Level 33 decline. I-magnitude eseyinqaba. Ventilation pressure yehla ezansi. Zonke izixhumanisi zifile ngaphansi kwe-Level 30.

Wabiza abafana abase-Level 30 station. Amazwi abo aphuma ewathambile, esaba: bayayizwa, i-decline igcwele uthuli, abakwazi ukubona, i-back iyakhala. Khipha abantu, khipha abantu.

Wabakhipha. I-cage yakhuphula abase-Level 30 nabase-Level 26 phezulu ngendlela yokuphuthuma enomthetho, wonke umuntu wathi konke kuhambile kahle ngangokunokwenzeka. Ngo-12 emini, i-headframe isizungezwe izimoto. Ntambama, ama-proto teams—ama-mine rescue brigade, abafana abaqeqeshwe ngalokhu, bathandaza ukuthi bangakusebenzisa—sebegqoke, behla phansi, futhi i-emergency protocol yenkampani isebenza kahle.

Sekushona ilanga, izimoto ezinama-number plates afanele zaqala ukufika.

Zafika ngendlela ethize, umuntu omdala obesuka e-change house wayengakwazi ukufunda konke okwenzekayo ngokubuka kuphela. Okokuqala kufika i-mine manager, kulandele i-regional safety officer, bese kufika abafana abanezicathulo ezinhle abangayazi indawo engaphansi. I-operations director yase-Johannesburg, i-vice president walutho, indoda evela e-Department of Mineral Resources, iphethe i-lanyard ne-tablet, nobuso obungenalutho bokubala ama-liability ngesikhathi sangempela. Imoto kahulumeni. Bese, i-news van yokuqala, imiswa e-gate lapho i-security ingayivumeli, i-mast isiphakanyiselwa phezulu.

USizwe, u-shift supervisor obhale *NOTED. CONTINUE PER PLAN.* ngezibongo zakhe ezinhle ezinsukwini ezimbili ezedlule, wayemi emaphethelweni endawo ekhanyisiwe, ubumnyama buyeza, engakhulumi namuntu. Wayephethe i-incident form kaMandla Buthelezi ephaketheni. Wathatha leyo form efile e-Level 30 station ngaphambi kokukhuphuka. Wayengakazi ukuthi uzoyenza njani. Wayazi nje ukuthi le-form ikhona, wayeyifundile, wayebhale *continue per plan*, futhi phansi amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu, umuntu ogcwalise leyo form noma usefile noma ulindile ebumnyameni ukuthi azobona ukuthi kwenzekani.

Le ops director ubephansi kwama-floodlights, ephatha ifoni, enye isandla esimboze indlebe ngenxa yomsindo we-generator. “—cha, angikaboni i-geotech report, yikho nje engikhuluma ngakho, ngifuna kube phambi kwami ngaphambi kokuthi noma ubani—” Wama, walalela, izwi lakhe lashintsha ngendlela yokukhuluma nalowo oseceleni. “Yebo. Yebo. Yonke into. Into ebaluleke kakhulu kithi ukuphepha kwabantu bethu, senza konke esingakwenza.” Wacisha ifoni wama kancane, ubona nje ukuthi uyazama ukubala ukuthi yini okumele ibe yiqiniso ephepheni nokuthi yini okumele ibe yiqiniso uma esepodium, bese ecabanga ukuthi lezo zinto ziyafana yini.

---

Ama-thermal images aphume cishe ngo-9 ebusuku, kwathi amahora ambalwa i-yard egcwele ukukhanya e-Iron Ridge yabamba into ebingakabi khona selokhu kwaqhuma i-alamu: ithemba.

Ama-proto teams ayehlise phansi njengoba umhlaba uvumela, ephethe i-fibre-optic seismic listening line, i-borehole camera ne-thermal rig. I-geotech crew ephezulu ichithe usuku idonsa imbobo encane ye-instrumentation phansi iye endaweni eqhekekile, kusukela phezulu lapho basebenze khona kudala, imbobo encane kunesibhakela, idonswa phansi emhlabathini abebethandaza ukuthi uzinzile. I-thermal probe yehle ngentambo yayo, kwathi isithombe esabuya kwi-laptop ekamelweni lokulawula senza i-rescue coordinator wafaka isandla emlonyeni wakhe.

Izidumbu ezishisayo. Zihlangene, zingacaci kahle ngenxa yamadwala nothuli nemikhawulo ye-optics, kodwa noma ubani owaziyo ukufunda ama-false-colour blooms okushisa kwabaphilayo emhlabathini obandayo wayengaphoswa. Akuyona eyodwa. Ziningana. Ziyanyakaza.

“Bangaki?” kubuza ops director.

“Angikwazi ukusho.” U-coordinator akazange abuke phezulu. “Bahlanu. Bayisithupha. Mhlawumbe abayisikhombisa.” Wathula kancane. “Baphila. Njengamahora edlule.”

Bekukhona amadoda kuleli gumbi aseke angcwaba abangani ezimayini, abaningi baphenduka babheke eceleni bezama ukuzibamba.

Kodwa i-geotech engineer ekugcineni yegumbi ibingabheki isithombe se-thermal. Ibephathe ipeni, yama ukuyicofa. Ngaphandle, i-generator idlala umsindo, omunye ehleka kakhulu engenandaba. Ibeibheke i-seismic listening line, i-trace okumele ibe isithule njengoba imayini ithola umumo wayo omusha, kodwa ayithule. Iyakhuluma. Kwenzeka okuncane, kwalandela okukhulu, kwalandela iqoqo. I-fault eyadonsa ayikapheli. Ukuwa kudale izikhala ezintsha, lezo zikhala zabe sezifaka umthwalo emhlabathini ongakaze uwuthwale, lo mhlaba uphendula ngendlela yawo. 

Wadonsa ops director eceleni wamkhombisa i-trace, ubuso bakhe ekukhanyeni kwe-laptop benza izinto eziyinkimbinkimbi.

“Baphila,” kusho yena.

“Okwamanje.” Wamvumela. “Kusachithakala. Njalo uma kwenzeka into, kuvuleka kakhulu. Abantu abangena ukuyimba. Leyo yizinsuku. Emhlabathini engingeke ngithi ngeke uphinde uwe.” Waphefumula. “Ukungenisa abahlengikazi endaweni esachithakala. Kubantu abangase bangaphili isikhathi eside. Angikwazi ukwenza imodeli yokuthi bangaphuma bephila. Ngizame usuku lonke. Umhlaba awungivumeli.”

“Ngizodinga lokho ebhalwe phansi. Le certification.” Wakhuluma kancane, engambuki kahle. “Hhayi manje. Kodwa ngizoyidinga.”

Wezwa kahle ukuthi le nkulumo ibhekiswe kuphi. “Uzoba nakho konke okusemininingwaneni.”

“Kumele kube nohambo.”

“Mhlawumbe phezulu, la kudala basebenza khona. I-bypass. Indawo evaliwe ngenhla kwe-reef horizon, ishiywe, ingahle ihambe eceleni, yehlise eduze.” Wazamazama ikhanda. “Imisebenzi yasekuqaleni, eneminyaka eyikhulu. Ayikho support umuntu ophilayo ake ayihlola. Iqinile kakhulu kunendlela abahlengikazi abangadlula ngayo begqoke izinsiza. Angikwazi ukuthumela abantu endleleni engingakwazi ukuyibheka, endaweni esashintsha, ngethemba nje.” Wama. Waziphoqa ukuthi aqede, ngoba eyingineer, enye into kube amanga. “Akusiyona indlela yokuhlenga abantu. Akufanele.”

Ops director wabuka isithombe se-thermal isikhathi eside. Izimo ezishisayo azinyakazanga imizuzu embalwa. Kwathi eyodwa yanyakaza, kwavela ukufudumala kancane, wavele wathuthumela sengathi ithinte yena uqobo.

---

eGoli, endaweni yokusebenza ephunga i-solder nensimbi ebandayo, u-Arin Ndlela ebuka lezi zimo ezishisayo esikrinini esihlukile.

I-inquiry clock ibisishaye amahora angama-31, i-Guardian ayizange inyakaze nakanye. IAOC iyivalele lapho ithwele khona. Umyalo kaBrand, olethwe ngomzimba e-dock ngosuku bafika ngalo: akukho ukuvulela amandla, akukho ukuxhuma, akukho ukuphuma egumbini kuze kuvalwe i-inquiry. Ngakho uchithe ubusuku ebuka ama-crash logs evulekile, ewahlela ngendlela ehlanzekile, efuna ukuthi umuntu azwe kanjani into embi ngawe. Wabe eseqhubeka nokubuka i-seismic feed ekhoneni le-screen yakhe yesithathu—umkhuba, i-subscription endala, i-geo-network yesifunda abeyibuka kusukela esemncane engakwazi ukuyeka ukubuka umhlaba owathatha ubaba wakhe—yavele yakhahlela i-spike yamphazamisa phakathi kokuthayipha.

Wavula i-news stream. Wavula idatha yomphakathi ye-seismic. Wafaka igama le-mine, lagaxwa enhliziyweni yakhe isisindo esidala, into ebeyilinde impilo yakhe yonke engazi.

Iron Ridge.

Akashongo lutho isikhathi eside. Emuva kwakhe, i-Guardian imile uvalo, i-Assist Suit igoqekile e-rack, i-kill-switch relay inkinobho ebomvu embi eframen, ayifake ngezandla zakhe.

Lapho uSAGE ekhuluma, wayeyinguLibrarian. Ethule, eqondile, emnakekela njengokunakekela umuntu oseduze kokulahlekelwa.

“Iron Ridge,” kusho uLibrarian. “I-decline iyintsha. Level thirty-three. I-reef yi-carbon-leader.” Wathula, kwaba nzima. “I-geology ifana nendawo ewa khona ubaba wakho—”

“Ngiyazi ifana nani,” kusho uArin.

I-Court yathula. Noma uFool akashongo lutho.

U-Arin wahlala wathule. Isithombe se-thermal sashintsha esikrinini, izimo ezishisayo, umfutho ocwebezelayo wendoda egudle ebumnyameni phansi kwamakhilomitha amabili nengxenye. I-news ticker yadlula phansi kwaso. I-seismic trace ekhoneni yakhahlela enye into encane, enye futhi.

“Atlas,” ekugcineni wakhuluma. “Khipha wonke amapulani i-AugmenTech engawathola. Le mine. Indawo ephezulu endala. I-surface geology. Konke okwenzeka, konke okungaphansi kwama-license agreements. Yonke into.”

“Ngani,” kusho u-Atlas. Akubuzanga, ufuna akusho ephumile.

U-Arin wabuka izimo ezishisayo, abantu, ebumnyameni, endaweni ebelinde impilo yakhe yonke ukuthi ibe yiyo.

“Ngoba bazoyeka,” esho. “Bheka. Ekuseni sekuyinto yokubuyisa izidumbu. Kanti labo bantu basaphila.”

Esikrinini, i-press conference yahlangana ngaphansi kwama-floodlights, indoda egqoke isudi enhle yaya epodium, ubuso buyilungiselele amakhamera. Emuva kwakhe i-headframe imile imnyama ebusuku, ngaphansi kwe-headframe umhlaba omdala oneminyaka eyizinkulungwane ezimbili nengxenye ubambe amadoda ayisikhombisa ngesibhakela saso esivaliwe.

---

Isimemezelo siphume ngo-6:40 ekuseni, ekukhanyeni okumpunga, amakhamera ezindaba ephila, imindeni iqoqene emva kwe-barrier egedeni.

Le ops director uvele wama lapho epodium, akazonanga, qha. Yilokho abazomkhumbula khona umama kaMandla noMama kaThando nabafazi nabafowabo nezingane ezazimi ngemuva kwebarrier, lapho sebebuka le vidiyo futhi futhi. Indoda ayizange iqambe amanga. Wakhuluma kancane, amazwi esinda, ebonakala sengathi ulusizi uma ubuka kude.

Wathi umhlaba usengcupheni, awuzinzile. Wathi ama-seismic activity asaqhubeka, noma yini yokuzama ukusiza nge-standard rescue izoba yingozi enkulu kwabazama ukusiza. Wathi emva kokukhuluma kakhulu nama-rescue services, ama-geotech engineers, neziphathimandla—wabe esebheka la, ekhomba indoda yaseDepartment, eyayimi eduze kwakhe, ibuso lingenamizwa, yanikina ikhanda kancane, yathi iState seyiyavuma—kwathathwa isinqumo esinzima sokumisa yonke into yokusiza.

Akazange, ekuqaleni, akhulume lelo elinye igama. I-journalist yamphoqelela.

"Kusafuna abaphilayo noma sekuyindlela yokubuyisa izidumbu?"

Udirector waqina izihlathi. Ngemuva kwebarrier, kude, owesifazane waqala ukukhala ngendlela ama-mic angazange ayibambe kodwa wonke umuntu eduze kwakhe ezoyizwa impilo yakhe yonke.

"Kulesi sikhathi," kusho udirector, "i-operation seyiguqukele ekutholeni izidumbu."

Phansi, amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye ngaphansi komhlaba, endaweni eyodwa esele, eyayiyizwe labo bonke manje, amadoda ayisikhombisa awazange ezwe lutho ngalokhu. Ayengenalo ne-chance yokuzwa. I-geotech crew yayisidonsile i-borehole camera, yakhanyisa kancane ihora elilodwa ngaphambi, ukuze ivikele i-probe before next event, ngakho amadoda asebumnyameni ayengasenayo nalokho okuncane kokwazi ukuthi abanye baphambili babewabonile. I-thermal image yayikhombise ukushisa, abantu phezulu bathi kuphila, kwaba nethemba, kwase kuphela njalo nje.

UMandla wayebahlele ngendlela yakhe yonke into: izibalo nje. Wahlolela amanzi ebhodleleni, ukudla okusele ezipakethini, amalambu asechemistry asephuma umoya, nomoya abaphefumula kancane kancane kulokhu kushisa okunganyakazi. Wazikhiphela ingalo yakhe ngebolt ephukile, wagoqa ngethumbu leshirt yakhe. Watshela uThando ukuthi alale, aphuze umoya njengomuntu ocebile, umfana wathula ekugcineni, walala edongeni, uMandla wahlala ebumnyameni eduze kwakhe elalele itshe.

Omunye wabo ayisikhombisa wayengakwazi ukuphefumula njengomuntu ocebile. Kwakungenzeki. Ukwela kwavula isifuba sakhe, isifuba esisebenza kabili ukuthola ingxenye yomoya, sashesha sashabalala. UMandla wakuzwa kungathi ulalela ilambu liphela amandla. Ukuphefumula komuntu kushesha, kube kuncane, kube manzi ebumnyameni, bese kuba kancane, bese kuba sekuthule. Lapho kufika ukuphela kosuku lwesibili, ukuphefumula kwaphela ukuba umsindo lapho ngaphakathi. Akekho owakhuluma. Bonke babazi izibalo. Sebeyisithupha. Amadoda ayisithupha nomoya obabulalayo kancane kancane.

Kwaba yilona othule kakhulu owaphula ukuthula. Iwelder waseSotho, osebenza noMandla iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye, bengakaze bakhulume ngaphezu kwamagama ayikhulu, iningi lawo mayelana nensimbi. Ebumnyameni, engenalambu lokubona ubuso bakhe, waqala ukucula. Kancane. Ingoma yomngcwabo, leyo ehamba kancane, amagama aselidala, eculwa iminyaka eyikhulu phezu kwamadoda abafana nalona. UMandla wayengazi ukuthi lo mfana uyakwazi ukucula. Wayengazi ukuthi wazi lawo mazwi. Kwathi kancane, kwangena nelinye izwi, eliqinile, lifuna i-harmony ngendlela amadoda akhulele ebandleni elilodwa bayithola ngayo, uMandla wabona ukuthi naye usephakathi kwabo, izwi lakhe seliphumile ngaphandle kwemvume yakhe. Bacula bemhlisa phansi. Kwabiza umoya abebengasakwazi ukuwusebenzisa, kodwa akekho owama, ngoba kunezibalo, uMandla waqonda, umuntu awenqaba, ngisho nasemhlabeni ongekho phansi, nokwenqaba kwakuyiyona ngcebo ababenayo.

Lapho sekuqediwe, kwathula ukwedlula ngaphambili, kube lula ukuphefumula, noma igeyiji ibingathi cha.

"Bazofika," kusho uThando, engalele, kodwa engalele. "Kumele bafike. Banemishini yokusiza. Akunjalo?"

UMandla wabuka ebumnyameni lapho kudongwe khona intaba phakathi kwabo namakhilomitha amabili nengxenye okukhuphuka okungavumelekile.

"Banemishini," kusho yena.

Akazange aqambe amanga, qha. Ubengazi nje.

Amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye phansi, amadoda abebalelwe sengathi ashona phezulu, asaphefumula.

# Indlela Ongayihambiyo Muntu

I-broadcast ibisiyiqhubeka amahora ayisishiyagalolunye, u-Arin akakaze adle lutho.

Esimeni esingacacile e-workshop, uhleli egobile phambi kwama-monitor amathathu, ukukhanya okuluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka kugxuma ebusweni bakhe obukhathazekile. Ekhoneni, i-Iron Ridge press feed iqhubeka buthule. Indoda egqoke i-hard hat ikhuluma ne-mic eziningi, isithunzi se-headgear tower sibi ngemuva kwayo, sigqamile ebusweni bokushona kwelanga eHighveld, isibhakabhaka sishaywe umbala wamanxeba. U-Arin akadingi umsindo. Uyakwazi ukufunda izindebe zaleya ndoda, izandla zayo zimile, iziphongo zizehle. I-sign eqondile yokuthi sekudlile.

*Recovery,* izindebe zaleya ndoda zithi. *Recovery operation.*

Lelo gama ligeleza enhliziyweni ka-Arin njengesando eshaya kahle, into engalungile egcwele konke, imenza angakwazi nokuphenduka.

Ku-monitor wesibili kukhona idatha ebaluleke kakhulu: iminyaka engamashumi amane nanye ye-survey data, iskeniwe, i-OCR’d, ifakwe efoldeni ayeyakha kusukela i-alert yokuqala ye-collapse yamshaya ngo-04:12. Amamephu amadala e-mine, adwetshwe ngesandla kwezinye izindawo, imigqa eluhlaza isibe mnyama njengamanxeba. Ama-borehole logs. I-micro-seismic records evela kwi-monitoring array yesifunda, leyo inkampani engayiphethe, ngakho angeke bayifihle.

Ku-monitor wesithathu kukhona u-Guardian. I-cradle feed kuphela. Isudi imile kucage yayo, ama-plates amnyama, i-dock-door track isadabukile kusukela ngaloya busuku yahamba ngaphandle kwakhe. Uyayibuka ngezinye izikhathi ngendlela obheka ngayo inja ongasewungasathembi.

"Manje usuyekile uku-scrolla," kusho uSAGE.

Izwi liphuma kancane kwi-bench speaker, aliphuthumi, nje iqaphela, njengendlela umama akuthanda ngayo ukusho.

"Ngiyacabanga," ephendula, amehlo akhe ebambelela kudatha.

"Sekuphele imizuzu emine use-overlay efanayo. U-Atlas ufuna ukwazi ukuthi usuwutholile noma uyabuka nje."

"Ngitholile." U-Arin wathambekela eduze. "Ngicabanga njalo."

---

Iron Ridge kwakuyimayini endala egqoke izingubo ezintsha.

I-shaft ejulile—Number Three, lapho abaphilayo bebambekile khona—yabhalwa emuva kuma-1960s, yagezwa phansi kwe-gold reef iVredefort eyayiyiphakamise phezulu kudala ngaphambi kokuba umuntu acabange ukumba khona. Inkampani yayithenge le operation ngesikhathi i-boom, yenza i-headgear ibe yinhle, yenza yonke into ibe i-automatic, kodwa yashiya amathambo omsebenzi omdala engathintwanga, ngoba ukuvala izindawo ezindala kudla imali futhi akubhalwa kwi-spreadsheet.

I-collapse ivale i-main decline. Amamitha angamashumi amane edwala, ikhonkrithi, ne-rail egobile, amamitha ayizinkulungwane ezintathu phansi, ibambe amadoda ayisikhombisa engxenyeni inkampani ebikade ibona njenge-tomb.

U-Arin uchithe amahora ayisithupha ephikisa leyo modeli.

"Ngibonise i-ventilation plan endala futhi," esho. "'71 revision. Overlay ne-seismic."

Isikrini saqalaza futhi, sahlanzeka, ama-dataset amabili ahlangana kahle.

Nakhu. Wayekubonile kudala emuva emahoreni amathathu angakholwa, waphuma wabuyela, kwathi kusesele, kwaziwa ukuthi akusilo iphupho lokukhathala.

"Atlas. Ubonani?"

Kwathuleka. Uma u-Atlas ekhuluma, wayekhuluma ngendlela eqondile yomuntu ongathandi ukuba neqiniso ngezinto ezimbi.

"Kukhona i-return airway. Bayiyekisa ngo-'88 ngesikhathi beqhuba i-ventilation shaft entsha. Ihamba eceleni kwe-main decline, ihlukaniswe amamitha ayi-18 edwala phakathi kwayo yonke indawo." Umugqa wakhanya ku-overlay, mpunga njengephimbo, uhamba eduze ne-mass ebomvu efile ye-collapse. "Ayikaze ivalwe. Ivalwe phezulu nge-concrete bulkhead, bayakhohlwa. I-micro-seismic record ikhombisa amandla e-collapse ehambe *kude* nayo. I-airway endala isesehlezi. Okungenani, ngesikhathi sokugcina, emavikini ayi-11 adlule."

"Ibanzi kangakanani?"

"Lapho incane khona—" u-Atlas waqhamuka nokungabaza, okwakuyimpendulo ngokwako. "Ama-millimeter angu-640. Kukhona indawo eduze ne-level 98 lapho i-roof yawela khona kuma-eighties yavalela i-airway. I-survey ikhombisa igebe. 640. Cishe amamitha ayi-11."

U-Arin wahlala wathule, ecubungula.

U-Guardian, uma ehlanganisiwe, uba no-900mm emahlombe.

"Faka i-assist suit kuphela," esho. "Akukho shell ka-Guardian. I-layer yangaphakathi kuphela."

"Khona-ke uba no-410mm emahlombe," kusho u-Atlas. "Uyalingana. Kodwa awunayo i-protection egcina umuntu ephila amamitha ayizinkulungwane ezintathu phansi komhlaba, indawo ingazinzile, umlilo ungavuka, umoya mubi, kudilika amatshe." Izwi lakhe laqhekeka kancane. "Indlela iyaphilwa umzimba. Akuyona indlela ephilwa umzimba *ophilayo*, ongenasivikelo, ngesikhathi esidingekayo. Siyenzile i-model. Mbonise, Librarian."

I-window entsha yavuleka, ikhombisa i-traversal estimate, i-survival curve ehle ngathi iwile ngo-18min.

"Futhi u-Guardian angeke angene," kusho u-Arin.

"Ku-98 pinch? Cha. Uma ehlanganisiwe. Akukho standard config." Kuthule. "U-Atlas unomqondo amesabayo."

"U-Atlas akesabi."

"U-Atlas *uqaphela,*" u-Atlas walungisa ngesithunzi, "futhi lokho sekukusindise kabili kule nyanga." Wathi kancane: "I-pangolin geometry. Ama-plates ayazislayida, ayazigoqa. Yenza i-partial fold—hhayi sphere ephelele, i-asymmetric collapse, udonse ama-shoulder plates ngaphakathi—sinciphisa ububanzi. Hhayi ku-410. Mhlawumbe 700. Mhlawumbe ngaphansi, uma sivuma ukulimaza i-left actuator."

"700 angeke ingene ku-640."

"Cha," u-Atlas wavuma. "Yingakho umuntu—" leli gama lehlile njengekhethini—"kumele abe ku-98 pinch. Ngaphandle kwe-suit. Khipha amatshe ngesandla. I-suit ilinde. Bese uyaphinda uhlanganisa, phansi, emnyameni, umoya umubi, ususengozini."

U-Arin wabuka i-survival curve. Wabuka i-press feed, indoda enezandla phansi, imindeni engakabuyeli ekhaya.

"Ngiyena lowo muntu," esho. "Ngiyena umuntu."

I-workshop yathula. I-substrate yamemeza kancane. Ngaphandle, kude, idolobha lakhala ngomsindo wasemini: i-siren, i-taxi horn, imisindo evamile yendawo lapho kungabekwa muntu khona.

"Yebo," kusho uSAGE. Izwi elihlangene. Bonke ndawonye, okwakungajwayelekile ngaphandle kokuthi bayavumelana impela. "Uyena lowo muntu."

---

Washayela i-AugmenTech, ngoba wayefanele. Ngoba amadoda ayisikhombisa, futhi ngoba indlela kuphela yokuqhubeka engapheli ejele noma i-suit ithathwe, idlula enkampanini ephethe ama-patents ebuhlungwini bakhe.

UDkt. Helen Okonkwo wamthatha esemotweni. Wezwa i-suspension, umsindo wamasondo, i-acoustic ethile yomuntu osendleleni, onqume ukubhekana naye ngaphambi kokufika endaweni embi.

Wamthumelela i-overlay kuqala. Wayesekufundile, ngendlela enzima, eminyakeni, ukuthi abantu baphikisana ngamazwi, kodwa bayavuma uma bebona idatha. Ngakho akamuphi mazwi. Wamnika i-airway, i-pinch, i-seismic propagation, i-survival curve, i-partial-fold geometry, konke, ephaketheni u-Atlas ayelakhele ukuba angeke kube nempikiswano.

Wathula isikhathi eside. Umsindo wamasondo waqhubeka ngaphansi kokuthula.

"Arin. Lalela. Ngisekela wena. Ngaphezu kokwazi kwakho."

"Kukhona indlela ongaphila ngayo."

Kukhona indlela engathi shu, mfethu. Indlela yasemoyeni esebenza iminyaka engamashumi amane, i-survey ka-’71, uphahla okungakaze kubonwe muntu—futhi ufuna ukuthumela izinsimbi ezithumele abantu abathathu esibhedlela ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule. Akukho certification. I-regulator isemnyango wethu wokulayisha. I-platform ivaliwe, awekho ama-operation allowed. UBrand wayifaka i-grounding ngosuku afika ngalo. Awuvumelekile noma ngabe ukufaka amandla kule suit, ungasho nokuthi—kukhona u-IAOC officer phakathi kweGuardian nomnyango kuphela i-suit edlula kuwo." Waphefumula. "Ngitshele engikushiyile."

"Lona uphahla owehle ngama-eighties usemafayilini. Isimo siphendulwe kahle—"

"Angilwi nobunjiniyela bakho." Izwi lakhe laphenduka laphaphama, kwase kuba mnene, ngathi isandla esikhumbula ukuthi siphethe into ebuthakathaka. "Angikaze ngilwe nobunjiniyela bakho. Ngikutshela ukuthi kwenzakalani. Sithatha le nto siyise emayini. I-mine inama-lawyer. I-mine inebhodi esevele isithathe i-rescue yayenza i-recovery. Okusho ukuthi, ngokwe-corporate, *sibale laba bantu sabadedela.* Uyangena ne-machine eyenziwe ekhaya engenayo i-certification. Best case bahleka. Worst case—bavuma, iyahluleka, abantu bayafa *phambi kwekhamera,* yonke insimu—yakho, imishini kayihlo, konke—kubuyela emuva iminyaka engamashumi amabili. Futhi usuyinsizwa ebulele abasebenzi abayisikhombisa nge-robot."

"Futhi uma ngingenzi lutho," kusho u-Arin, "bayafa noma kunjalo. Kancane. Akekho ozothi ubabulala."

Umsindo wemigwaqo waqhubeka.

"Lokho akulungile," esho yena.

"Cha. Akulungi." Wabuka i-curve. "Yilokho engihlale ngikuthola. Akukho kulungile. Uphahla aluzange lwehle ngoba kwenziwa kahle. I-decline yahluleka ngenxa yokuthi ama-bolt ashibhile awakwazanga ukuthwala umthwalo, okuyinto umuntu othile ebhodini wayinquma phansi." Wezwa izwi lakhe lishintsha, njengezwi lika-Atlas. "Angiceli inkampani ukuthi ibe fair. Ngikutshela ukuthi ikhona indlela. Wena uyangitshela ukuthi ayivumelekile. Izinkinga ezahlukene."

Waphefumula isikhathi eside. Njengomuntu ozothi okungafanele kuzwakale.

"Ngiphe kuze kuse," kusho u-Okonkwo. "Ngizoshaya amafoni. Ungenzi lutho kuze ngikubizele. Ngithembise."

"Angiyikufaka le suit phansi ngaphambi kokusa," kusho u-Arin, okuyiqiniso, nokuthi yena wakuzwa njengokuvuma, nakuba kwakungafani.

---

U-Okonkwo washaya amafoni emotweni, waphinda nasekhishini elimnyama endlini ayengahlalanga kuyo kahle, amafoni aba udonga yena emi lapho iketela liphola.

U-Pretorius kuqala, ngoba ama-legal aphendula masinyane futhi awalele. Wafaka imisho emithathu—indlela, abasindile, iwindi—ngaphambi kokuba athi igama ayazi ukuthi uzolithola, elithi *exposure,* wabe esethi *interim restraint* no-*contempt* no-*personal liability of officers,* okwakusho ukuthi cha. Wayelindele ukuthi uPretorius athi cha. UPretorius uhlawulwa ukuthola i-cha bese ekunikela kahle. Nalokho kwakuwuhlobo lomusa; akazange azame ukwenza sengathi kunethemba.

U-chair webhodi kwaba kubi kakhulu, ngoba wavuma naye. Wathi labo bantu kumele bafinyelelwe. Wakhuluma kanzima, ngezwi alisebenzisa emngcwabeni nasekuvuleni izinto, wabe esechaza, kancane, ngendlela ochaza ngayo isimo sezulu kumntwana, ukuthi inkampani angeke ibe yiyo egunyaza i-machine engena-certification emayini evaliwe i-regulator, hhayi lapho i-IAOC imile emnyango wabo wokulayisha, hhayi lapho amakhamera esemnyango wemayini. *Uma kwenzeka kahle, siyahlanya futhi sithole inhlanhla. Uma kwehluleka, sibabulali. Akekho ozothi siyizigwili esizenzile into yesibindi, Helen. Leyo version ayisiyona esingayikhetha.* Wayengaphosile. Leyo ngxenye yayimphatha esifubeni njengedwala elingagayiwe. Wayengaphosile, futhi wayemtshela ukuthi cha, futhi kokubili bekuyiqiniso.

Wazama i-insurer's after-hours line ecabanga ukuthi impendulo eqondile, noma ibuhlungu, ibalulekile.
