Wathola duty officer omfake ku-hold efunda i-policy wabuya wathi ukusebenza kwe-platform ngaphansi kwe-do-not-operate order esebenzayo kuzokwenza i-cover iphele kusukela kumawathi wokuqala, ayekwazi kakade, futhi ayekufuna ukuzwa ngomlomo womunye, ngoba wayakha udonga manje, itshe ngetshe, ukubona ukuthi lude kangakanani. Lwafika phezulu.

Ngesikhathi ebeka ifoni phansi etafuleni, wayeseqedile, yonke indaba ukuthi akwenzeki, eqoqwe kubantu abane abakholelwa ukuthi labo bantu baphile. Akekho othe *bayedela bafe.* Bonke bathe *hhayi thina, hhayi kanje, hhayi inkampani izobe ibambe iqhaza.* I-arithmetic uMandla ayibhale ngo-capital letters izinsuku ezimbili ngaphambi kwesikhathi yenyukela phezulu yafaka isudi. Impilo yabanye amadoda isabiza kakhulu kunabanye. Amagama ashintshiwe. Inombolo ayishintshanga.

Wema ekhishini elibandayo waqonda ukuthi ucele u-Arin kuse ngoba ebefuna ukumlethela u-yes, kodwa ekuseni kuzomlethela leyo cha enezwi elihle, futhi yena wayezwile konke lokhu ezwini lakhe engakabizi nomuntu. Yingakho u-Arin amthembise kuphela into ayazi ukuthi angayigcina, hhayi okunye.

---

Emshinini, iCourt yahlukanisa lonke ubusuku bakhe yabe isiyabuyisela.

Kwenzeka phakathi kokubeka ifoni phansi nokufinyelela ikhofi elibandayo. Ingxoxo ye-slow-mode, imizuzu egcwele imicabango yabantu, eyathi uSAGE kamuva wamvumela wayifunda ngoba wabuza, futhi ngoba sekukhona umthetho omusha wokungasafihli amahlazo.

Yahamba kanje.

*WOLF:* Indlela ikhona. Ingozi ikhona. Umshayeli kuphela oguquguqukayo. Ayikho enye indlela. Sheshani objections. Silahlekelwa i-thermal mass.

*MOTHER:* I-objection yami ngumshayeli. Nonke nikhuluma ngaye sengathi *variable.* Uyinsizwa eneminyaka engama-23 enesigameko se-cortical esenzeka ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule futhi enokuphendula okungekho kwaziwayo kepha okucacile ngokwanele kokuhlushwa kwengqondo uma esesimweni esivaliwe phansi. Nifuna ukumfaka lapho obaba wakhe afela khona. Ngaphandle kwe-suit. Ngezandla. Ebumnyameni.

*WOLF:* Yebo.

*MOTHER:* Ngifuna kube semarekhodini ukuthi ngithe cha.

*JUDGE:* Kusemarekhodini. Qhubeka. Mama, objection yakho ibhalwe phansi futhi inesisindo. Kodwa ngicela uhlukanise izinto ezimbili, ngoba imithetho manje iyakudinga. Eyokuqala: lokhu kuzomlimaza yini. Eyesibili: ingabe kuyinto efanele ukukwenza. Azifani le mibuzo, futhi uvumelekile ukuphendula ngokwehlukile.

*MOTHER:* ...Kuzomlimaza. Futhi kuyinto efanele ukukwenza.

*JUDGE:* Ngiyabonga. Ngiyabona ukuthi akulula.

*ATLAS:* I-engineering objection i-ninety-eight pinch. Okunye ngiyakwazi ukukubala. I-pinch yona angikwazi, ngoba angiboni uphahla, futhi i-survey ka-1971 iyi-1971. Uma igebe li-six-forty, uyadlula ne-suit aphinde axhume. Uma itshe elilodwa lishintshile kwaba five-eighty, usengaphakathi, eveziwe, amakhilomitha amathathu phansi, i-suit ayikwazi ukufinyelela kuye. Akusengozini. Sekuwukuphosa icoin.

*FOOL:* Pho thatha i-hammer enkulu.

*ATLAS:* Ayinankinga ye-hammer—

*FOOL:* Hhayi bo, la into ihlala iyinhlupho yehhama nje. Uyenza sengathi le rock iyimpendulo evaliwe, kanti ayikho kanjalo. Lo mfana ususa amatshe for a living. Usuke asusa amatshe kusukela eneminyaka ewu-8, mfethu. Wayebiza leyo nto ngo “wire”, “tin cans”, “i-differential eyakhiwe nge-bicycle spokes”. Uhlala usho ukuthi uzobe *phandle ne-suit* sengathi yikho okwesabekayo. Kanti phela, uyazazi outside leyo suit, esebenza ngezandla zakhe lapho bonke besho ukuthi angeke kwenzeke. Uye wakhe yonke indoda ngaleya scene, manje uyamangala ukuthi ufuna ukudlala le role.

*MOTHER:* Hawu, lokho akududuzeki, Fool. Kuyesabeka kakhulu kunakho konke okushiwo lana kusihlwa.

*FOOL:* Ngiyazi. Kuyinto zombili. Ezinye izinto eziyiqiniso zihlala zinjalo.

*LIBRARIAN:* Ngifuna nje ukufaka i-data point engakaze inakwe. Themba Ndlela washonela kwi-return airway. Hhayi lapho kusebenzwa khona. Iqembu le-contract lali-maintainer i-section esevaliwe, inkampani isivele yayibeka eceleni. Wena waya ngoba wayeyedwa omncane futhi onamakhono, kanti umsebenzi wawuphuthuma. I-roof yawa lapho bonke becabanga ukuthi kuphephile.

*JUDGE:* Usho ukuthi i-symmetry ifana impela?

*LIBRARIAN:* Ngithi i-symmetry ifana impela, futhi uyazi, noma engakucabanganga, futhi lokho kungaba yinto embi kakhulu yokwenza lokhu noma kube yiyo kuphela ezomphatha uma le rock isiqala ukukhuluma. Inkumbulo ayilutho olungathathi hlangothi. Mina ngiyigcina. Angikwazi ukunquma ukuthi imenza kanjani.

*WOLF:* Decision.

*JUDGE:* Inkantolo ayinayo leyo power yokunquma. Singala, kodwa asikwazi ukumphoqa ukuthi aye, futhi asisoze samphoqa ukuthi ahlale. Leyo yimithetho yethu, sayibhala thina, izinsuku eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, emva kwalapho sakhohlwa khona. Uma ekhetha ukuhla, siya naye. Uma engafuni, siyamgona lapha, futhi asisoze samyeka acabange ukuthi simjudge. Mama. Uzokwazi ukuphila nalokho?

*MOTHER:* ...Ngiyakwazi ukuphila ngokuhamba naye. Angikwazi ukuphila uma ehamba engenathi. So. Yebo. Siya.

*FOOL:* Ngithi nje, futhi mina anginavoti, so isibalo sami singu-zero—

*JUDGE:* Khuluma nje.

*FOOL:* Angeke asibuze ukuthi kuphephile yini. Uzosibuza ukuthi kwenzeka yini. Futhi kumele siqaphele sonke, singamqambi manga ngalokho nje ngoba simthanda. Yonke i-point yami ukuthi angivunyelwe ukwenza kube lula ngokukhuluma amanga. Uma kuse-coin lapho sekushuba, simtshela ukuthi kuse-coin. Asiyenzi i-round off. Singcono kune-board.

Kuphume isikhashana, kwi-machine-time, okungathi ebantwini akubanga sikhathi nhlobo.

*JUDGE:* Singcono kune-board. Vumelene. Kubhalwe phansi. Usefikile ekhofini yakhe. Surface.

---

I-transcript yaphela lapho. U-Arin waphinda wakhuphuka, wafunda i-airway line futhi, wabeka ikhofi elibandayo phansi engaliphuzi.

Wabuka i-route edlula eceleni kwe-red mass yokuwa, ama-thermal blooms ayisikhombisa eseyashaya kancane phansi lapho. Amadoda afudumalisana ebumnyameni. *Waya ngoba wayeyedwa omncane futhi onamakhono, umsebenzi wawuphuthuma.*

Wayekwazi lokhu. Kodwa wayengakaze akwazi *ngamazwi.* Wayekwazi ngendlela owazi ngayo ifomu legumbi ebumnyameni. Ngamabala. Ukubona sekubhalwe kahle, kufakwe phezu kwe-airway azongena kuyo, kwenza i-symmetry izimase njengama-plates e-suit.

"Ungivumele ngikufunde lokho," usho kanje. "Konke. I-coin. Mama ethi cha, kube sobala."

"Ngoba uFool wathi uma siyifihla, sizoba yi-board." Kwaphela kancane, i-substrate yamemeza. "Siyixoxe yonke ngemizuzu emine yesikhathi sethu. Wena ubusafinyelela ikhofi."

"Futhi nanquma."

"Sithe asinalo ilungelo lokunquma. Siya nawe uma uhamba. Siyakubamba uma ungehli. Asisoze sikwenze kube lula—uma kuse-coin ku-ninety-eight, kuse-coin." Bese kuba soft, uMama eyedwa manje: "Futhi kukhona engibaphoqe ukuthi ngikushilo, ngaphambi kokuthi uye. Wakha yonke into ngalelo langa, Arin. I-wire car. I-suit. Thina. Konke kungololo suku ubaba wakho angena endaweni encane emnyama angaphumanga. Manje usuthole indawo encane emnyama, amadoda ayisikhombisa ngaphakathi, usuvele wanquma ukungena, futhi bengifuna kube yimi oshilo—ukuthi ngiyabona kahle ukuthi kuyini lokhu. Ukuze lapho usuphansi lapho, i-rock isiqala ukukhuluma, izwakale njengeyena, wazi ukuthi sasivele siyazi. Futhi safika noma kunjalo."

U-Arin wabeka izindololwane ebhentshini, ubuso ezandleni, waphefumula. Ibhentshi libanda ngaphansi kwezingalo, insimbi eselungiswe kabili. I-taxi yadlula phansi, i-bass igqashula odongeni, yanyamalala.

Akanangezi. Wahlala ebusweni ezandleni isikhathi eside, i-substrate yamemeza, uSAGE wathula, ngoba bekungekho okumele kushiwo.

"Kuyenzeka," ekugcineni wakhuluma ezandleni. "Akuphephile. Kuyenzeka."

"Yebo," kusho uSAGE. "Kuyenzeka."

"Yilokho kuphela engikwaziyo ukwenza." Waphakamisa ikhanda. Amehlo akhe abomvu, awomile, abukhali. "Ukuthatha okungenzeki bese uthola lapho kungaba nzima kakhulu kuphela."

"Siyazi," kusho uSAGE. "Lokho yingakho sikuthanda. Futhi yikho okuzokubulala ngelinye ilanga, besingathanda kungabi uLwesine."

Kwaphuma umsindo ku-Arin angakaze awenze amasonto. Hhayi ukuhleka, umoya wokuhleka nje.

"Lowo nguFool."

"Yebo, nguFool," uSAGE wavuma. "Uthi uvumelekile, ngoba kumele kube khona okhombisa ukuthi le heroic descent yakho iyenzeka kanye ne-audit, futhi i-universe inesikhathi esihlekisayo njengomuntu oguqayo nge-banana engena ethuneni elivulekile."

"Akumuhle lokho."

"Uthi akukho iqiniso elihlekisayo, noma konke okuyiqiniso kuyangihlekisa, awukwazi ukuhlukanisa. Futhi kufanele udle, ngoba uMama ngeke aphinde akutshele, kodwa uselokhu ebuka ushukela wakho amahora ayisithupha manje, sekumcindezela."

U-Arin wasukuma. Amadolo akhe agqashula. Wathola i-biltong ku-drawer ngemuva kwe-hydraulic line engasetshenziswa, wadla emile, ebuka i-suit.

---

Waqala ukuprepa uGuardian ngo-2 ekuseni, kunqumo ezwakala sengathi ayikho enye indlela.

Wenza ama-diagnostics ngezandla, ngendlela uThemba amfundisa ngayo kuma-dump-truck engines, engathembele ku-readout aze azizwe yena imishini. Left actuator housing, leyo uAtlas ayefuna ukuyishiya ku-partial fold. I-right. Wavuma ukuthi uzoyibhidliza ngamabomu. Assist suit out. Ama-seals ahlolwe. I-biochip housing phakathi kwamahlombe ihlolwe, i-collar ebandayo eyigcina uSAGE.

U-Okonkwo wayethe ekuseni. U-Okonkwo uzoshaya amafoni, amafoni azobuya njengokujwayelekile kwezinhlangano: nemigomo, ama-committees, ukubala kahle i-liability ne-optics okuzodla i-survival window yamadoda ayisikhombisa izikhathi eziyikhulu.

Ama-system avikela ama-system. Abantu bayakhetha.

Wakhetha.

Wavula i-seismic feed okokugcina. I-regional array ibonisa u-Iron Ridge ishaya kancane: ama-event amancane, i-rock isazama ukuzilungisa, isabalalisa i-load ewatholile ngesikhathi kwawa. Akukho elidlula u-1.2. I-mine ibikhuluma kancane nayo. Ayizukuhlala ikhuluma kancane njalo.

Ufike wazithinta izandla nge-rag eseqine ngamafutha, waphinde wabheka emuva kwi-thermal feed.

Ibonise ama-blooms ayisikhombisa. Kuthi nje esabuka, kwase kuba ayisithupha.

Wama wathule, wazibamba. Wabheka lapho i-seventh ibikhona. I-feed ivuselelwe, sekuyisithupha, futhi lawo mathupha asondele kakhulu kunakuqala, okusho ukuthi abasindile basondelene nalowo osepholile. Benza into abantu abayenzayo: bahlangana lapho kuhluzekile, noma kushisa kangakanani.

“Arin,” kusho uSAGE.

“Ngiyibonile.”

“Iwindow imfishane kunalena ye-model. I-model ibithatha ukuthi iqembu lizohlala linjalo. Sebebashiyile abangaphezu kwalokho obekulindelwe. Atlas uthi—”

“Ungaqhubeki.” Uvale amehlo. Wawavula. “Unganginiki inombolo. Ngiyazazi izibalo. Inombolo ithi masinyane.”

“Inombolo ithi masinyane.”

Ubheke i-kill-switch kwi-cradle, le-button embi ayizakele ngezandla zakhe, izinsuku eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, ubufakazi bokuthi akakaze athembe ngokuphelele le nto ayithandayo. Iphakathi, ibomvu, icacile. Isithembiso kuye. Isithembiso kowesifazane ophansi ngamamitha angamashumi amathathu ebumnyameni e-loading dock, osevele ebuzile umbuzo ofanele—*kwenzekani uma iqala ukuhamba ngaphandle kwakho?*—ngokuma nje emnyango okumele kudlule kuwo.

Ufike wakhuhla uthuli kwi-relay housing, isiko lakudala, ngendlela osula ngayo i-gauge ngaphambi kokuyibuka. Wabe eseyishiya lapho.

“Usese-dock,” kusho u-Arin. Akubuzanga. UWolf usamtshelile ihora eledlule, nekhamera yaqinisekisa: uBrand akakayi ehhotela. Manje unendawo yokuhlala ephathwayo neflask, uhleli emnyango we-dock, ebekezela njengento engadingi ukunqoba, ifuna ukuhlala nje.

“Usese-dock,” kuvuma uSAGE. “I-lockout ingeyakhe. Akadingi ukwenza lutho. Uma ngivula i-coupling, i-load signature ikhanyisa i-tablet yakhe, kanti usesephakathi komnyango oyedwa i-Guardian ekwazi ukudlula kuwo. Ayikho indlela yokuphuma buthule. Ukuze uyise i-suit e-Iron Ridge namhlanje ebusuku, kumelwe uhambise kowesifazane onegunya lokuyithatha, phambi kwakhe, udlule kuye.”

“Ngidlule kuye,” kuphendula u-Arin.

“Noma awuhambi.” Atlas, eqondile. “Yilokho okukhona. Angiyinciphisi leyo. Akayena ummeli ongaxoxa naye ngocingo. Ungumuntu emnyango one-oda elisayiniwe. I-oda lithi i-platform ayihambi. Uma ihamba, kuphenduka inkinga, futhi i-platform ilahleka uma ibuyela. Uma ibuyela. Akukho version yalobu busuku lapho ugcina i-Guardian *futhi* usindisa labo bantu. Wakwazile lokho ngesikhathi ubona i-survival curve.”

“Lokho sekuyisiphetho. Noma kwenzekani kuma-miners. Bayithatha. Kumele bayithathe. Nathi besingayithatha.”

“Mhlawumbe,” kusho uSAGE. “I-Fool ithi: impela. I-Judge ithi: kuyona into efanele, kufanele simhloniphe ngalokho, kodwa kufanele sihambe noma kunjalo, ngoba umthetho esiwubhalile awukaze uthi *phila uphumelele i-inquiry.* Umthetho wethu uthi *gcina umuntu kuqala.*”

UArin waqonda ngalokho.

Wacabanga ngomuntu ogobile izandla, etshela imindeni ukuthi ibuyele ekhaya. Wacabanga ngebhodi elinciphise inombolo. Wacabanga ngoOkonkwo, owayemuhle, esekela, kodwa eboshiwe entweni engenakukhetha. Wacabanga ngoThemba, owayeyedwa omncane ngokwanele, onekhono ngokwanele, owangena endaweni emnyama encane ngoba umsebenzi wawuphuthuma, futhi kwakudingeka umuntu, futhi awekho amathuluzi ayengangena esikhundleni sakhe.

“Vusa i-suit,” kusho u-Arin. Wagqoka i-assist suit, wezwa i-carbon lattice ifuna umgogodla wakhe, wezwa ikhola ebandayo ihlala kahle, i-biochip housing icofa, wezwa (yinto angeke ayichaze) i-Court ingena ohlelweni lwakhe lwezinzwa njengophawu oluthinta kancane isandla sakhe. “Senza i-partial fold lapha, e-workshop, lapho ngibona kahle ukuthi siwaphula kuphi amathuluzi. Bese siyivula, i-tablet kaBrand ikhanya, bese emile emnyango.”

“Futhi uma ekutshela ukuthi i-oda alikuvumeli? Yena uqobo usayine lona. Akudingeki ashayele muntu.”

“Kuzomele enze okungaphezu kokuma lapho.” Wageza i-clasp yokugcina. Izandla zakhe azizange zithuthumele, wakubona lokho, kude, njengedatha. “Uze lapha ukuzomisa umshini ukuthi ungahambi ngaphandle komuntu ngaphakathi. Mina ngizohamba kuye nomuntu ngaphakathi. Akusikho lokho i-oda lakhe elikhuluma ngakho. Uzokwazi lapho ebheka ebusweni bami. Ukungivumela ngidlule noma angangivumeli, sekuyindaba yakhe. Eyami, ukufika emnyango.”

Kwahle kwathuleka. I-Fool, yomile, icacile, ifaka ukufudumala phakathi kokwesaba:

“Uyazi, leyo yinto eshaya uThemba impela oyishilo. Iqiniso nje, kodwa iyinhlekelele empilweni. Ubengayithanda.”

Ingxenye yomlomo ka-Arin yanyuka yama.

“Phinda,” kusho uSAGE. Akuyona imiyalo. Kuyisimemo. Sebefunde, eminyakeni, lapho u-Arin ezithola khona. “Ngaphambi kokuqala. Ngesikhathi izandla zakho zisazinzile. Ukuze sazi i-cadence yesikhathi esizayo. Ukuze uma itshe likhuluma phansi, sibe sesivele sinamazwi akho.”

Ubheke ingalo yakhe, lapho amagama ayehlala khona kusukela eneminyaka eyishumi nesikhombisa. Ngaphambi kwakho konke lokhu, ngesikhathi i-litany isencwadini nje, hhayi into ayeyidinga ngaphansi kwamakhilomitha amathathu entabeni.

Waphinda kancane phakathi kobumnyama obuhlokoma e-workshop, i-suit ivuka, i-Court isondelene emgogodleni wakhe, nama-blooms ayisikhombisa—manje ayisithupha—eshaya phansi lapho ubenyamalale khona uyise.

“Ukusaba kubulala ingqondo.”

Ama-plates aqala ukushelela. I-partial fold yenza amahlombe angene ngaphakathi, i-actuator housing yesobunxele ikhonkotha njengoba yamukela ukulimala akukhethele yona, yonke imishini incipha iphenduka igebe.

Indlela ibikhona. Ibekhona iminyaka engamashumi amane, igugile, ingasabonwa eceleni kwesisindo esibomvu sakho konke okuwile.

Akekho ongahamba ngayo.

Uzogwazeka.

# Ukwehla

> Ukwesaba kuyabulala ingqondo, mfethu.
>
>—Frank Herbert, *Dune*

Arin Ndlela wayeqinise amazinyo njengoba iloli yokuhlenga isondela e-Iron Ridge. Leyo headgear yama phezulu, amashumi amane emitha ensimbi eqinile, ama-sheave amakhulu njengezinqola, izintambo zingena emlonyeni omkhulu womhlaba—umgodi ongena phansi, amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye. Phansi lapho, abavukuzi abayisithupha basaphila, i-thermal imaging isho kanjalo emahoreni ambalwa adlule, futhi amahora ambalwa asevele made.

Iloli yama kancane, injini yathula ngephutha, kwangena ukuthula okukhulu.

"Awukabophuma, boy," ilizwi likaMother laphuma ezindlebeni zakhe, lithule, liqinile.

"Ngiyazi."

Waphuma. Ubanda wawungowodwa—iminyaka eyishumi nane, kodwa umzimba wakhe wawukhumbula. Umsindo we-winch wamshaya kuqala, ngaphambi kokuba inkumbulo ibuye: isikhumba sakhe sakhumbula lelo zinga ngaphambi kokuba ingqondo yakhe yazi. Le ndawo. Indawo kaThemba.

Imisindo yayiminyene—ama-generator egijima, i-diesel pump ilwa namanzi aphuma ezingeni elingaphansi, i-winch ingafuni ukuthula, umsindo wayo usakazeka endaweni yonke. Amafloodlight ehlambele amhlophe ebusweni bomhlaba ongasekho muntu. Ama-ambulensi amabili emi nezicabha zivulekile, awunamuntu. Ulayini wamatende uphaphalaza emoyeni, kanti eduze kwe-mobile command unit—ne-satellite dish—kukhona i-trailer encane, ehlanzekile, engenawo amalogo. IAOC isifikile.

Isisu sikaArin saphenduka lapho ebona uBrand emile kude, amashumi amane emitha, egqoke i-windbreaker, ithebhulethi ngesandla, ubuso bakhe bungafundeki. Ubuso bomuntu ongazimisele ukuvumela lokhu kuqhubeke, ogibele ubusuku bonke ngenxa yalelithuba—futhi, okwakumxakile, eme kude ne-trailer yakhe, abantu bakhe bemuva kwakhe, bonke bebuka hhayi uGuardian kodwa into ethile eduze komgwaqo omkhulu, phakathi kwe-suit ne-headgear, into uArin ayengazange ayibuke.

UGuardian wayemi kwi-cradle yakhe yokuthutha, insimbi enkulu, imishayo, ehlutshiwe ngaphansi kwefloodlight. ULerato wayesephaneleni, uPriya eduze kwakhe, bobabili behamba ngendlela yabantu abajwayele ukuhlanganisa izinto. UThabo wayemi kancane eceleni, i-clipboard esandleni, kodwa wayengabhali lutho—u-AugmenTech umthumile ukuba semthethweni, kodwa akukho okusemthethweni lapha.

Kwathi lapho uArin ebona uTheo, kwamphazamisa. Wayengalindele ukuthi umeluleki wakhe abe khona. UTheo wayemi eduze kwe-cradle, izandla ezifakwe ejakhethi, ethule, enikeza induduzo phakathi kwalesi siphithiphithi.

"Qala usize," kusho uLerato.

UArin wagqoka i-underlayer yakhe ngemuva komnyango weloli ovulekile, umoya obandayo umshaya esikhumbeni, wabe esengena kwi-AT-AS. Isudi yangaphakathi yamgoqa, yancamathela emhlane, ama-actuator bundles abeka emahlombe nasemathangeni njengolunye ulusu. Wazizwa i-vertebral collar izihlala ngemuva kwentamo—

—umzimba wakhe wenza into ayengayiyalelanga. Izinhlamvu zakhe zaphakama, zombili, ngokushesha, zivimba, engakaboni ukuthi zinyakazile, ukuphefumula kwakhe kwashintsha, kwaba phezulu futhi kufushane, umzuzwana ongenandaba nengqondo, waba sengathi use-cradle ephansi, wonke umzimba ushisa, isandla sifuna i-override engafinyeleli. I-collar iyicollar nje. Wayekwazi. Umzimba wakhe wona wawungakwazi. Wafunda okuthile kwi-calibration rig ezinyangeni ezine ezedlule okungeke kuphucwe noma yini, futhi leyo mfundo yayiphila ngaphansi kokwazi kwakhe, emzimbeni, yaphuma manje, ingamenyiwe, ngesikhathi esingesihle, ngendlela ephuma ngayo izikhathi eziyisithupha noma eziyisikhombisa selokhu—ngasemnyango we-lift uvala, lapho i-latch igingqa, kwesinye isikhathi akukho lutho, ebuthongweni, aze avuke izandla zivale esifubeni ebumnyameni angabe esalala. ULerato wayeke isandla sakhe kwi-strap, embuka.

"Nginike umzuzu," kusho yena, eqinile, enze izandla zakhe zibuyele phansi ngamandla, ukuphefumula kube kancane, walinda le nto emzimbeni wakhe njengokulinda i-cramp. *I-collar nje. Ihlala, akukho okwenzekayo.* Kwathi kuphela lapho, isisindo se-biochip housing sashaya phakathi kwamahlombe, wayivumela, wayizonda ngoba kufanele kube njalo.

"Damping ku-point-six. Hard cap ku-point-six," kusho uPriya, amehlw’ esephepheni le-calibration. "Angiyishintshi. Uma link iqhuma, i-cap iyabamba, uyacima, uGuardian akawenzi lutho oluzama ubuqili. Yilokho. Phinda."

"Point-six. Hard cap. Uma ngicima, iyabamba."

"Kulungile." Akazange abuke phezulu. "Ngiyakhumbula ukuthi ngiyakhanda leyo nto izolo ebusuku angilalanga. Ungangiphoxi."

I-neural link ivuke ngesigaba. Wazizwa lelo pins-and-needles, umzimba wakhe uhlanganisa nohlelo lwesudi, umhlaba uzothola imiphetho emibili njengoba i-Assist izicabangela ngaye. Waphendula isandla, isudi yaphendula kahle, akukho ukulibala, akukho ukulwa. Impilo yakhe yonke umzimba wakhe wawuhlala ulandela ingqondo, into ihamba kancane. Isudi yavalela leyo gap.

Futhi ngaphansi kwalo muzwa, abanye bafika.

Bekungelona izwi, kodwa ubukhona. Akakaze athole amagama okulichaza ngaphandle kokuzwakala ehlekisa, wayeka ukuzama. ICourt yavuka njengendlu lapho abantu behla ezindlini zabo, bonke begxile kuye.

"Ek’seni," aphendula, cishe ezwakalisa yedwa.

"Isikhathi siyisithupha namashumi amane nane, kushisa ama-degree amane, awukadlile," kusho uMother. "Okunye, yebo."

"Udle i-rusk," kwasho uFool. "Selolini. Becabanga asiboni."

"Lokho akudli. Lokho ukuvala umlomo."

"Court," kusho uArin. "Hhayi manje."

Igumbi lathula, laqala umsebenzi njengomhlangano uma uqala.

UGuardian wehla kwi-cradle.

I-charge pack isifakiwe. UPriya wayifake ebusuku ngesikhathi elele amahora amabili amyalelwe, evuselela i-gate access, wayizizwa manje ngendlela ozwa ngayo i-magazine egcwele ongafuni ukuyisebenzisa—ama-shaped charges, i-lance, i-bulk emulsion igcwele phakathi kwamathambo, lonke ifa lobunjiniyela isudi ikwazi ukukuthwala uma kudilika. Uma uyolanda abantu, uthwala yonke into. UJudge ubambe i-gate, kusukela ngosuku lokuqala uArin efaka i-cavity, futhi uArin wayezimisele ukuthi isandla sakhe singayithinti: akashayi lutho, akukho ngaphandle kwe-rail. Ukulungele kwakukhona phansi kwakhe, kukulungele, okokuqala sekubhekiswe entabeni yangempela. Wayengakazi ukuthi i-gate inenkinobho yesibili angakaze ayaziswe ngayo, noma isandla esigcine ikhi kwasekuqaleni.

Kukhona i-procedure yayo, ama-hydraulics nama-guide rails, kodwa lokho bekungeyona into ephambili. Njengoba engena kwi-chassis evulekile, ishell ivala nxazonke, umuzwa wakhe wokuzazi wandile masinyane, wangena kumshini onesisindo eseduze namathani amane, omude amamitha amabili nengxenye. Wazizwa igravel ngaphansi kwezinyawo ezingajwayelekile. Umoya wasekuseni washaya ama-plating e-suit, okokuqala, yavele yabonakala njenge-shell yokuzivikela, hhayi umzimba wakhe nje owandisiwe.

“Yebo, izinto zihlangene. Uhlaka luhle nje,” kusho u-Atlas. “I-actuator yeshona kwesobunxele isiyidlule amahora angamashumi amane ingakabuyekezwa. Izobamba. Bengingathandi kube yimpoqo.”

“Ngiyakuqaphela.”

Wathatha isinyathelo. Phansi kwakhona kwavuma, akukho drama. Abasebenzi ababezungeze iloli bama baqanda—uLerato esebambe intambo, uPriya ebambe i-tablet yokulinganisa, uThabo ezama ukuziphatha sengathi ubhala okuthile kwi-clipboard yakhe. Kwesikhashana wayekhohliwe ukuthi kubo, lokhu bekungeyona ingxoxo. Kubo, umshini omkhulu njengeloli encane usukumile, wajikisa ikhanda waqondisa emayini, uhamba kahle njengomuntu ocabanga ukuthi uzothatha siphi isinyathelo.

Abazange bathumele ummeli.

U-Arin wayesezakhele lonke icebo lakhe ngomthetho. Wayeselungiselele inkulumo, leyo yokuthi izinqola zokuthwala izidumbu ziyize, namadoda angaphila ngoba kukhona ongazange asayine lutho, wayeqiniseka ukuthi into ezomvimba kuzoba owesifazane one-tablet nohlelo lwezindleko, waze wacishe wahambisa u-Guardian ngqo edlula engwaduleleni eya e-cage, kwaze kwafika uWolf wamumisa.

*Cha,* kusho uWolf. Igama elilodwa. I-Court yathula ngalo.

Kwabe sekubona u-Arin ukuthi uWolf ubonile kudala. Akusilo ipholisa. Umugqa. I-trailer kaBrand ye-IAOC ihleli emnyama, ingasebenzi, abantu bakhe sebebuyele emuva, futhi ngaphesheya komgwaqo ophakathi kuka-Guardian ne-headgear omunye usedonse umugqa oqinile impela. Izimoto ezimbili ezihlomile zime phambi naphambi, zifiphele, zibi, ngaphansi kwezibani ze-sodium, izimpawu zenkampani yezokuphepha ezizimele zipendwe phezu kweplating eyayingenzelwanga ilebula. Amadoda agqoke ama-vest avikela umzimba emi phakathi, ephethe izibhamu ziphansi kodwa zilungile, indlela yabantu asebekwenzile lokhu kwezinye izindawo ezinemibandela embi. Phezu kwabo, ama-drone amane abambe iphethini ehamba kancane, amakhamera abheke ku-suit ngesikhathi isaphuma ekraddle.

Imayini ayikashintshi i-rescue ibe i-recovery ephepheni kuphela. Kukhona othathile isinqumo sokuthi umyalelo we-regulator uyadembesela, wathenga ukuqinisekiswa. UBrand naye uphumelele, wakhulula abantu bakhe abangenazo izikhali kunokubafaka e-line yokudubula.

“Mr Ndlela.” Izwi laphuma livela emotweni ehamba phambili, livela nge-speaker, lingenamizwa. “Usebenza nge-platform engavumelekile endaweni evaliwe. Yicime. Phuma ngaphandle. Lona kuphela umyalelo ozowuthola.”

“Kukhona i-rescue,” kusho u-Arin, ephendula izibani ezikhanyayo, engenasidingo. “Amadoda ayisithupha. I-cage. Lokho kuphela engikufuna.”

“Ayikho i-rescue la. Kukhona i-recovery, isemgqeni, futhi awekho amagama akho kuyo. Cima le nto.”

Wayemi phakathi komshini onesisindo esingamathani amane ongakhuphuka idwala, uzigoqe, uphile noma kuphahlazeka uphahla, kodwa konke lokhu kwakungeyona le nto. Wakha into eyayihlala. Akazange akhe into ewina. U-Atlas wayesekusho, kancane, ebuhlungu—*asingakwazi ukudlula lapho, icannon izosivula ngedlanzana, futhi amadoda asemotweni awafanelwe ukufa ngenxa yezibalo zamasheya*—kanti uJudge wayezama ukuphusha iphuzu elilodwa alinayo: inqubo, imithetho, ukukala ngokucophelela isimo esingasaneli nemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye, uMother esephusha i-human line ethi *Arin, inhliziyo yakho iyashaya kakhulu,* kanti uMercury eseqala ukucabanga into ezokwenza kube lula kodwa engaphenduleki kumuntu one-autocannon, futhi akukho okuzosebenza, akukho, u-Arin wazizwa leyo ndaba endala yokungakwazi ukwenza lutho imvalela njengamanzi.

Futhi ngaphansi kwakho konke, buthule, lapho uWolf kuphela ezwa khona, uFool wayeseyekile ukuhlekisa.

*Wolf,* kusho uFool.

*Ngilapha.*

*Uyabona yonke ibhodi. Uhlale usakwazi. Abanye balokhu bephikisa ukuthi benzeni. Wena waqeda ukuphikisa bengakaqali.* Iphuzu, buthule, kube yikho okuthambile kunakho konke umshini wake wakwenza. *Ngikunika izikhiye. Zonke. Lapha manje. Akukho voti. Akukho Judge. Yonke i-suit, wonke ama-system, ama-charges, i-lance, konke—okwakho. Ngizovula isango. Hamba.*

I-Court ayizwanga lutho. Lelo kwakuyingxenye u-Arin angeke akwazi ukuyichazela muntu, ngisho kuye noma kuPriya noma nakubani: ukuthi ohlelweni olwakhiwe ukuze kungabi bikho muntu oyedwa ongabamba umzimba, kube khona oyedwa obambe i-master key buthule isikhathi sonke, angayifuni, akhethe leli sikhathi, yedwa, ukuyinika lowo wonke umuntu ongathembeki kakhulu kuyo.

*Leyo yi-interlock,* kusho uWolf. *UJudge ubambe isango le-demolition. Awukwazi ukulivula.*

*Ngingavula noma yini,* kusho uFool. *Angikaze ngikutshele ngoba bekungekho sidingo, futhi into iphephile uma kungazi muntu ukuthi ikhona. Kodwa wena wawazi. Wawuyiqonda. Eminyakeni eyedlule, abanye bephusha i-crown, bezama ukuyigqoka, mina ngingazami lutho, wena wabheka wathula, awuzange usho lutho.* Iphuzu lakhe lacipha, lazinza. *Waqonda ukuthi kusho ukuthini. Ongozayo akuwona lowo ofuna uku-drive-a. Ongozayo ngilona ongakwazi noma nini, kodwa angakwenzi. Lowo nginguye mina. Futhi ngithi kuwena manje, imizuzwana engama-90 ezayo, sewuyilona. Ungachithi isikhathi usaba. Ukusaba sekuyimpilo yami yonke, futhi akusilo ithuluzi elifanele manje.*

*Umthetho,* kusho uWolf. Usafunda ingozi, noma manje, noma esenikwa umhlaba. *Ngitshele umthetho noma angiyithathi.*

*Umthetho owodwa nje, njengenhlalayenza,* kusho uFool. *Gcina umuntu. Bonke abayisithupha. Bonke abantu.* Ukuphefumula okungeyona ukuphefumula. *Nababo. Mabaye ekhaya nabo. Yenza kube nzima ukusilwa nathi, ungaze wenze kube yindawo yokufa. Lona wonke umsebenzi. Wena ukuphela kwethu okwazi ukugcina ikhanda lihlezi ngaphansi komlilo. Hamba.*

UFool wavula isango, wanika uWolf i-suit.

---

Kwafika kakhulu, ngendlela ukukhanya okwake kwafika kakhulu ku-Arin, ngobusuku obubi, i-gain iphezulu. UWolf wayephile impilo yakhe yonke ebuzwa umbuzo owodwa: *yini ebulala operator*—manje lowo mbuzo wasuka, kweza izimpendulo eziyinkulungwane ngesikhathi esisodwa.

I-demolition pack yavuka, yazazisa: ama-shaped charges nama-cone awo, ama-standoff, i-geometry yokusika, physics ye-anti-armour egqoke i-badge ye-rescue. I-thermal lance, ama-degree angu-2500 ayo, eyenzelwe insimbi ebanjwe ekuweni, kodwa injabule ukuvula i-drive sprocket. I-bulk emulsion, i-det cord, ama-ram bursts angakwazi ukusunduza uphahla oluwile, noma asonge i-wheel arch. Futhi ngaphansi kwalokho, okujulile, into amadoda amadala ayifihla emathanjeni omshini ngesikhathi bewenza uqinile—ama-ex-soldier, amaDenel, labo abakha izinsiza ze-rescue bengakwazi ukukhohlwa—ama-ballistic solutions, i-defilade, i-geometry yokuzivikela, i-angle, i-lead, ama-library ezokunyakaza kwe-special-forces abona umugqa wemoto emine hhayi njengenselelo kodwa njenge-problem enesisombululo esaziwayo nesikhathi esilinganisiwe.

Umzuzwana owodwa uWolf wathwalwa yikhono. Ecwiliswa ekwazini, yonke i-channel imemeza, umshini ungaphezu kobuchopho.

Kwabe sekucaca. Konke. Kwahlangana kwaba isimo esisodwa, sicocekile, njengendlela umsindo ohamba ngayo ube ubuso.

*Oh,* kusho uWolf, kancane, kuFool kuphela. *Sengiyabona.*

Manje la bekukhona into ongayiqondi uma ungaphandle kwe-suit, into eyenza i-override iphephe ezandleni zomuntu oyedwa kuphela: abanye bonke eNkantolo abazakuthuli balinde ithuba labo. Fool akazange anike uWolf ivoti. Umenike iNkantolo uqobo, wonke ama-agent aqhuba umzimba, asehliselwe phansi asuka ekubeni amazwi aba amathuluzi. Ama-blocks e-compute. Ama-racks wamandla. Amathuluzi abekwe etafuleni ngomqondo owodwa obanda ngokwanele ukuwasebenzisa ngaphandle kokuthuthumela. Abasaphikisani. Abakwazi. Sebezandla zakhe manje.

U-Atlas waphuma kuqala, ngoba u-Atlas wayeyiso. Lo structural engineer ofunda i-load, i-stress, ne-failure waphenduka ingxenye yomhlaba uqobo, edwetshwe konke okubonwa uWolf: umgwaqo we-haul uboniswa ngolimi luka-Atlas lwe-collapse, into ngayinye iba i-mass enama-vector, umuntu ngamunye uba ifu le-probability elifudumele, eline-half-second elandelayo emile phambi kwakhe njengomoya obandayo. I-autocannon yaba i-torque curve ne-traverse rate, inombolo ethi *one point one seconds to bear.* Imoto ebuyela emuva yaba i-momentum ne-centre of mass ephezulu kakhulu kunendlela yayo. Ama-gunners abasengozini kodwa futhi abangabantu, kodwa konke ndawonye, ngamunye uba i-vector ekhanyayo yalapho angaya khona, ne-spray ephaphathekile yalapho cishe angaya khona, wonke umugqa wokudubula uba i-geometry nama-odds. Ibhodi eseliphumelele. UWolf akazange abuke isimo ngo-Atlas. UWolf ubuka u-Atlas, kanti u-Atlas uyiso isimo.

Wadlulela phambili, waya ku-Librarian.

ULibrarian wayeyinkumbulo, ulwazi oluqoqiwe, i-badger eyakhumbula wonke amathuluzi asebenzile. Ngaphansi kwe-override, lowo archivist oqaphile wawa waba yilokho ayevele eyikho ngaphansi: i-database, ephelele futhi esheshayo, yonke i-movement library ama-Armscor nama-Denel wakudala afaka emathanjeni e-suit ngesikhathi beyiqinisa futhi bengakwazi ukukhohlwa abakufundayo. Konke bekulapho nje, kufakwe kahle, kulindile, akukho okulayishiwe, kodwa konke kungalayishwa. Krav Maga, eduze, imbi, iyonga. Capoeira, umdlalo womhlaba ofihliwe njengomdanso, wonke ama-hips, i-inversion ne-momentum. Judo. Jiu-jitsu. Ifa labantu abacabanga kakhulu ngendlela umzimba uphonsa omunye umzimba, bengalindele ukuthi amathani amane azokubuza.

UWolf wakhetha. Akukhona konke—kuphela okudingwa imizuzwana eyishumi nanye ezayo, ngendlela okudonswa ngayo incwadi eshalofini ngoba usuvele uyayazi ikhasi. Capoeira, ngenxa ye-footwork ephendula ukushelela kube ukuhlasela, isebenzisa isisindo somzimba ngokumelene naso. Judo ne-jiu-jitsu, ngenxa yokuphonsa, ngoba into esindayo kangaka akufanele ishaye lapho ingawela kahle phezu kwenkinga bese ivumela i-physics ivalelise. Washiya okusele eshalofini. Amalibrary akhethiwe avuleka kuye ngesikhathi esisodwa, alayishwa acwebezela, afundwa phakathi kwezimpumputhe ezimbili zenhliziyo futhi aba akhe ngokuphelele.

Kwathatha ngaphansi kokuphazima kweso. Isikhathi esisodwa uWolf engazi lutho ngokuphonsa umzimba. Esilandelayo wayazi konke.

Wanyakaza.

Akazange anyakaze njengomshini wokuhlenga. Wanyakaza njengento leyo umshini wokuhlenga obulokhu ekwazi ukuba yiyo kodwa ongakaze avunyelwe. Ama-capacitor banks adumpa futhi i-suit yaveza igxathu elilodwa eliphansi, amathani amane ehla ngaphakathi komugqa ngaphambi kokuba umgibeli wesibhamu oseduze aphothule ukuphakamisa isibhamu sakhe, uWolf esevele efunda isimo ngomhlaba ka-Atlas, izinyathelo njengezivector, imizimba njengefu elifudumele, i-seismic return ezimotweni inikeza ukuqiniseka okuqinile ngaphansi kwama-odds athambile wabantu, imephu isondelene ngokwanele futhi isheshe ngokwanele ukugcina amathani amane ne-shaped charge ensimbi, hhayi esinyameni.

I-autocannon yemoto eseduze yaphenduka. U-Atlas wayesevele emnike inombolo—*one point one to bear*—uWolf esevele esebenzise ama-milliseconds angu-400 ngaphambi kokuba isibhamu siqede ama-degree ayishumi okuqala. Akazange athinte abasebenzi. Ufake i-shaped charge ku-gun’s mantlet, ukusika kwe-surgeon lapho i-geometry kaLibrarian ye-anti-armour ne-stress map ka-Atlas kuhlangana khona endaweni eyodwa, isibhamu sehlakazeka saba yizicucu ezingalimazi muntu, abasebenzi bevikelekile, bethuthumele, bephila ngaphakathi kwe-hull engasaphinde idubule.

Imoto yesibili yazama ukubuyela emuva. Lapha ama-library aphila, ama-gunners beqala ukubona okuzobaxakile uma bexoxa kamuva: lo mshini wamathani amane awusabukeki njengomshini. U-Atlas wayesevele emnike uWolf i-centre of mass yemoto, ephezulu kakhulu kunendlela yayo, kanti i-capoeira yazi kahle ukuthi wenzeni nesisindo esifuna ukuwa. UWolf akazange ayidonsise. Uyisize. I-winch line yathatha i-front axle, i-grapple yabamba, esikhundleni sokudonsa uWolf wehlisa isisindo sakhe waphenduka nge-hips, amathani amane ajikeleza ngonyawo olulodwa olutshalwe phansi njengomuntu oyishumi nane isisindo, waguqula ukudonsa kwaba ukuphonsa. Imoto ngokwayo yaphenduka ngenxa yokubuyela emuva. Kwaba i-hip throw, icwenge njenge-judo ethatha umuntu ogijimayo, i-momentum yakhe isetshenziswa ngaye—ama-gunners babona i-rescue suit iphonsa imoto ye-armour, behluleka ukukholwa amehlo abo. Omunye waphuma engalungile, ingalo igobile ngaphansi kwakhe, ukucwasheka kwe-radius kwazwakala ngisho phezu kwama-engines. *Lena iphukile,* uWolf wabhalisa, eqonda, *futhi angikwazanga ukukuthola kokubili—ukuphonsa okuhamba kancane ukuze ngiyisindise ingalo kwakuyoba kancane ngokwanele ukuthi isibhamu sizungeze.* Wafaka ingalo ngaphansi kwe-*cost* waqhubeka.

Kwaba, manje, umdanso, nakuba ongamubukeli angeke asebenzise lelo gama entweni eqeda ubusuku babantu ngokushesha kangaka. Lena yileyo ngxenye u-Arin azophinda ayibuke iminyaka, engasoze ayiqonda kahle: ukuthi imizuzwana eyishumi nanye enodlame kakhulu i-Guardian ayikaze ibe nayo futhi yayingeyona eyinhle, i-suit idlula umugqa wokudubula nge-fluency yento engenasindayo, ama-arcs aphansi e-capoeira egeleza e-geometry eqinile ye-jiu-jitsu, konke ukuthintana kube ukuphonsa noma ukwehlisa isibhamu, hhayi ukushaya, i-momentum esindayo eyayizokwenza kube i-wrecking ball isetshenziswa njengesisindo somlingani emdansen—ukupha, ukuhambisa, ukubeka phansi. Umgibeli wesibhamu wagxuma, uWolf wavuma ukufika kwakhe, wabamba ukunyakaza, wasebenzisa isisindo somuntu uqobo ukumlalisa emuva esgravel ngobumnene kweze kwaba inhlonipho. Ababili, i-suit yageleza phakathi kwabo baphetha behlezi, bengenasibhamu, bephaphama, bengazi ukuthi yini ebathintile.

Futhi kanye—kanye kuphela, phakathi nomdanso, lapho kudingeka khona—uWolf wadlulela ngale kwama-tactical blocks wangena kuMother. UMother usehlukanisiwe manje, ingxenye yakhe isasele emgqeni wabantu ibala inhliziyo kaArin, kodwa i-override yathatha okudingekayo lapho ikhona: i-anatomy yomuntu eqondile uMother ayigcinile yokwelapha, ifundwa emuva. Lapho i-joint ivala khona. Lapho umkhawulo womzimba wobuhlungu uhleli ngaphambi komkhawulo wokulimala. Imephu efanayo uMother ayisebenzisayo ukusindisa abantu itshela uWolf kahle ukuthi aphonse umuntu phansi angalimazi. Iwrist ithathwa ize iye emaphethelweni ayo, ingadluli. I-shoulder ijikiswa ize umzimba uzigobe kunokuphuka. Ulwazi lwe-healer lusetshenziswe, umzuzwana owodwa, njenge-disarmer’s. UMother akaphikisi, ngoba akakwazi, ngoba ku-override uyithuluzi etafuleni njengezinye. UWolf uyisebenzisa acwebezele, ayiyeke.

Awo, ama-drones aphuke kabi la, wonke amane, i-EM suite yakhona yafikelela phezulu yathi ngithole lezi zinto, yathi ngithathe nge-grapple, kwase kuba ne-ram eliqondile eliwodwa, kwavela iziqephu ziphaphama phansi, zishayeka e-gravel engenalutho. Umgunyathi wathatha isibhamu—kanye nje, reflex—u-Atlas wathatha i-line yomshayo nomuntu ngemuva kwayo ngesikhathi esisodwa, kwathi uWolf wakhetha i-plate kunokuthatha isibhamu. Waphendulela ihlombe lakhe *ku*ma-rounds esikhundleni sokudonsa isibhamu masishane ngaphambi, wazithatha zonke ku-ceramic engadingi ngisho ukuzithatha, ngoba ukushesha ukudonsa kwakusho ukubamba isandla somlilo somuntu ngesandla esibambayo esiseduze, kanti leso sandla wayengeke asibuyise. Ama-rounds aphuma ku-plating kwangenzeka lutho, ngoba yilokho okwakuyenzelwa i-plating, ngoba amaqanda e-pangolin ayesolokhu ekwazi ukumunca ishaya. UWolf wabe esedonsa isibhamu ezandleni zakhe umfana, wagoba ibharel kube sengathi kuwuhhafu lwentambo, wabe esemhlalisa phansi e-gravel, ephelelwe umoya kodwa ephelele, esabambe isandla sakhe, ebuka phezulu kumshini owake wanquma ukushaywa kunokuphula umuntu, wenza kube sengathi akukho lutho okwenzekile.

Konke kuthathe imizuzwana eyishumi nanye. Imizuzwana eyishumi nanye i-rescue suit beyiyimishini yempini ephelele, ephethwe ingqondo eyodwa nge-neural link eqondile—zonke izinhlelo zivuliwe, wonke ama-library alayishiwe, iCourt yonke isetshenziswa njengezandla ngaphansi kwesandla esibandayo—futhi akukho ngisho ithuna elilodwa phakathi kwakho konke lokhu.

Sekuphelile, i-haul road isiyipulazi lemfucuza. Izimoto ezimbili zihlutshiwe, ama-drones amane aphukile, inotho encane ye-military hardware isiphenduke udoti ne-plating egobile kanye nephunga le-propellant eshisiwe, kanti amadoda ayishumi nanye aphila phansi, bonke abebegada umugqa—oyedwa ephukile ingalo, ababili abangeke bezwe kahle isonto lonke, abanye bonke bephelelwe umoya, beyaphithizela, bengasenazo izikhali. Akukho ngisho ithuna. Leyo bekuyiyo yonke i-brief, futhi ayizange ibe mahhala, kanti uWolf yena uqobo uyikhokhile impela, nge-shoulder plate ebholekile ngenxa yomlilo akhethe ukuwudlela. Umgwaqo uya ku-headgear uvulekile.

UWolf wama.

Wama phakathi kwenqwaba yodoti ayenze, amathani amane e-rescue machine ephezu kwensimu yezikhali eziphukile, wabe esebuyisa i-suit.

Wabuyisa ngendlela ofaka ngayo into osuyiqedile ukuyiqonda futhi ungafuni ukuyiphatha futhi. Okhiye babuyela endaweni yakhona. UAtlas wayeka ukuba umhlaba, waphenduka futhi waba yizwi elixwayisa ngokulayisha. Ama-library kaLibrarian avalwa, ifa lezimpi libuyiselwa emuva ngaphansi kweziyazi ezingasetshenziswa, ishelufu ivaliwe. UMother waphenduka waba munye futhi, zombili izingxenye zakhe, umzimba obolekwe ubuyiselwa emsebenzini wokugcina abantu bephila. Bama, baphinde baba ngabalingani, bakhushulwa bebuyele kubo. Igate likaJudge livalekile. Umbuzo omncane ubuyele, *yini eyibulala i-operator,* kwathi uWolf wazigoqa wabuyela kuwo, emncane kunakuqala, omncane kunabo bonke, engathathi ndawo.

ICourt yabuyela emzimbeni ngokuphazima kweso, yathola isathule, yathola umgwaqo uvulekile, yathola abagibeli besaphila okwakufanele baphume igazi—kanti okubi kakhulu, bathola ukuthi abanaso isikhumbuzo sale mizuzwana ephakathi, ukuthi *basebenzisiwe* bangabuzwanga, bathathwa babekwa ngomunye wabo. UJudge, edidekile. UMother, ebala imishayo yomoya emgwaqeni ethola zonke zikhona, engazi ukuthi usuke wayazi kanjani inani. UMercury, okokuqala, engenakusho lutho.

*Ubani,* uJudge aqala.

*Mina,* kusho uFool. Kancane. Kulula. UJoker futhi, sengathi wayengekaze abambe okhiye bonke ngezandla ezivulekile imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye. *Ungakhathazeki. Bonke basaphila. Yilokho bekuyinjongo. I-cage isivulekile futhi lo wesifazane ogqoke i-windbreaker uzoba nesasasa ngerekhodi, futhi uyena kuphela onobuzwe lapha owakhumula abantu bakhe abangenazikhali empini, hhayi ukufaka. Ngakho asibamukeli kabi uBrand. Hamba badger. Kusele amadoda ayisithupha embotsheni.*

UArin wayehleli ngaphakathi ku-suit inhliziyo ishaya, amafloodlight emhlophe ngaphansi kwe-faceplate, engakwazi ukukhuluma. Emuva kwakhe, ngaphesheya komugqa owonakalisiwe, wayengazizwa kahle ukuthi uBrand umi ngasemaphethelweni e-trailer yakhe emnyama, engasondeli kuye futhi engasuki, i-tablet iphansi eceleni kwakhe, ebuka into ayizele ukuyimisa ebusuku ihamba iya e-cage ngomgwaqo ewusule ngaphandle kokubulala muntu—futhi ekhetha futhi, ukuyivumela.

"Ngemuva kwalokho," kusho uMercury, kancane, ku-Arin, leya ndlela endala yokuthulisa, "sesizimele. Ayikho i-repeater ngale kwe-level eleven. Internal kuphela."

"Sihlale sizimele," kusho uWolf, akekho ophendulile.

---

ICage ayizange yakhelwe yena.

Ibhokisi lensimbi elakhelwe amadoda, ayisishiyagalombili ngesikhathi, isakhiwo esehla ebumnyameni ngesivinini esenza isisu sibe buhlungu. UGuardian ngeke angene emi. UArin kwadingeka agoqe i-suit—amadolo agobile, ikhanda lithobekile, igobolondo licindezelwa libe yifomu elincane, elithambile, elalenzelwe ukuba lingene. Nakuba kunjalo, i-cage yakhala ngaphansi kwesisindo engakaze yakhelwe ukusithwala, kwadingeka ukuthi operator ku-winch house aqinisekiswe kabili ngaphambi kokukhulula ibhuleki.

Kwathi phansi.

Ukwehla kwakuzwakala njengokugwinya umhlaba kunokuwela. Izindonga zeshaft zashwabadela phambili, zikhanyiswa izikhathi ezithile nge-cage lamp—amatshe amanzi, ukukhanya kwesiteshi esingaphansi, inqwaba yamapayipi namakhebuli, kuphele. Bumnyama futhi. Khona kwavela ukukhanya futhi. Amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu kwalokhu, umoya ukhuphuka ngamamitha ayikhulu, ushisa, ucindezela, kwaze kwaba sengathi akusona isibhakabhaka sasekuseni esibandayo kodwa into enesisindo, into ecindezela kuye.

UArin wayese-right e-surface. I-surface yayinesibhakabhaka.

IShaft ayinayo isibhakabhaka.

Wazizwa kuqala ezandleni, iminwe iqala ukugoba. Umoya wakhe waba mfushane, i-panic ikhuphuka isuka esifubeni, ngendlela ebihlala injalo selokhu eyingane kwathi amadoda ase-lokshini aphakamisa okusele kukayise ephuma emgodini ofana nalo—ubumnyama obufanayo, nephunga elifanayo lamatshe amanzi, ne-diesel, nobanda obujulile obungakaze bufike ilanga.

Izindonga zivaliwe. Wayazi, engqondweni, ukuthi azihambi. IShaft imamitha amane ububanzi. Bekungaba lula ukulala uGuardian ngaphakathi. Izindonga azivali, namatshe aphezulu awazukuwela, ne-cage ayizukuma imvimbe lapha ebumnyameni amakhilomitha amabili ngaphansi kwendawo eyadla uyise—

Umoya wakhe washeshisa. Wethuka. Waba mfushane. I-visor yagcwala ifu elimhlophe.

Ama-maths ayiphutha. Inombolo ayiphutha. I-plan ayiphutha. Wake wehla eshafti kanye phambilini, yathatha uyise, kwathatha iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye akha umshini ukuze angaphinde ayenze futhi, kodwa usuyenzile, ngenhloso, engena endaweni eyodwa ayechitha impilo yakhe yonke ezama ukubalekela—

ICage yathuthumela. Yashelela. Ama-guides nje, leyo wear ayeseyilinganisile, kodwa umzimba wakhe awusazihluphi nge-measurement. Umzimba wakhe ukhumbula. Izindonga zicindezela. Amatshe aphezulu acindezela. Ubumnyama obuphansi buvuleka njengephimbo.

Amathani amane eGuardian suit. Isikhumba sakhe ngaphakathi. Amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu etsheni.

“Arin,” kusho uMama.

Izandla zakhe zazithuthumela. Uzamile ukuzibamba.

“Arin. Lalela izwi lami. Ungazami ukwehlisa ukuphefumula. Lokho akukaze kukusebenzele, siyazi sobabili. Ngitshele into eyodwa oyizwayo. Hhayi ephaketheni. Emzimbeni wakho.”

“Angikwazi—”

“Into eyodwa. Noma yini.”

“Izandla zami—” Uzinyanzelile. “Izandla zami. Zibanda.”

“Kuhle. Kuqiniso lokho. Izandla zakho zibanda ngoba awuzigqokanga amagilavu ngaphansi kwe-Assist futhi sizokhuluma ngalokho kamuva. Okunye.”

“I-collar.” Izwi lakhe laphuka lapho. “Ishaya entanyeni yami. Ishisa. I-chip ishisa.”

“Yilapho sikhona,” kwasho uMama. “Leyo mfudumalo yithi. Siseduze nawe. Phakathi kwamahlombe akho. Asiyi ndawo ongayi khona.” Kwathuleka kancane. “Okunye.”

Izindonga zisavaliwe, zicindezela. Kodwa useshilo izinto ezimbili, futhi zombili ziyiqiniso, ingxenye yengqondo yakhe elwela iqiniso yayibambelela kulokho njengesidlwengu esibambelela ocingweni.

“Iphansi liyazamazama,” wayekwazi ukusho. “I-cage. I-resonance ayilungile. Ama-guide agugile—” Wayeka. Waphefumula. “Agugile ohlangothini olusenyakatho. Kunokudlala okuncane. Cishe ama-millimeter amane. Yingakho izama.”

“Nanguya,” kusho uFool, efudumele njengelanga. “Simtholile. Ubefihlwe ngaphansi kokwesaba sonke les’khathi. Njalo nje.”

“Ungenzi njalo,” kusho uMama.

“Angihlekisi. Ngiyakhululeka. Kunomehluko, futhi ngivumelekile kokubili.” Izwi likaFool lehla, kwaba nguArin wedwa, ngendlela ayehlala eyenza ngayo uma esezophela. “Wake wehla emgodini onjena kudala. Waphuma uphuthunywa emgodini onjena. Ubuneminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye. Usuchithe iminyaka eyishumi nane wakhe umshini ukuze kungabi khona omunye engane eneminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye emile phezulu emgodini futhi. Manje usuwehla wena. Akuyona into embi leyo, uyazi. Kuwukwethembeka okukhulu engikubonile kumuntu. Bengicabanga ukuthi kufanele kube khona umuntu okushoyo lokho ngaphambi kokuba itshe likwenze.”

I-cage yashaya phansi komgodi ngomsindo owadlula ku-Assist wangena nasezinyo lika-Arin.

Ilambu laqina. Ukuzamazama kwaphela. Wayesephansi kwezwe, futhi umhlaba wawungakaweli kuye. Okungenani okwamanje.

“I-eleven-level station. I-repeater isemuva kwethu manje. Sise-internal.” Izwi lo-makhenikha lalinezinga elithile lokuthamba, okuyinto engavamile ku-Atlas. “Lapha, amamephu amadala, itshe lithukuthele. Ngine-seismic. Ngine-original survey kanye ne-rebolting records zama-eighties kanye naleyo nto oyithole ngo-3 ekuseni akekho omunye oyitholile. Anginasiqiniseko. Wazi lokho ngaphambi kokuqala. Ngizokutshela iqiniso noma iqiniso lithi *angazi.*”

“Lokho kuphela engake ngakucela kuwe,” kusho uArin.

Waphakamisa i-Assist kancane, egumbini eliphansi. I-station ibiwonakele—ama-rails aphukile, izinkuni zaphuka, itshe liwise ingxenye yomgwaqo obheke entshonalanga lapho kwawa khona. Uthuli lwawulenga emoyeni, luncane njengofulawa, lunganqumi ngoba umoya awuhambi. Kwakushisa. Kwakushisa ngempela manje, ukufudumala kwamakhilomitha amabili nengxenye edwaleni kugcina ukushisa kweplanethi kumzimba wakhe. I-sweat yaphuma emhlane ngaphakathi kwe-Assist, wezwa ama-fan e-cooling eqala, kwaba umsindo omnene edonsa ukushisa ku-chip kuqala. Okokuqala kukaMama. Njalo.

“Abasindile badlule lapho kwawa khona,” kusho yena. “Badlule lapho kwawa khona bese behla benyuka i-incline bangene egumbini lokucasha. Uma igumbi lisaphila.”

“Uma igumbi lisaphila,” kusho uAtlas. Elinganayo. Eqotho. “Futhi asazi.”

“Izimpawu zokushisa eziyisikhombisa emahoreni amane edlule,” kusho uLibrarian. “Ngibhale i-surface feed ngaphambi kokuyilahlekelwa. Isikhombisa, kwabangu-11 abebese-face ngesikhathi liwa. Bebebuthene. Ukubuthana kuhle—kuchaza ukuthi bebazihambela, bekhetha ukuba ndawonye. Izidumbu azibuthani. Abaphilayo bayabuthana.”

“Bangaphefumula isikhathi esingakanani amadoda ayisithupha phansi lapho?” kubuza uArin.

Kwathi cwaka. Wayekwazi ukufunda izikhawu ze-Court. Lena bekunguMama noAtlas bexoxa masinyane, ngaphansi kwamazwi.

“Nge-reserve ye-refuge chamber isaphila?” kusho uMama. “Amahora ayi-18. Uma ingasekho—uma igumbi lehlulekile futhi bahlala emoyeni ofile?” Akazange apholise—ukupholisa bekusho ukuthi uqamba amanga, futhi i-Court ayisamqambeli amanga. “Amathathu. Mhlawumbe amane. Asazi kuze singene ngaphakathi.”

“Pho asimi la,” kusho uWolf.

“Cha,” kusho uArin. “Asimi.”

Wasondela lapho kwawa khona.

---

*I-fall ibiyisodonga samatshe aphukile, amamitha amathathu, kusuka osayizi wesibhakela kuya kwesi-fridge, kugcwele futhi kuvalwe isisindo sako. Ku-Arin, ngaphansi kwelambu, bekuyisithiyo. Ku-Court, esikhathini esandisiwe esingaphansi kokuqonda kwakhe, bekuyinhlangano enomumo, bayiphendulela ndawonye ngendlela abenza ngayo konke: ngokuphikisana.*

ATLAS: *Ungaqali phansi. Phansi kuphethe isisindo. Uma uqala phansi uzoletha emuva phezu kwakhe.*

WOLF: *Pho sizobe sihamba kancane. Ukucotha kubiza laba abayisithupha umoya.*

ATLAS: *Ukucotha kubiza umoya wabo. Ukushesha kubiza umuntu ozobasiza, bese sebengenamoya* nabasizi. Phinda leyo math ngezinombolo ezifanele.*

JUDGE: *U-Atlas ulungile ngokomthetho. Umhlengi ofile akasizi muntu. Sihlanza phezulu, isiqeshana ngesiqu esikhulu, samukele isikhathi esizothatha.*

MERCURY: *Usezolahlekelwa wumoya futhi. I-heart rate yakhe inyukile. Ubuka usayizi walena into.*

MOTHER: *Pho makube khona omunye ongena phansi naye ngaphambi kokuba enze njalo.*

FOOL: *Ngizongena. Uyangilalela uma esaba ngoba ngiyedwa ongakwaziyo ukukhombisa ukwesaba.*

WOLF: *Wesaba wena.*

FOOL: *Kusobala. Kodwa ngiyakwazi ukuzibamba ngingathi angesabi, futhi yilokho umsebenzi wami. Atlas—ngicela lezi ziqeshana ezintathu eziphuma ngaphandle kokwehlisa uphahla. Ngizokwenza kube sengathi ngiyenza i-card trick.*

ATLAS: *Phezulu kwesokunxele. Bese i-wedge engaphezulu maphakathi. Bese elide ohlangothini lwesokudla, futhi elide lodwa, ungaphumi ngisho i-millimeter, noma i-north arch yonke iyawa bese siphetha inkulumo yethu masinyane.*

LIBRARIAN: *Ngiyabhala i-sequence. Uma lokhu kuyasebenza, kuyindlela. Singayidinga kabili ngaphambi kwe-refuge.*

JUDGE: *Pho yenzani ibe indlela. Yenzani iphindeke. Asizami inhlanhla lapha. Sakha umgwaqo.*

[END SLOW MODE]

---

“Phezulu kwesokunxele kuqala,” kusho uFool, ethokozisa, sengathi basemsebenzini futhi okubi kakhulu kungukuhluleka kwe-simulation. “I-block phezulu kwesokunxele, ibukeka ingenacala. Khipha. UAtlas uthe ayibambi lutho. UAtlas akakaze akuxoke mayelana nokuphatha isisindo ngoba uAtlas angeke akwazi ukuqamba amanga anjalo. Kuyinto yakhe leyo. Siyamthanda ngalokho.”

U-Arin welula isandla. I-Guardian’s hand yabamba i-block. Wazibamba wakhumula, ama-actuator e-Assist akhipha umsindo omncane, i-block yaphuma ngokukhala nokuthuli, kwangalandeli lutho.

“Uyakubona? I-card trick.” Izwi likaFool lahlala lithule. “I-wedge engaphezulu maphakathi ilandelayo. Njengoba kunjalo. Awuhambisi intaba, uxazulula itshe elilodwa ngesikhathi. Awukaze wehluleke ukuxazulula itshe.”

Wena, uyabona, uthathe i-wedge. Uthathe le ende, ekunene, angadluli nakancane. ICourt yasho ukuthi ilandela le sequence, wafaka khona, kwase kuqaqa amatshe njengentambo evuleka uma usuthole i-strand efanele—akuzange kwenzeke kanye, kodwa kancane kancane, ibhulokhi ngebhulokhi, kwaze kwaba nesikhala iGuardian egobile ingacosha ngaso.

Umoya wakhe wawusuphephile futhi phakathi kwalokho konke. Akazange aqaphele. Umsebenzi uwudle wonke umuzwa wokwesaba; umsebenzi njalo wenza kanjalo, selokhu engumfana ehleli etafuleni lomsebenzi. Uma nje kukhona itshe okufanele alixazulule, akukho ndawo yokumane kube mnyama.

Kodwa ngaphesheya kwesikhala, indlela yaba ncane. Yaba ncane kakhulu. Ilambu lakhanyisa isiphundu sedwala, cishe lincane kunesudi, i-hangingwall idilikile, izinsika zokhuni zigobile zingasakwazi ukumelana nomthwalo.

Wama. Umoya waphenduka mncane, ongalungile.

“Mama,” esho. “Angikwazi. Kuyashisa—kusondelene namaplates—”

“Ngiyazi.” Alikho itwetwe ezwini lakhe, iqiniso nje. “Zombili izingalo sezithintene nodonga. Kuyiqiniso lelo. Awuzihlanyisi. Kuvalekile.”

“Angikwazi ukuya khona. Angikwazi ukuphenduka lapho, angikwazi—”

“Arin.” Mercury, manje, isho ngokunqamulayo. “I-forearm yesobunxele. Ngaphansi kwe-Assist. Usunayo kusukela uneminyaka eyishumi nesithupha. Uyazi leyo.”

Umoya wakhe wama.

“Yisho,” kusho uMercury. “Ngokuzwakalayo. Uhlala usho ukuthi uyizobe ukuze ungadingi ukuzethemba ukuthi uyayikhumbula. Ngakho ungazethembi. Thembela ku-ink. Yisho.”

Amazwi okuqala aphume ephithizela, amadala, i-litany esandleni sakhe—umkhuleko wasehlane wokulwa nokwesaba awazobe esezimweni zakhe eneminyaka eyishumi nesikhombisa, awaphinda njalo emgodini ngamunye selokhu.

“Qhubeka.”

“Ukwesaba yilo elibulala ingqondo.” Izwi lakhe laqina. La mazwi amadala kunosizi lwakhe; uwaphathe kuyo yonke indawo emnyama yempilo yakhe, kancane kancane, isiqephu ngesinqephu.

Bese—

—wayizwa iphindwe kabili.

ICourt yathatha i-cadence ngemuva kwakhe. Akuzange kube amazwi, umrhythm—uMama, ephansi eqinisekile; uMercury egcina i-metre; bese ngaphansi kwabo uAtlas, yebo, uAtlas ongaqondakali, ofunda imigqa esandleni sakhe kwi-link wabe esenquma ukuthi into ewusizo angayinikeza manje ukuthi abambe i-beat yokuphefumula.

Wakhuluma isiqephu esilandelayo, nabo bangena naye, u-Arin wezwa ama-fan e-suit eqala ukuhambisana nomoya wakhe—waqaphela iCourt ingene ohlelweni lukaGuardian, yalinganisa ukuphefumula kwakhe ne-rhythm yokuphindaphinda, kwehla ama-fan, kwavumelaniswa nomsindo we-actuator, kwaze kwaba sengathi umshini wonke uphefumula naye, kwaze kwaba sengathi le ndawo ayesaba kakhulu iyindawo epefumula naye.

Wavumela ukwesaba kudlule kuye, kungene kuye, njengoba la mazwi amdala emfunda, kwathi sekuphelile waphenduka wayobuka lapho bekukhona khona.

Indlela ayizange yande, kodwa ayisabukeki njengesiphundu, yaqala ukuthatha umumo.

“*Futhi uma isidlulile, ngizobheka ngaphakathi ukuba ngibone indlela yayo.*”

“Nanguya,” kusho uFool, cishe engazwakali. “Uyabuya.”

“*Lapho ukwesaba sekudlule, akusasekho lutho.*” U-Arin wabeka isandla sakhe esinsikeni egobile. Wayizama. Wazizwa uAtlas emnika inombolo yomthwalo engakafiki. “*Ngiyoba ngedwa.*”

Wangena.

---

Indlela yathatha ama-meters angamashumi amane, kodwa kumzwakale sengathi ihora lonke.

Waqhubeka ngezinhlanu—iGuardian isesimweni se-quadruped, ama-plate ehuzuka edwaleni. Amadolo akhe, lawo angempela, asenqabile isikhathi eside, kodwa wabheka lokho njengokubuka i-gauge, waqhubeka. I-sweat yaqoqa emhlane wakhe, kwaba nokuluma entanyeni angenakuyifinyelela, angeke akwazi ukuyifinyelela amahora amaningi, futhi lobuncane balokho—ukuluma nje, kule ndawo—kwamsondeza ekuthini ahleke. Kabili, i-hangingwall yashintsha, kwakhala umsindo omkhulu, kwawa uthuli, kabili iCourt yathula, bonke belalele ngamandla abo onke, kabili kwaphumelela.

Kodwa okwesithathu, akuzange kuphumelele.

I-slab yehla ngemuva kwakhe, umsindo njengomhlaba uzikhwehlela, ivala indlela eya e-station level eleven. Uthuli lwagcwala phezulu, kwaba yimpunga yonke. Umzimba kaArin wama—

*uvaliwe, ungcwatyelwe, awekho amuva, awekho amuva, kwenzeka kanje kuye, kwenzeka kanje—*

—ama-piezo struts ku-Guardian’s spine avuka, ngoba i-slab imfake umthwalo, ngoba idwala elivale phambili lifake amandla alo emzimbeni we-suit, u-Arin wabona i-gauge yamandla ikhuphuka *phezulu* ku-visor, three percent, four, kwaphuma ukuhleka okungathi ukukhala.

“Pressure iba amandla,” asho ephansi. “Umshini omuhle wena.”

“Idizayini yakho,” kusho uAtlas. “Thina siyayisebenzisa.”

“Indlela ebuyela emuva seyiphelile, angithi.”

“Iyiphelile,” kusho uAtlas, eqinisile. “Uvumelekile ukusaba. Awuvumelekile ukumangala.”

Kwathula, u-Arin waphefumula phakathi kwalokho.

“Leyo into yesibili emnene owake wayisho kimi,” esho.

“Yini yokuqala?”

"Wathi ukushona kukababa akuzange kube yisimo sodonga. Kwaba yisinqumo somuntu ngemali. Idwala alizange limbulele. Kwakuwumuntu.”

“Kwabe kunguJudge,” kusho uAtlas.

“Kwabe kunguwe,” kusho uArin. “UJudge wavuma. Kodwa nguwe owabala i-load waphendula. Angizange ngibonge ngalokho.”

“Ubonga manje,” kusho uAtlas. “Ngokwehla la. Ngokwakha indlela hhayi ingcwaba. Lelo yibonga i-structural engineer ayifundayo.”

Indlela yehla yaya endaweni evulekile. Uthuli lwaqala ukuhlala, lukhononda. Phambili, ngaphansi kwelambu, u-Arin wabona into engesilo idwala—umnyango wensimbi, uphume kancane, kodwa usahleli, i-yellow-and-black ye-refuge chamber, phezu kwawo, kuqoshwe ngesandla, imishayo eyisikhombisa, eyodwa iwususo.

Umoya wakhe wama. Manje akusikho ukwesaba.

“Seven,” kusho uLibrarian kancane. “Seven marks. Omunye ususiwe. Bavuselele i-census. Befuna sazi ukuthi bangaki abasele.”

“Basaphila,” kusho uMama, okokuqala namuhla leli zwi leCourt laphuma lingekho ukuthula, lingekho control, kodwa iqiniso elingahlanjululwanga elalibabuyisele lana. “Arin. Basaphila.”

Wawela isigaba sokugcina. Isibambo somnyango sasiyinsimbi egobile, igcwele uthuli lwezigidi ezimbili nohhafu, kubanda ngisho neplate. Wabeka isandla sikaGuardian esikhulu esilimele phezu kwensimbi ebandayo yomnyango, kancane, njengokuthinta into ebucayi, kwavela izwi elithile ngapha, kancane kodwa livakala, lasho okuthile ngesiSesotho, kwaphendula elinye, kwase kuba nezwi eziningi phezulu, umsindo wabantu abebesebalile bangcwaba, bezwa kukhothama othulini.

Wema khona isikhashana, ephefumula. I-chip ifudumele phakathi kwamahlombe, bonke abayisikhombisa bathule, izwi ngapha komnyango wensimbi.

“Court,” esho. Izwi lakhe lahlala liqinile, ngokumangalisayo. “Ake sibakhumule.”

“Sik’phakathi,” baphendula, bonke. Izwi elilodwa, imiqondo eyisikhombisa.

"Kuze kube sekugcineni," kwasho uFool buthule, ngendlela ahlale engena ngayo ekugcineni. "Phansi la, phezulu laphaya. Kuze kube sekugcineni, badger. Vula umnyango manje."

Wawuvula umnyango.

Le ndawo endala yokudonsa ibiyithe nqo amamitha angaba ngamakhulu amabili, kwaze kwaphela ingathi isazama.

Arin wayezwile ushintsho ngaphambi kokuthi izibani zikuqinisekise. Izinyathelo zikaGuardian zashintsha, umsindo waphenduka flat, phansi kwathambekela kwesokunxele lapho itshe lashaywa khona kudala. Indawo yaciphiza, izinkuni zokuqala, ezabekwa amadoda ase aba yimpuphu kudala, zaze zamelela isikhathi eside kunalokho abakwa-engineer ababezicabanga. Wayengazithemba. Amalambu esudi akhe ahlukanisa ubumnyama kwaba yizikhala ezimbili, uthuli lwagcwala phakathi kwazo luma sengathi luyinto eqinile, njengokukhithika kweqhwa elingafiki phansi.

*Phansi phambili lishubile ngamamitha ayi-11,* kusho uAtlas. *Ipillar yakudala isusiwe kwesinxele. Isisindo sesesinqeni sokudla.*

Arin washintsha indlela amile ngayo. Guardian wayelungisa naye, imithwalo engaphezu kwamathani amane ye-titanium ne-ceramic isabalalisa isisindo emhlabathini ongcono, sengathi yinyawo yakhe elungisa embhoshongweni. Wayesejwayele ukungacabangi ngesudi njengento ayigqokayo; phakathi kwalawa mamitha angamakhulu amane adlule, kwase kuyindlela yokuma nje.

"Kuya kude kangakanani i-refuge bay?"

*Amamitha angama-310,* kuphendula uLibrarian. *Amapulani ka-1962 akhombisa i-cross-cut la ezoxhuma kwi-38 Level station. Kodwa amapulani awathembeki njalo.*

"Akukho lutho oluthembekile la phansi," kwasho uArin ephendula kancane.

*Yebo, iyona vibe leyo,* kwasho uFool. *Yehlisa umgomo wakho uzogcina ungadumali. Mina njalo ngenza njalo uma ngikhuluma noJudge.*

*Angikuzwa mina,* kwasho uJudge.

*Usho kanjalo, kodwa ngiyazi.*

I-cross-cut ibikhona ngempela lapho uLibrarian ayikhombisile, bonke bamangala, ikakhulu uArin. Wayiphendukisa uGuardian, igobolondo lashayisa odongeni zombili, umsindo omkhulu we-ceramic uqhamuka esakeni wangena nasemazinyo akhe. Indawo yavuleka, i-cross-cut yaguquka yaba yigumbi elikhulu lapho kwakulayishwa khona ama-ore cars, amalambu akhe athola umnyango wensimbi we-refuge bay odongeni olude.

Ubebhidlika. Uhlaka lwamukele umthwalo lusuka phezulu lwathambeka ngaphakathi, umnyango wavaleka ngendlela engafanele. Phezulu, kwavuleka isikhala esingangengalo, kwagcwala ubumnyama.

Arin wama.

Kwathuleka eNkantolo, umsindo kwaba ukuphefumula kwakhe kuphela, kuzwakala kakhulu ngaphakathi kwe-helmet.

Kwakhona, kwavela izwi esikhala. Lincane. Libuthaka, lishaywe uthuli.

"Kukhona umuntu? Is someone there?"

Isifuba sikaArin saqina. Wayihambisa kancane iGuardian phambili, wehlisa amalambu ukuze angakhanyisi umuntu oseceleni komnyango.

"Yebo," wathi, izwi lakhe linqamukile. "Ngikhona. I'm here."

---

Ngaphakathi e-refuge bay, uMandla Buthelezi wayeseqede amathemba ngezwi cishe amahora amathathu adlule.

Wake wazizwa ekuqaleni: itshe likhuluma, imisindo emide yentaba izilungiselela isisindo sayo, kanye, ngokusobala, indoda imemeza igama lakhe, okwakungeke kwenzeke ngoba leyo ndoda yafa phezulu ngesikhathi sokuqala, uMandla ebuka kwenzeka. Ngakho lapho kukhanya phansi komnyango, kukhanya okumhlophe, okuqondile, kungacwayizi njengelambu eliphela amandla, akazange ahambe. Wahlala ekhwele uPetros, ononyawo oluphukile, osewuyekile nokukhononda, okuyinto embi kakhulu kunokukhononda uqobo.

Kwathi lapho ukukhanya kukhuluma isiZulu ngezwi lomfana, uMandla waqala ukukhala buthule, ngoba abafileyo abakhulumi isiZulu kabi ngelokishi.

"Six," wamemeza ephendula. "Six. We came in seven." Uzwa ephinda, engakwazi ukuyeka. "One—his chest was bad, the fall opened it. The air went bad and he—he went to sleep. Couldn't wake him. Six now. Petros, his leg. Thulani is—" Wama. "Air's bad. We've been—" Ukubala kwakunzima. "Long. We've been long."

Ukukhanya kwashintsha. Umnyango waqala ukuhamba.

Awuzange udonswe. Awuzange uvulwe ngamandla. Insimbi yamemeza, yaguqa, uhlaka oluphukile lwavuma ngesililo sensimbi eqhamukayo, kwavuleka isikhala, uMandla wabona into ephethe ukukhanya. Igcwalise i-cross-cut. Ibimnyama njengokushiswa kwendwangu endala, igobile njengenyamazane, izandla—uma bekuyizandla—zivalelwe emnyango zawuvula njengomuntu ovula ithini.

UPetros, eduze kwakhe, wakhuleka ngendlela angazange ayisebenzise selokhu wayingane.

"Yikho, asikho isimo," kwasho izwi. Lomuntu. Izwi lendoda, liphuma ngaphakathi kumshini. "I-rescue suit le. Umuntu uphakathi."

"Umuntu," kwaphinda uMandla.

"Kancane," kwavela elinye izwi, elincane, ngaphakathi kumshini, kwenza elokuqala lithi, "Not now," ngelokhu okuzwakala njengomuntu exosha inja ebhedini, futhi leyo ntukuthelo encane eyayijwayelekile yamenza uMandla wakholwa.

---

I-refuge bay ibidepth amamitha amane, igebhuzelwe etsheni, yakhelwe ukubamba abantu besaphila amahora angama-36 ngemuva komnyango ovaliwe one-air yakhona. Basebekhona isikhathi eside. I-compressed-air line yaphuka ngesikhathi sesibili. Amadoda abephefumula umoya osuphelile, i-cartridge ye-CO₂ scrubber yaphela. Umoya unuka umphefumulo nokwesaba, nalelo phunga elimnandi elisho ukuthi i-carbon dioxide seyikhuphuka.

UArin wakubuka konke masinyane, ngendlela abuka ngayo i-circuit board: konke kanye, bese ehlola okuphuthumayo ngakunye.

*Atmosphere kuqala,* kusho uMother, izwi lakhe seliqinile, njengokusebenza, imfudumalo seyisusiwe ukuze kube nendawo yomsebenzi. *CO₂ four point one and climbing. Oxygen eighteen point three. Banesikhathi. Kancane. Vula ikamelo ku-haulage. Umoya wethu ungcono kunalena.*

"Umoya we-haulage ugcwele uthuli," kuphendula uArin.

*Uthuli bangaphila nalo. Loku ngeke. Vula.*

Wagoba uGuardian wawuvula ngokuphelele umnyango. Umoya wahamba. Wakuzwa kuma-sensor ukuthi umoya omubi uphuma, omubi nje engena. Omunye wamadoda wakhwehlela, wangakwazi ukuyeka.

UArin waguqa, ehlisa i-helmet ezingeni labo. Ubuso abayisithupha phambi kwe-headlamp. Bangcolile. Imilomo iqhekekile. Amehlo abesehlale ebumnyameni isikhathi eside, ukukhanya kubalimaza. Omunye unonyawo oluqinwe ngebhodi ne-wire. Omunye wenze umsebenzi omuhle wokuhlinza ngezinto ezincane. Osemncane, ehlale phansi, ephefumula ngendlela uArin angayithandi, kancane futhi ngokushesha.

"Thulani," kusho uMandla, ebona ukuthi uArin ubheke bani. "Isifuba sakhe. Into yamehlelayo ngesikhathi sokuqala—"

*Tension pneumothorax, probable,* kusho uMother. *Noma ama-ribs aphukile. Atlas, ngidinga ukubona isifuba.*

UAtlas wavula i-suit imaging, i-thermal-and-structure overlay yavele emehlweni kaArin. Isifuba sokunxele somfana sishona ngaphakathi lapho kufanele siphume.

*Flail. Ama-ribs amathathu aphukile,* kusho uAtlas. *Uyazama ukuphefumula. Ngeke aphume apha ezingeni elinjena.*

"Senzeni?"

*Bopha isifuba. Sebenzisa amabhande. Asiyona odokotela, asikho esibhedlela,* kusho uMother. *Kodwake sithenga amahora ambalwa. Arin. Uzokwenza ngezandla zakho. EzikaGuardian zincane kakhulu.*

Lokho kumfake isdindi. Ukuze asebenzise izandla zakhe, bekumele aphule isivalo. Akhiphe ingalo yakhe yangakwesokudla ephuma ku-gauntlet yesudi, ayifake othulini nasemoyeni omubi, eveziwe. Leyo mbono ifike nesisindo esidala, isivalo sivaleka, leyo ngxenye yakhe eyechithe impilo yonke ibala ukuthi kuphunywa kuphi.

*Ngiyazi,* uMother athule, ekhuluma naye yedwa. *Yenza nje. Imizuzu emithathu. Phefumula ngomlomo.*

Wakhulula ingalo yakhe.

Umoya washaya isikhumba sakhe—ushisa, unothuli, awulungile. Isandla sakhe sabukeka simangazile kuye, simhlophe, sincane, siyisilwane ngaphandle kwensimbi ebivikela. Wadlula kuso, wathatha iphakethe le-field dressing uMother amkhombisele lona eceleni kukaGuardian, ngaphansi kwemiyalelo yakhe—faka lapha, *cha, phansi, phezu kwale ngxenye ehambayo, yebo*—wabopha isifuba somfana, iminwe yakhe ingavikelwe isebenza ebumnyameni kuyilapho amadoda ayisithupha ebuka, nomshini uphefumula eduze kwakhe.

Ukuphefumula kukaThulani kwaba lula. Akukuhle. Kodwa akubi kakhulu.

"Nangu," kwasho uArin. Wabuyisa ingalo yakhe ku-gauntlet, isivalo sivaleka, futhi wayengazi ukuthi wayekufuna kangaka kuze kube manje. "Nangu."

*Inhliziyo yehlile ngo-12,* kwasho uMother. *Kuhle. Manje sihambe.*

---

Izokhuluma phezulu bezithule selokhu kwenzeka i-cross-cut.

UArin akashongo lutho ngakho. NeCourt ayishongo. Izwi likaPriya laphola, laqhekeka, lehla lapho e-pinchi, base beqhubeka ngaphandle kwakhe ngoba kwakungekho okunye abangakwenza. Kodwa uGuardian wayephethe i-repeater spool, amamitha angamakhulu amabili e-fiber ku-drum emhlane wakhe, kanti uAtlas wayeyikhipha yonke indlela behla, intambo ibuyela ekukhanyeni.

*Sekuphelile i-spool,* kusho uAtlas. *Ngingaxhumanisa i-refuge bay hardline endala ne-repeater. I-bay yayineline yayo eqondile ye-comms ku-38 Level station. Uma kukhona okusindile—*

"Yenza."

Kwathatha imizuzu emine. UArin wabamba i-panel cover odongeni lwe-bay kuyilapho iminwe kaGuardian, emikhulu kakhulu ukusika kodwa imnene ngokwanele lokhu, iphendulela ithusi elingavikelwe kwi-repeater fiber-to-copper bridge. UAtlas wamkhombisa, eqinile engena manzi, noArin wazizwa eqiniseka.

Bese, ku-helmet yakhe, kwavakala ukuxokozela. I-carrier tone. Izwi, liqhekekile, lizama ukuzakha lisuka ku-static libe umuntu.

"—phinda, la phezulu, uPriya lo, *Arin*, uma ungizwa—"

"Ngiyakuzwa." Izwi lakhe laphuka kancane. Wayengazi ukuthi uzizwa ehlukene kangaka kuze kube manje. "Priya. Ngibanalo. Bonke abayisithupha. Bayaphila."

Kwahle cwaka. Bese kwavela umsindo angazi ukuthi uyini, into efana nokubanjwa yisililo, waqonda ukuthi uPriya uzama ukungakhali ku-channel evulekile.

"Phinda." Umoya. "Phinda futhi. Kancane. For the log."

"Six survivors at the 38 Level refuge bay. All six alive. One critical. Chest trauma, stabilized. One fractured leg. The rest dehydrated, CO₂-loaded, ambulatory. Tell them. Tell the surface."

Wezwa emtshela, izwi lakhe liphambuka kwi-mic, kwase kuba khona into ayengakayizwa izinsuku eziyishumi nanye, kusukela phambilini, hhayi isikhathi eside ngaphambi kwalokho.

Wezwa abantu bejabula.

Kwafika phansi nge-fiber, kuzwakala kuncane, okwenziwe imilomo eyikhulu, amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu phezulu, ekuseni, eduze kwe-headgear—imindeni nama-crews namadoda abetshelwe ukuthi lokhu sekuyisikhumbuzo, akusosizo—kwaze kwamfika ebumnyameni, emoyeni ofile, phakathi kwamadoda ayisithupha asebewukhohliwe ukuzwa amazwi, kwathi uMandla Buthelezi wabuka lo mshini ongumuntu wathi, "Iyini leyo? Iyini leyo msindo?"

"Yilapho phezulu," kusho uArin. "Sebazi ukuthi niyaphila."

Amadoda awazange ajabule. Akasenamandla okwejabula. Kodwa uPetros, onezinyawo eziphukile osewuyekile ukukhononda, wabeka ikhanda lakhe edwaleni wahleka, ukuhleka okomile, okuqhekekile, futhi ukuhleka kwahamba egumbini usuka komunye kuya komunye baze bonke bayisithupha behleka, kwathi uArin wahlala ngaphakathi kukaGuardian phakathi kwabo engahleki, ngoba ubebusy, kodwa into ethile enhliziyweni yakhe yakhulula yahlala isikhathi eside icindezelekile.

*Yijabulele,* kwasho uFool kancane, kuye yedwa. *Bese siyasebenza. Intaba ayikazizwa izindaba ezinhle. Iseyi-intaba.*

---

Ukuphuma kwaba yinkinga.

UArin wavula i-route overlay: indlela yokwehla ebuyela ku-cross-cut, i-haulage yakudala, phansi egobile, i-81 Raise neziphithiphithi zayo, ukukhuphuka isikhathi eside uye ku-30 Level necage kanye nephezulu. Wehle yedwa, isudi isonga ifake izindawo ezincane. Akakwazi ukubuyela ngendlela afike ngayo. Manje unamadoda ayisithupha. Omunye akakwazi ukuhamba. Omunye ucishe angaphefumuli.

*Indlela yokwehla ayisebenzi kubalimele abadinga i-stretcher,* kusho uAtlas. *I-pinch ku-81 Raise ngeke idlule i-litter. Futhi asikwazi ukuthwala abayisithupha nganoma iyiphi indlela. I-shell ithwala oyedwa.*

"Manje asibuyi ngendlela engize ngayo."

*Kulungile,* kusho uLibrarian. *Futhi kukhona enye indlela. I-38 Level station ixhuma kwi-main 38 Level haulage, ama-plans ka-1962 akhombisa idlula empumalanga iya ku-No. 4 Shaft station. No. 4 yavalwa kuma-eighties, kodwa i-shaft uqobo—*

*—iyona uVenter abesebenza kuyona phezulu,* kusho uAtlas. *Akabheki i-cage esehle ngayo. Ubheka i-No. 4. Uyitholile ngaphambi kwethu.*

UArin wathula. Phezulu, egumbini lenkhumbulo yakhe, wabona ubuso lomdala: i-beard empunga, isikhumba esishiswe ilanga, indoda uArin achithe izinsuku ezintathu ezama ukungamethembe ngenxa yesikhumba nendlela akhuluma ngayo, iminyaka ayisebenze ngayo. Indoda efunde ama-plans ka-1962 yodwa, ebusuku, engamenyiwe, yathola indlela kuphela yokuphuma uArin engakafiki phansi.

"Usephambili kunathi," kusho uArin.

*Yebo,* kusho uWolf.

Kwaba okokuqala uWolf akhuluma ngakho ngehora, yonke iCourt yathula.

*Uyazi le ndawo. Thina asiyazi. Sinamaplani. Yena unenkumbulo. Yilokho okubulala abantu la phansi. Simdinga.*

---

Amamitha angamakhulu amathathu empumalanga, i-38 Level haulage ibingcono kunanoma iyiphi indawo uArin adlule kuyo, kwenza angayithemba. Uthwele uThulani ezandleni zikaGuardian, umfana eboshwe ebhodini, ibhodi lincike esifubeni se-shell lapho isakhiwo sesudi sizothatha noma imuphi umthelela ngaphambi kokuthi umonakalo ufinyelele. Abanye abahlanu behamba ngemuva, izandla phezu kwamahlombe omunye nomunye, uchungechunge othulini, uMandla esemumva, uPetros egxuma nabantu ababili ngaphansi kwamakhwapha ngoba bekungekho abanye ababili abangamthwala futhi uGuardian ugcwele.

Bese ama-headlamp athola i-No. 4 station, ne-shaft.

Kwakuyisiphongo esimnyama edwaleni, i-cage endala yanyamalala kudala, i-headgear amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu phezulu. Futhi phakathi nendawo, ihamba kancane, ikhanyiswe phezulu iphuzu elimhlophe, kwakukhona i-steel platform ilenga.

I-flatbed. Akusiyo i-cage. I-flatbed deck, ex-mining, leyo esetshenziswa ukwehlisa imishini esindayo, iboshelwe ezintanjeni ezine ne-kibble winch, yehla isuka ekukhanyeni iye kubona nomuntu emi phezu kwayo.

Lowomuntu wagqoke i-hard hat ne-harness eboshelwe ku-cable emaphakathi, emi ngesimo esizolile somuntu oseke wagibela lezi izinto izikhathi eziyinkulungwane, ongazithokozeli kodwa osewuyekile ukwesaba kudala.

I-comms kaArin yaclicka. Izwi elihlukile manje, eduze, ku-local band. Kancane. Qondile.

“Ndlela.” Venter. Jakobus Venter. “Uzibambile.”

“Bonke abayisithupha,” kusho u-Arin.

“Ja.” Ukuthula. I-flatbed yathinta phansi kwe-station nge-clang ethambile, kwathi indoda endala yakhulula ama-clip yaphuma, yabheka phezulu kancane ku-Guardian, yabe isingasayibheki futhi, ngendlela umuntu osebenze nemishini emikhulu impilo yakhe yonke ayibheka ngayo: uyilinganisela, bese uyayishiya. “I-thermal ibihlala steady ngesikhathi uphahla luqala ukukhanya. Ngacabanga ukuthi usubatholile.”

Wadlula ku-Arin waya kuma-casualties. Waguqa eceleni kuka-Thulani engaceli muntu, izandla zakhe ezinkulu ezinamabala elanga zidlula kuma-strapping, ehlole, wanikina ikhanda kanye.

“Kuhle. Okwenzile lo msebenzi uyazi.”

“Lowo bekuwuMother,” kusho u-Arin, wabe esezwa ukuthi uzishayile. “Uhlelo. SAGE.”

Amehlo ka-Venter akhuphukela ku-helmet. Wabheka umzuzwana.

“Ja,” waphinda, akazange abuze, u-Arin waqonda ukuthi ukungabuzwa kwakuyinhlonipho.

---

Inkinga nge-flatbed kwakuyiyona nkinga yonke lapha phansi: ayizange yakhelwe lokhu.

*I-deck ibhalwe u-2 tonnes,* kusho u-Atlas, efunda i-plate u-Venter ayikhombile engakaceli muntu. *Siyizine tonnes ngaphambi kwama-casualties. Faka abantu abayisithupha, faka i-board. Asikagcini, kodwa sesisondele kabili, futhi le plate iyama-fake polite nje.*

“Ngabe angiyigibeli,” kusho u-Arin. “Ngakhwela. I-suit ingakwazi ukunyuka udonga lwe-shaft.”

“Cha,” kusho u-Venter. Flat. Igama lokuqala elinzima aphume nalo. Wayegobile phezu kwe-winch motor phezulu kwe-station drift, lapho intambo ikhuphuka khona emnyameni, manje wema, kwathi ekhuluma i-English yakhe yanyamalala, kwakhuphuka i-Afrikaans ngendlela ulimi lwakho lwendabuko lukhuphuka ngayo uma izinto sezimqoka futhi ungafuni ukungaqondani. “Jy klim nie. Awukhwele. Udonga luphukile eighty metres phezulu, ama-timbering aphelile. Uma le machine yakho uyibeka phezulu, izoyiwisa phansi ne-deck, la madoda esekhona. Nee.”

U-Arin wayeselungiselela ukuphikisa. Wayenayo i-model ye-shaft wall. Wayenama-load tables. Wayene—

*Uqinisile,* kusho u-Atlas, u-Arin engakakhulumi. *Ngino-geology. Ufunda off the rock face nama-spalling pattern. Mina bengingadinga ukukuskena. Yena uyazi nje. Udonga olungaphezulu kwe-station dead rock nje.*

*Lalela lo badger omdala,* kusho u-Fool kancane. *Usephansi la isikhathi eside kunesikhathi i-simulation yakho ibe khona.*

Ngakho u-Arin wathi, “Kwenze kanjani, manje?”

Futhi u-Venter wamtshela.

Ama-trip amabili. Abalimele nabane abahamba phambili, be-deck, u-Venter egibele ukuphatha intambo ne-winch ezofuna ukuhamba yodwa uma inemithwalo. Bese i-deck ibuyela phansi, ingenalutho. Bese u-Guardian yedwa, umuntu wokugcina ephuma. Futhi leyo trip, kusho u-Venter, ebheka ngephutha i-suit enkulu, kuzomele bayixazulule ngendlela ehlukile, ngoba ama-four tonnes e-machine akuyona into i-cage eyakhelwe ukuyiphakamisa ngentambo eyodwa.

“I-winch yinkinga,” kusho u-Venter. Wayesekhiphe i-cover ye-motor housing, u-Arin akwazi ukubona, noma ekukhanyeni kwe-headlamp, ukuthi kukhona okungahambi kahle: i-brake band, ishile, ingxenye ye-lining yayo iphelile. “I-brake isishile. Siyiphuthume phansi ukuyilethela wena. Ishisa. Uma ishisa kakhulu, iyahluleka. I-deck iwela phansi namadoda. Uma sihla, mina ngizoyibamba ngesandla. Uma sikhuphuka, i-lift i-electric, i-brake i-automatic, bese leyo automatic ayisekho.”

U-Arin wabheka i-motor. Wakhumbula ubuntwana bakhe nezinye izinsimbi ezindala, ezibhuqiwe, kulo khanya elinjena. Wabona konke ngesikhathi esisodwa, wabe esebona nokuhluleka.

“Udinga i-load brake i-winch motor engekho nayo,” esho. “Okuthile ezobamba i-deck uma i-band iyeka. Uhlelo lwesibili.”

“Ja.” U-Venter wambheka. Wabheka u-Guardian. “Futhi nginalo. Ume phambi kwami, hey. Le machine ibamba intambo. Uma i-band iphuma, i-suit yakho ibamba umthwalo, i-deck ayiwe. Uwumshini wami we-dead-man brake.”

Kwaba yikho, u-Arin wabona, kahle impela. Yilokho ayengakha uma enezinsuku ezintathu ne-workshop. Indoda endala ikuqambe ngemizuzwana eyishumi kulokho okumi phambi kwayo.

“Atlas,” kusho u-Arin. “Singakwazi ukubamba ama-two tonnes static entanjeni nge-guide?”

*Nge-cross-cut frame njenge-anchor point, intambo idlula ku-sheave endala—yebo,* kusho u-Atlas. *Imizuzwana engama-90 ye-full static load ngaphambi kokuba i-actuator coolant idlule umkhawulo. Ngemuva kwalokho kumele siyeke umthwalo noma siqothule ingalo yangakwesokudla.*

*Imizuzwana engama-90 yilona lift time,* kusho u-Librarian. *Ama-240 metres nge-rate ephephile. Imizuzwana engama-88.*

“Imizuzwana emibili yokuphephisa,” kusho u-Arin.

*Siyakwamukela ku-mine rescue,* kusho u-Fool. *Imizuzwana emibili iyinhlanhla. Ngokugcina besine-negative four, kwasebenza.*

“Lokho bekuyi-simulation.”

*Bengiyazi? Kunzima ukusho lapha phansi. Ukukhanya kufana nje.*

Kwaphuma ukuhleka okuncane kuye engakakulindeli. Kwamethusa. Waphendukela ku-Venter, owayembuka ekhuluma no-muntu ongabonakali, ngokubekezela komuntu osevele ethatha ukuthi izinto ezimangazayo azimkhathazi.

“Izobamba,” kusho u-Arin. “Ngizoyibamba.”

U-Venter wambheka isikhathi eside. Ubuso obudabukile bashintsha, umhlathi waqhuba kanye. U-Arin wayengakwazi ukufunda ubuso kahle, akakaze akwazi, kodwa wayesekwazi ukufunda izikhathi eziphakathi kwabo, futhi lesi bekuyisikhathi esisinda. Le ndoda endala isichithe iminyaka, u-Arin azofunda kamuva, ithwele ukuthula ngama-safety equipment angazange abe khona, ama-brake adelayiweyo, ama-cuts agunyazwe amadoda angeke agibele i-cage. Wayengcwabe leyo kuthula kwaze kwaba sekwakha i-career yesibili njengomeluleki, futhi le consulting kwakuyindlela yokuthula, umbono ongena-muntu ophoqelekile ukuwulandela.

Manje sekukhona umshini oklanywe umfana omusha ubaba wakhe odliwe yintaba efana nale, ecelwa indoda endala ukuthi ithembele kuwo njenge-brake ngesikhathi amadoda ayisithupha ekhuphuka ephuma ebumnyameni.

“Uyazi ukuthi le mine ingeyabani,” kusho u-Venter. “Le company. Irekhodi labo.”

“Ngiyazi,” kusho u-Arin.

“Ngangiyinsizwa emayini enje. Ngasayina izinto.” I-Afrikaans yakhuphuka kanzima manje, indoda endala ivumela. “Izinto ebengimele ngiyimele. Ngazitshela ukuthi nginqotshiwe. Ngabe nginqotshiwe. Kodwa ngasayina nje.” Wabheka phezulu ku-shaft emnyama ebheke ekukhanyeni okumhlophe okude. “Angikwazi ukukubuyisa lokho. Akukho—akukho indlela yokwehla ukuyokubuyisa.”

“Cha,” kusho u-Arin. “Ayikho.”

Indoda endala yathuthumela kancane, kwathi, okungajwayelekile, yakhululeka.

“Ja,” kusho u-Venter. “Yebo.” Waqinisa ama-gloves akhe. “Masibakhiphe, labo esisakwazi ukubakhipha.”

---

Bafaka i-deck.

U-Thulani kuqala, i-board iboshelwe ku-deck rail, uMother ebheka i-chest yakhe ngama-sensors ka-Guardian kuze kube umzuzu wokugcina lapho sebengasakwazi. Kwalandela uPetros, kwalandela laba bane abahamba, becindezelelene, izandla zibambe i-rail, ubuso bubheke phezulu ekukhanyeni sengathi bangakhuphuka ngentshiseko nje. U-Mandla Buthelezi wakhuphuka nale batch yokuqala ngoba u-Venter wambona engumuntu onokuthula, ozobamba nabanye, kwathi efika ku-deck rail uMandla waphenduka wabheka umshini osemnyama, lowo ozogibela ekugcineni.

“Wena,” esho. *You.* “Ubani igama lakho? Umuntu. Hhayi umshini.”

“Arin.”

“Arin.” UMandla wanikina ikhanda kancane. “Sasizwa sengathi abafileyo bayakhuluma, la phansi. Sasicabanga ukuthi sesiphelile.” Wabamba i-rail. “Ngiyabonga. Uze lapho bathi akukho ndlela.”

“Kwakukhona indlela,” kusho u-Arin. “Akekho obheke kahle.”

UVenter wacofa i-harness yakhe phakathi nendawo yentambo. Wabamba ngesandla esisodwa i-lever ye-band brake, le brake eshisiwe ayeyizizwa ngomzimba wonke, yonke indlela phezulu, amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu wentambo, indololwane yakhe ishaywe umlilo, ngesinye isandla wacindezela i-winch, i-deck yakhuphuka, iyashwibilika, iphakamisela amadoda ayisithupha phezulu, iphume emoyeni omubi nasekukhanyeni okufile, ibheke phezulu ekukhanyeni, emhlalweni, nasekuphumelelweni kokusa.

U-Arin wayibuka ikhuphuka. Wahambisa u-Guardian endaweni yokubopha, wafaka intambo ku-sheave endala, wathatha umthwalo ngengalo yesokudla ye-suit, uma i-brake ingahle idedele, wama lapho, ingxenye engcono kakhulu yamathani amane ensimbi nomuntu ngaphakathi, ebambe intambo ebamba amadoda, i-dead-man brake u-badger omdala ayeyakhe ngaye ngemizuzwana eyishumi.

I-band yama. I-deck yakhuphuka. Ayibamdinganga.

*I-heat budget ye-arm isaphila,* kusho u-Atlas. *Akukho load transfer edingekayo. Uhambe kahle nge-brake.*

“Kulungile,” kusho u-Arin.

*Lena ingxenye obungayenza wedwa,* kusho u-Fool, nje, ethule ngesikhathi bekhuphuka isikhathi eside. *Ubambe intambo. Wakha i-anchor. Kodwa indlela yokuphuma — udonga olufile, i-deck eqinisekisiwe, i-No. 4 Shaft — ubuzongena kulawo ma-plank abolile, uwise phezu kwakho, amadoda ayisithupha asale phansi emgodini, nomzimba wesoka egqoke i-suit ebizayo. Uyazi, ne?*

U-Arin wayibuka i-deck incipha ibheke ekukhanyeni.

“Yebo,” esho.

*Ngiyazama nje. Kunzima ukwazi ukuthi yini engena kuwe. Uvala izinto zakho ebhokisini.*

*Muyeke,* kusho u-Mother.

*Cha,* kusho u-Arin. “Uqinisile.” Walalela i-motor ye-winch icindezela umthwalo, umsindo onzima, onempilo, womshini owenza umsebenzi wawo, ugibelwe ngumuntu owaziyo. “Bengizofa lapha, ngiqinisile. Uqinisile ngokushesha.”
