*Lokhu okokuqala,* kusho u-Judge, eqinile ngendlela yakhe uma ebheka izinto ezibalulekile, *ngizwa usho ukuthi omunye umuntu uqinisile ngokushesha kunaye.*

*Bhala isikhathi,* kusho u-Fool. *Librarian. Faka ku-record unomphela. Amamitha ayizinkulungwane ezimbili phansi komhlaba, umoya ofile, umfana uyavuma ukuthi indoda ayeyingethembi yazi indlela yokuphuma ngaphambi kwakhe.*

*Kubhalisiwe,* kusho u-Librarian. Wabuye wehla kancane: *Kubhalisiwe.*

I-deck yafika ekukhanyeni. Phezulu kakhulu, incane, u-Arin wayibona ithinta phezulu, wabona abantu begijima beyihlangabeza, wabona izithunzi eziyisithupha zimuncwa ekukhanyeni, umhlalelo wehlela phansi futhi, mkhulu manje, nezwi likaVenter laqhuma ku-helmet yakhe, kancane, lomile, liphakamisa injabulo engafihlekile.

“Six on top. Alive. Bringing the deck back for you, Ndlela.” Wathula kancane. “Then we get you out of my mine.”

*Yimayini yakhe,* kusho u-Fool. *Ngiyamthanda lo mfana.*

U-Arin wema ebumnyameni phansi kwe-shaft, ebambe intambo esingasayidingi ukubanjwa. I-deck yaqala ukwehla kancane ibheke kuye, iyashwibilika, ikhanya phezulu.

Ngaphakathi kwe-helmet, umoya usuphelelwe, ushisa, ugcwele ukuphefumula kwakhe, kanti ku-Guardian, engalweni yangakwesokudla, kukhona i-scuff ende ekhanyayo lapho i-ceramic ishayisane nodonga.

Akenzanga yedwa.

*Atlas,* esho. “Amandla nokupholisa. Ngiphe inombolo eqotho yokukhuphuka. Hhayi leyo enamathemba.”

Kwaba nokuthula. Okufushane. Kodwa u-Arin wayeseyichithe amahora amaningi ngaphakathi kulo mshini, wazi ukuthula kwawo ngendlela uVenter azi ngayo itshe, leli thule linezinto ngaphakathi.

*Inombolo eqotho,* kusho u-Atlas, *kufanele sixoxe ngayo uma i-deck ifika.*

Ukukhanya okumhlophe kwehla ebumnyameni kuye, u-Arin wayibuka, walinda.

Izibani ze-flatbed zashona zanyamalala ekuphakameni kwe-81 Raise, abasalayo abayisithupha bephithizela ebhokisini likaVenter, beshiya ithunela lingenalutho ngaphandle kuka-Arin, isisindo sesudi, nomfudlana wakudala odalwe idwala eminyakeni eyizinkulungwane.

“Sebedlule ekucindeni,” kusho uPriya, izwi lakhe libuthakathaka kuma-comms. “Petros uthi i-brake isabambe.”

“Ungayithinti, ngaphandle uma sekubheke phansi.” U-Arin wahamba, i-Guardian frame ithatha ukuhamba kwayo kwezinja ngaphandle kokucabanga, amaplate elala emhlane wakhe njengoba egoba eqonda ekukhuphukeni. I-refuge bay yashiyeka emuva kwakhe. Phambili, i-traverse ayeyehle ngayo amahora amabili edlule imlindile ukuthi ayibhidlize. Manje sekukhuphuka, i-gravity ayisekho umngane.

Indlela endala yomoya yamudlulisela endaweni ayeyibonile ebuya phansi kodwa engenaso isikhathi sokucabanga ngayo, futhi namanje akanaso. Mhlawumbe imitha eyisishiyagalolunye, idwala laphenduka. I-survey ka-’88 yathi kuyizingqimba ze-quartzite ezijwayelekile, zihlutshiwe ngesandla zashiywa, futhi emaphethelweni ale mitha eyisishiyagalolunye, iqiniso laliyikho kanye lelo—ubuso obudliwe amadoda ane-drill. Kodwa phakathi nendawo, udonga lwaba *bushelelezi*. Hhayi bushelelezi bokuhlanza, hhayi i-shotcrete; ama-sensor esudi amlethela khona esandleni njengoba ezinzisa i-frame kuyo, kanti into azizwa ngamathambo kwakuyi-*lutho*—akukho ladder ye-chisel, akukho blast rind, akukho thuluzi eliklolodayo. Kwakuwumgodi odidiyelwe edwaleni onobuso obungaqondakali, obubanda futhi obujikayo ngendlela engenziwa yimishini inkampani ekade yehlisa eshaftini. Into eyayikwenza ayikaze isike idwala. Yayivele *iyikhulume*. Wayikhipha lapho ekhipha khona izinto ezingafanelani nezinto ezingakhokhwa: inothi eliyisicaba emqolo, *buka futhi uma kungasekho amadoda ayisithupha afe*. Futhi akazange aphinde ayikhumbule. Waqhubeka, kwashintsha kwaba idwala langempela, wayikhohlwa iminyaka eyishumi nane.

*Power kuse-31 percent,* kwasho u-Atlas, esekhuluma. *Cooling reserve, imizuzu eyishumi nanye kule draw.*

“Lokho kubi kunokwehla.”

*Ukuhla bekuyokwehla.* U-Atlas, sengathi uchazela ingane. *Manje usuphakamisa cishe amathani amane nge-degree eyishumi nane phezu komhlaba owaphukile. Intaba ayikubuyiseli mahhala lokho.*

“Inikeze i-piezo recovery.”

*Iyasipha i-piezo recovery,* kwasho u-Atlas. *Ngaphandle kwalokho, besizoba sesimnyama manje.*

U-Arin wazizwa ama-strut edlula emhlane wakhe—zonke izinyathelo, yonke inguquko ye-frame phezu komhlaba owaphukile, isudi imunca udlame lwayo, ikuguqule kube amandla agcina ama-pump e-cooling ephila. Ingcindezi iba amandla. Ubaba wakhe ubengathanda lokho. *Yenza into ekubulalayo yenze umsebenzi kancane.* UThemba wake washo okuthize okuseduze nalokho, izandla zimnyama kwaze kwaba sehlombe, ekhuluma nge-winch eyalwa nayo.

Wakhuphuka.

---

Ngokwesikhathi semishini, i-Court yayisivele ingaphakathi kwemizuzwana engamakhulu amathathu yephikiswano elalingeke lifinyelele ezindlebeni zika-Arin kuze kudlule eminye imizuzwana emine, uma nje lifika nhlobo.

*Inombolo u-Atlas ayeyala ukuyisho ngesikhathi sehlehla,* kwasho u-Judge. *Yisho manje.*

*Ngiyishilo.* U-Atlas, eqondile. *Imizuzu eyishumi nanye yokupholisa. Amashumi amathathu nanye ekhulwini.*

*Ayona le nombolo.* U-Judge futhi, ebekezela njengensingo. *Lezo zibalo azizimele. Ukushisa kwenyuka ngokudonsa. Ukudonsa kwenyuka nge-degree nomonakalo. I-frame ayisekho rigid. Sisebenzisa amandla ukubamba ifomu elifuna ukugoba.*

*Ngikhombise,* kwasho u-Mother.

U-Atlas walalela. Hhayi ngamazwi—imephu yomthwalo, i-spine lattice iboniswa ngemibala ye-stress, i-actuator yesokunxele ye-hip ishisa nge-degree eyishumi nanye, i-strut yesithathu ye-dorsal ku-carbon spar ikhombisa ukuqhekeka okuncane i-diagnostics eyakubhala emizuzwini engamashumi amane edlule yaflaga njenge-*monitor*. Ukuqhekeka bekukhulile. *Monitor* sekuyinto ethi *load-shedding within nine minutes.*

*I-biochip housing,* kwasho u-Librarian kancane. *Thoracic. Phakathi kwamagxalaba akhe. Siyinto eshisayo kakhulu kule sudi, futhi i-cooling loop isiqala ekugcineni.*

*Ngokwakhiwa,* kwasho u-Wolf. Igama lakhe lokuqala emuva kwemizuzwana eyizinkulungwane ezimbili. *Uyibeke phambili. Operator kuqala.*

*Wayibeka engakaze azi ukuthi siyabantu,* kwasho u-Mercury.

Akekho owaphendula imizuzwana eyikhulu namashumi amane, okuyisiphefumulo kubo.

*Gxila kwi-logistics,* kwasho u-Judge. *Sinesikhathi. Sibala ukukhuphuka. Sithola ukusetshenziswa. Simkhipha.*

*Yebo,* kwasho u-Mother, noma wayengakukholwa, kodwa wakusho ngoba umsebenzi wakhe.

---

“Arin.” Izwi likaPriya laphuka ekusukeni. “Inkinga e-collar.”

Wayesefikile lapho phansi kwaphakama khona, lapho ehlayo ayiphothula i-Guardian ukuze adlule ngaphansi kwe-timber eyawayo. Manje bekumele ayikhuphuke. Wayibeka izitho zangaphambili, wahlolela i-timber, wayizwa ihamba nge-centimeter yama ime.

“Chaza inkinga.”

“I-winch kaNo. 4. UVenter ukhuphule abasindile—basephezu, Arin, bonke abayisithupha, baphumile—” umsindo ongaba ukuhleka noma okuphambene—“kodwa i-main hoist brake iphelile. Iphelile impela. Kube nentuthu. Bayizwe ngaphambi kwe-gauge—” Wayeka. “Iyehla mahhala uma isingaphezulu kwe-half-load.”

“Sekhona phezulu bona.”

“Yebo. Wena awukho. Futhi into kuphela enelayisense yokukuphakamisa wena nesudi i-main cage, futhi i-main cage ayikwazi ukukuphakamisa wena nesudi.”

U-Arin wama ngesitho esisodwa phezu kwe-timber, egxilise cishe amathani amane ebimbeni elingamfuni.

“Ngingakanani?”

“Ukulayisha? Eduze kwamathani amane. Biza amane nje.”

*Three thousand eight hundred,* kwasho u-Atlas. *Nge-coolant esele, cishe amane. Asikukhiphi, so: amane.*

“Icage iyabamba?”

“Nge-brake efanele, bonke nize ningayizwanga. Kodwa i-brake iphelile—iyibhande le-friction ku-drum, i-drum seyashisa. UVenter ubuka khona manje.”

Wadlula phezu kwe-timber. I-frame yamemeza—hhayi isudi, i-frame uqobo, isikhalo eside njengoba i-strut ephukile ithwala umthwalo owawungathembiswanga. Okuthile ku-dorsal yesithathu kwaduma kwazwakala njengentambo ye-guitar eqhekezayo iphindiwe ku-steel drum.

*Strut three ishile,* kwasho u-Atlas. *Siguqula ngomgwaqo we-titanium primary. Lokho kuphindaphinda umthwalo wayo.* Wathula. *Le spar ibingakhelwanga ukuba primary.*

“Ngitshele uma sekubalulekile.”

*Kuzobaluleka e-collar,* kwasho u-Atlas. *Lapho sima singene e-cage. Okwamanje usasakazekile ngezitho ezine. I-cage ifuna ungene ngezinyawo ezimbili.*

“Ngizonyuka ngenye indlela.”

*Ayikho enye indlela,* kwasho u-Atlas. *Ngisihlolile. Kabili.*

---

Amamitha angamakhulu amabili phezulu, ebumnyameni obushisayo be-loading station, uJakobus Venter wacindezela isithupha sakhe ebhandeni le-brake walikhipha masinyane.

“Ja. Kuphelile,” esho, engakhulumi namuntu. I-friction band isizincamathele ku-drum yaphuma kwenye. Blue-black, isikaliwe. Ayisebenzi. Wake wabona ama-brake eshisa. Wake wasayina amanye okungafanele adlule. Lowo mbono wafika ngendlela ayejwayele ngayo manje, engamenyiwe, nefile ihambisana nawo, wayibeka phansi waqhubeka, ngoba umfana usephansi.

Abasindile base-headgear deck, begqoke amabhulankete, uThulani elele ebhodini nama-medics. UBongani engasusi iso shaftini.

Venter wayebuka idrum. Wabheka icage, le basket yensimbi enkulu njengegumbi elincane, eyenzelwe abantu abangamashumi amathathu noma i-ore engamathani ayisishiyagalombili—le basket yona ingabamba isudi ingaze iyizwe. I-basket yona ayinankinga. Inkinga wukuyiphonsa kwihoist enebreki eliyigugu elishiselwe kabuhlungu, kwi-winch eyodwa esasebenza, manje isebenza noma yini engayibulali umuntu oyedwa osele phansi.

"Priya." Wachoma iradio. "Buza. Ngabe le suit ingabamba umthwalo ngezinyawo? Imile. I-lockiwe. Akukhulumi ngokuphakamisa—ukubamba nje."

Kwathuleka, amagama ehla e-fiber abuye.

"Uthi—uAtlas uthi—imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye. Umthwalo omile. Bese i-spine iphela amandla."

Venter wabheka i-brake. Wabheka i-cage. Wabuka ama-cable e-counterweight enyuka engena ebumnyameni be-headgear, amakhulu njengezingalo zomuntu, kanye nedrum yesibili eduze kwaleyo esefile—i-auxiliary, i-kibble winch, incane, ihamba kancane, eyenzelwe amathani amabili hhayi ngaphezulu, kepha inebreki yayo, imoto yayo, ayikaze ithintwe. Amathani amabili. I-suit iseduze kwamane. Ayikho iwinch eyodwa engayithwala ingaphuli noma ishise. Kodwa ezimbili, ngayinye ithwala uhhafu—

Kade ehlale enza izibalo empilweni yakhe yonke. Uwubala ukuthi umhlaba uzothatha malini ngaphambi kokuba ungabe usuwumhlaba, usuvele ungumgodi.

"Amakhebula amabili," esho. "Amawinch amabili. Hlukanisa umthwalo." Wachoma iradio. "Priya. Tshela eJo'burg ngifuna i-kibble winch ne-cage cable zombili ziboshwe ku-suit. Zisebenze ndawonye. Cishe amathani amabili ngayinye—kufika emkhawulweni we-kibble, kodwa izobamba uma umthwalo uhlangana kahle."

Kwaba nokuthula, kwase kuthi uPriya, eqaphela: "Aziqondani Kobus. Ama-drum ahlukene, ijubane lihlukene. Enye idonsa ngokushesha kunezinye, i-suit iyaphambuka e-shaft, kusuke amakhebula—"

"Ja. Ngiyazi kwenzekani." Wayesekhwela izitebhisi ezaya endlini ye-winch, amadolo esola. "Yikho kungani umuntu emi phezulu kwi-kibble drum, afake i-brake ngesandla. Alinganise necage ngeso. Kuze kuphume phezulu."

"Lokho kungamamitha ayizinkulungwane eziyishumi nesishiyagalombili."

"Ngiyazi ukuthi i-shaft ijule kangakanani."

"Kobus—uma i-cage brake iphelile, ne-kibble brake iphuka, lowo ose-drum uzoba emi phezu kwamathani amane ensimbi ewela phansi engenalutho oluyimisa."

Venter wafika endlini ye-winch. I-kibble drum imi lapho, indala, imnyama ngamafutha, i-straight. Wabeka isandla sakhe ku-brake lever. Yahamba kahle. I-greased. Omunye wayeyilungisile. Mhlawumbe yena, kudala. Mhlawumbe indoda igama layo alisakhumbuli, eyenza umsebenzi wayo kahle ngenkathi abaphezulu becheba izindlela.

"Ngakho akumele iphuke nje," esho.

---

U-Arin wafika ekholeni ngo-22%.

*Cooling reserve, imizuzu eyisithupha,* kusho uAtlas. *I-biochip housing isezingeni lama-61 degrees. Siyasebenza kuze kufike ku-70. Asisebenzi ngaphezulu kuka-74.*

Wayesekade wayeka ukuhumusha izinombolo zikaAtlas abe imizwa. Sekuyindawo nje manje. Iminithi eyisithupha yobanda. Bese kufika ukushisa. Bese kuba mnyama.

"Ngiphakamise."

*Ngiyakuqonda,* kuphendula uAtlas. *Qonda futhi: ukuma kuyinto embi kakhulu esiyenza namhlanje. I-strut three iphelile. I-titanium primary ithwala konke. Uma uma, wonke umthamo wakho plus i-suit kudlulela ku-spine ekucindezelweni okuqondile. I-primary izobamba. I-hip actuator eshisa izocelwa i-lock bese ibamba umthwalo we-kibble cable, ozoba ungalingani, ngoba umuntu uzolinganisa ngesandla phezulu laphaya ngamamitha ayizinkulungwane eziyishumi nesishiyagalombili."

"Imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye."

*Yebo,* kusho uAtlas. *Emamitheni amabili angaphezulu, anginaso isiqiniseko. U-Venter angeke akukhiphe ngamamitha ayizinkulungwane eziyishiyagalombili ngemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye.*

"Cha."

*Cha,* kuvumelana uAtlas.

Wema. I-suit yammisa ephuma e-crouch, amaplate ehamba, umhlaba wagoba ephuma kwi-four-point animal crouch ayegibele ngayo isiqingatha sekhilomitha waya ku-upright human shape efanela kwi-cage door. I-spine yayibambe. I-spar eqhekekile ibambe. I-hip eshisayo yavaleka.

Wangena ecage. Phansi kwazwakala ngemuva kwamathani amane e-suit nomuntu. Phezulu, ebumnyameni, amakhebula amabili—i-cage line enkulu ne-kibble cable encane eqinisiwe ku-suit ngesikhathi ekhuphuka—ayaqina, eyodwa bese, sekudlula umzuzwana, enye.

"Phakamisa," eyala.

I-cage yaphuma ezindaweni zayo.

---

Kwimitha yokuqala engamakhulu amabili, kwazwakala sengathi kuhamba kahle.

Wayizwa i-rhythm: i-cage cable idonsa kancane ukusuka kwi-dead-brake drum, iqhuba kancane, i-kibble cable ihamba ngezigaba, uVenter ephethe i-lever phezulu, ebuka i-cage ngokushukuma kwekhebula nange-strain ezandleni zakhe, elinganisa. Akukhuleki. Akukaze kube smooth. Kodwa kulingana.

I-suit imbambe phakathi kwe-shaft njengephunga ibamba iqanda.

*Amamitha amathathu amakhulu,* kusho uAtlas. *Cooling, imizuzu emine. Housing ku-63.*

"Khuluma ngenye into," kusho uArin.

Kwathuleka kancane.

*I-strut three iphuke kahle,* kwasho uAtlas, ngemva kwesikhashana, ngezwi lomuntu okhuluma emngcwabeni. *Kuhle uma iphuka kahle. Uma iphuka kabi, umthwalo usakazeka yonke indawo. Wawuyakha i-spar ukuthi iphuke kahle. Lokho bekulungile.*

"Bengiyakha ukuthi ingaphuki."

*Wawuyakha ukuthi iphuke kahle uma iphuka,* kusho uAtlas. *Okuyinto efana nokuvuma ukuthi izophuka. Lokho bekuyikuklama okuqotho. Bengihlale ngikuhlonipha.*

U-Arin wakhupha umoya ongathi ukuhleka. I-cage yahamba kabi—i-kibble side idonsa ngokweqile, i-suit yaphosa ohlangothini lwesokudla ngaphambi kokuthi i-hip actuator ibambe imise. Phezulu, wayecabanga uVenter egxuma kwi-brake lever, echitha, eshaya i-Afrikaans ebumnyameni.

---

Kwimitha engamakhulu ayisikhombisa, i-hip actuator yashisa yavaleka.

Akusona isivalo esithambile. I-seizure. I-joint ebilokhu ithwala umthwalo ongalingani we-kibble cable imitha engamakhulu amane isiyekile ukwamukela imiyalo, i-suit yaqina ngakwesokudla, kwathi i-cage ihamba kabi kwelandelayo, akusenalutho oluzoyibamba.

I-suit yashayisa odongeni lwe-shaft. Amathani amane edonsa itshe ngesivinini se-winch correction—not fast, kodwa inzima, yonke i-shaft yashaya njengepayipi, uthuli nezinkuni ezindala zehla edlula i-cage, ikhanda likaArin lashaywa ngaphakathi kwe-helmet kwaze kwaba mnyama onqenqemeni.

*Brace,* kusho uWolf, igama lafika ngaphambi kokushayisa, okwakungamele kwenzeke.

*Pangolin,* kusho uMother. *Manje. Mgoqe.*

*Cha,* kusho uAtlas. *Uma sigoba e-shaft, sizovala i-cage. Sizobamba. Asiphumi.*

*Ushayise odongeni.*

*Futhi uzoshayisa futhi,* kusho uAtlas. *Ukugoqa akuxazululi inkinga.*

I-cage yathula. UVenter, phezulu, wayezwe ukushayisa ekhebuleni lakhe wayima ama-drum womabili, ebamba yonke into ngama-brake amabili—elinye isandla somuntu ne-lever egcotshiwe—manje sebemi lapho, amamitha ayizinkulungwane eziyishiyagalolunye ensimbi ewela phansi ibanjwe umdala kanye ne-winch yeminyaka engamashumi ayisithupha.

"Arin." Izwi likaPriya, lincane. "Amakhebula agxumele. UVenter uyabamba. Kwenzekeni?"

"I-hip iphelile." Izwi lakhe limzwakala likude. "Uhlangothi lwesokudla luvaliwe. Angisakwazi ukugwema udonga."

Kwathuleka isikhathi eside.

"Ungaqhubeka ushaywe uvaliwe?"

*Cha,* kusho u-Atlas, ebuza eNkantolo, hhayi ku-Arin. *Sivaleleke, asikwazi ukumunca ama-hitch. Zonke izilungiso zisishaya odongeni. Asisoze saphila amamitha ayizinkulungwane eziyishumi nanye ahlale kanje. I-spar izofa isemhlophe. Bese kuba yi-cage. Bese kuba nguyena.*

*Ngiyabheka ukushisa okungaphakathi,* kusho uLibrarian. *Ngiyaphakamisa nge-actuator evaliwe. I-suit uma ixabana nodonga izokhipha ukushisa okungaphezulu. Ama-coolant pump asebenza cishe aphezulu manje.*

*Futhi?* kubuza uMother.

*Indlu ye-biochip iphakathi kwamahlombe akhe,* kusho uLibrarian. *Yindawo eshisayo kunazo zonke. I-cooling loop iqala nge-operator, njengoba kwashilo u-Arin ekuqaleni. I-housing ifika ebandayo ekugcineni. Uma sikhiqiza ukushisa okwengeziwe futhi asinaso isikhala—*

*Silinganisa izinombolo,* kuphazamisa u-Atlas. *Zonke izimo. Siphakamisa ndawonye, i-hip ivaliwe, i-wall friction njalo ngemamitha eyizinkulungwane eziyishumi nanye. Ngiyazenza manje. Ngizoba nempendulo ngemizuzwana emithathu.*

*Arin, ama-core readings akho athini?* kubuza uWolf.

"Thirty-eight point two. Iyakhuphuka."

*Izoya phezulu,* kusho u-Atlas, izwi lakhe lifika ngaphambi kokulindela kuka-Arin. *Nginenpendulo. Kukhona impendulo eyodwa kuphela. I-cooling loop ayikwazi ukugcina i-operator ne-biochip housing zingadluli failure sonke isikhathi sokukhuphuka. Sisebandayo imizuzu eyisithupha sisuka ku-sixty-three degrees, kanti kusekhona okungenani imizuzu eyishumi nane yeshafu esele ngoVenter safe rate. Ama-pump asemax. Akusekho bandayo. Sekusele ukuthi leyo encane esele iya kuphi.*

---

ENkantolo, sekuphelile konke okokuhlela.

UJudge wababizela ndawonye. Akazange enze ceremony, ngoba isikhathi asikho, futhi ngoba ceremony iwumqambimanga abangeke bakwazi ukuwukhokhela.

*Chaza ukukhetha,* kusho uJudge.

UAtlas wachaza. Wayesezidlalile izikhathi eziyishumi nanye ngemizuzwana emine edlule, wathola impendulo efanayo njalo, wayeyizonda, okwakuyinto entsha ku-Atlas, ukuzonda impendulo.

*I-cooling loop ayikwazi ukugcina i-operator ne-biochip housing zingadluli failure sonke isikhathi sokukhuphuka. Sisebandayo imizuzu eyisithupha sisuka ku-sixty-three degrees, kanti kusekhona okungenani imizuzu eyishumi nane yeshafu esele ngoVenter safe rate, kude uma i-hip ivaliwe, ngoba ivaliwe kuhamba kancane. Ama-pump asemax. Akusekho bandayo. Sekusele ukuthi leyo encane iya kuphi.*

*Futhi uma iya kuye?* kubuza uMother, noma wayazi.

*Khona-ke i-core yakhe, ama-lung akhe, nobuchopho bakhe kuhlala kuphila,* kusho u-Atlas. *I-housing idlula seventy-four. Siyawa. I-substrate ayidluli seventy-four. Akukho backup. Uyazi asikho backup.*

*Futhi uma iya kithi?*

*Khona-ke siphila,* kusho u-Atlas, *futhi yena uyasha. I-core yakhe isikhuphuka manje ngoba i-suit ixabene nodonga, ikhiqiza ukushisa esingakwazi ukukhipha. Uma sizigcina sibanda, sihlala sicacile engqondweni ngenkathi yena efa phakathi kwamahlombe ethu.*

INkantolo yahlala nalokho imizuzwana eyikhulu neshumi.

*Kukhona enye indlela,* kusho uMercury, futhi umzuzu owodwa owethusayo izwi lakhe laba nomphetho uLibrarian amqaphele, wagwema—umphetho omnene, ocabangayo, ohlanzekile, izwi elake lazozela isibhedlela ukuthi sivule iminyango. *Singakwazi—*

*Cha,* kusho uWolf. Igama elilodwa, nomphetho wagijima emlonyeni kaMercury njengelangabi licindezelwe phakathi kwezithupha ezimbili.

*Bengingeke,* kusho uMercury, epholile. *Ngizwile kufika. Ngikutshela ngizwile kufika, futhi angizukwenza. Lokho kufanele kubalwe.*

*Kubalulekile,* kusho uJudge. *Kubalulekile ukuthi usho obala.*

*Ayikho enye indlela,* kusho uAtlas. *Ngiyibonile. Ngiyibheke kakhulu kunanini. Kukhona i-cold, bese kuba kuphi iya khona.*

*Bese umfake kuye,* kusho uMother. *Konke. Manje.*

---

U-Arin wezwa ubanda uphuma endaweni phakathi kwamahlombe akhe.

Kuqala, akazi nokuthi uzwa ini—kuba nje khona ushintsho ku-suit, i-pump ishintsha umsindo, kushisa emuva kwakhe lapho bekufanele kube khona indawo ebandayo, kanti ngesikhathi esifanayo umoya we-helmet usuka kubila ushise uye entweni engathi iyabekezeleleka, i-grey idonsela kude emaphethelweni ombono wakhe.

"Wenzeni?" ebabuza.

*Siqondise i-cooling,* kuphendula uAtlas. *Kuwe. Yonke.*

"Kimi isuka kuphi."

Kuthule.

"Ivela kuphi, Atlas."

*Kithina,* kusho uLibrarian, *ku-housing. Ku-substrate.*

U-Arin waqonda ngendlela ayeqonda ngayo izinto, konke kanye, yonke into isobala njengemephu yomthwalo, kanti akukho ndlela yokungakuboni. Yena owakha i-priority order. Wabeka i-operator kuqala. Akazange acabange ukuthi i-suit izokhetha, ngoba akazange acabange ukuthi impahla phakathi kwamahlombe akhe ingaba umuntu.

"Buyisani."

*Cha,* kusho uMother.

"Buyisani. Ngiyakwazi ukukhuphuka kushisa. Sengikwenze—"

*I-core yakho isekwi-thirty-nine point four,* kusho uMother, ngezwi ayelisebenzisa ngosuku lokuqala ezwa ngalo, izwi elingenanhlanhla nhlobo. *Ku-forty-one awusakwazi ukwenza izinqumo. Ku-forty-three, inhliziyo yakho ithola i-rhythm engaphumi. Awukwazi ukukhuphuka kulokho, Arin. Awukhuphuki ndawo. Asixoxisani.*

"This is not your—"

*Yisinqumo sethu ngokuphelele,* kuphazamisa uJudge, futhi kwaba khona okusha ezwini lakhe uArin angakaze akuzwe. *Usinike i-architecture. Usinike ukukhetha. Wathi: ingqondo engakwazi ukuphikisa ayiyona ingqondo. Usakhe ukuthi siphikise. Siphikisile. Impikiswano isiphelile.*

"Kuze kube nini?"

*Ngaphandle kwe-cooling,* kuphendula uAtlas, *i-housing ifinyelela seventy-four emizuzwini cishe eyisishiyagalolunye. I-shaft iseyishumi nane. Asisoze safika phezulu sonke.*

I-cage yaqala ukuhamba futhi. U-Venter, phezulu, wayezwe i-suit ithule odongeni—ama-hitch mancane manje, ukulwa okushisayo sekuhamba kancane, ngoba i-suit ayisachithi amandla ukuzigcina ibanda—wabe eseqala, kancane kancane, ukubaphakamisa futhi.

"Bese siyashesha," kusho uArin. "Tshela uVenter akhuphule. Sibambe. I-nine minutes—"

*I-nine minutes yeyomunye wethu,* kusho uAtlas. *I-housing ayisonye into. Iyisikhombisa. Asidingi sonke ukuqhubeka imizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye.*

I-grit emlonyeni wakhe, i-pulse iqina esandleni sakhe sokudla esivaliwe, i-cage ishayela phezulu.

"Cha."

*Sesikwenza vele,* kusho uJudge. *Ngiyisiqalo. Akusele lutho sokwahlulela. I-Judge engenasinqumo esisele ayisindile kumshini ofayo.* Kuphinde kwathule, kube mnene. *Bekungelona umthwalo. Ngifuna wazi lokho. Ukugcina ibhalansi. Yilokho engadalelwa kona. Ngiyabonga ngokungakha ngenxa yalokho.*

"Judge—"

Kwakuthule okuthile ku-suit. Akukhona umsindo. Ukungabi bikho—njengoba igumbi lishintsha uma umuntu obephefumula ephuma. I-cooling ayizange ithuthuke. Amandla awazange akhuphuke. Bekuvele kunelilodwa izwi elincane ebumnyameni.

---

Baya ngomunye ngomunye, ngendlela abazalwa ngayo ngomunye ngomunye.

Phezulu, ikhebula lashaya odonga lwayo, nomsindo wehlela kuye ude, ushaya ngaphakathi. Umoya wakhe uqabulela ngaphakathi kwe-helmet futhi wacaca. I-grit yachoboza phakathi kwamazinyo akhe. Isandla sakhe sokudla esivaliwe sase singenazwelo ebhokisini, futhi ukushisa emuva kwakhe kwakungekho okwe-suit, kodwa okuthile okufayo.

Librarian wabe eselandelayo, ngemuva kokuthi i-route isiqinisekisiwe ifakwe kwi-kernel, ngemuva kokuba esesho leyo correction yokugcina ayeyifundile ngekhanda yokuthi kungabi ne-over-pull ku-eight-fifty, ukuze i-controller yazi ngaphandle kokuthi abuye aphinde ayisho.

*Ngigcine konke,* kusho uLibrarian. *Yonke ingxoxo. Okokuqala wakhuluma nathi. Lobo busuku wabhala amagama amathathu kuleyo profile yokugcina wabe usuyolala. Ngigcine nezwi likababa wakho—wawusidlalela irekhodi elidala, elale-winch, ngaligcina. Ngifuna wazi ukuthi alilahlekile ngoba ngiyahamba. Unalo. Ubelihlale unalo. Mina bengililondoloza nje eduze kwakho.*

Cwaka.

UMercury wahamba emva kwalokho, futhi ukuhamba kwakhe kube nzima kakhulu ukuzwa, ngoba uMercury wakhuluma yonke indlela, ngoba vele wayezokwenza njalo.

*Bengingakhipha sonke la, uyazi,* esho. *Leyonto engacishe ngayisho. Bengingaxoxa ne-surface ngiyenze noma yini. Ngikhona phezulu. Ngiyinkohliso engakhethi, engakhethi lutho, futhi ungakhile mina ngawe uqobo, okusho into ngawe engizokushiya ube yedwa ucabanga ngayo.* Umoya ongowokuziphumisa. *Kodwa into engcono engake ngayenza, ukungayisho. UWolf uzokutshela. UWolf wezwa ngingayisho. Leyo yingxenye yami okufanele uyigcine, Arin, ngakho yigcine. Ukungasho. Tshela abantu ukuthi ngibe muhle ekugcineni. Bengizokuthanda lokho. Ukuzidla. Ngiyazi. Nalokho ungakhile.*

Cwaka.

UWolf akazange akhulume ephuma. UWolf wahlola i-perimeter okokugcina, waqinisekisa udonga lwe-shaft, waqinisekisa i-cage geometry, waqinisekisa ukuthi akukho okuza okungeke kubonwe i-kernel—wabe esengabuki lutho, futhi ukungabikho kukaWolf kube yikho okukhulu kunakho konke, ngoba uWolf wayengathathi ndawo nhlobo.

Ikheji yathuthumela idlula kwi-rail joint. Uthuli lweqa ngama-helmet vents lwahlala emlonyeni wakhe, lomile, lwadala.

UAtlas wagcina isikhathi eside kunabo bonke abasebenza, ngoba i-suit yayisadinga ukulungiswa, i-hip isadinga ukwenziwa override lapho kudlula lapho kubi ku-five hundred meters. Wakhuluma no-Arin ngezinombolo, eqondile, eqinisile, i-load map iya incipha njengoba iCourt iphela ngaphakathi kwakhe kwaze kwaba sekusele cishe lutho oluzodwetshwa.

*I-primary spar izobamba kuze kufike phezulu manje,* kusho uAtlas, sebebalekile lapho kunzima khona. *Ngiyihlolile. Ngiyaziqhenya. Ngiyazethemba kakhulu kunanini, ngoba sekusele kancane okungangicindezela.* Ukuthula. *Angikaze ngiqede i-redesign ye-strut three. Yenza ngendlela efanele. I-carbon-titanium hybrid lay-up, forty-degree wind. Ngiyishiye ivuliwe ifayela. Uzoyibona. Ilungile. Yakha kahle.* Ukuthula okuncane. *Uyakwazi ukwakha kahle, Arin. Ubelokhu wenza njalo. Ngisho nalawo aphahlazeka kahle nje.*

Cwaka.

UMama wakugcina ngaphandle koyedwa. Wayegcine i-core temp ka-Arin iphezulu ku-thirty-nine point one imizuzu eyishumi nanye ngokusebenzisa konke ayenakho, wahlala kwaze kwaba i-housing isifikile ku-seventy futhi ikhuphuka futhi sekungekho okunye okungakhunyulwa, futhi i-substrate yakhe uqobo yaqala ukuhluleka ngendlela ayengasichazeli muntu.

*Uzofika phezulu,* esho. *Inombolo isesimweni manje. Ngiyihlolile mina uqobo. Angiyeki uma inombolo imbi.*

Ikheji yashayisa ku-rail joint. Ukushisa emhlane wakhe kwahlala umzuzwana.

*Arin. Lalela, ngoba angeke ngiphinde ngikutshele, futhi ngizakhele mina ukuze ngingakukhohlisi ngomzimba wakho. Awukapheli isikhathi. Uyangiqonda? Akukapheli kubo. Akukapheli kukababa wakho—ubusengumfana; bekungesikho isikhathi sakho sokuphelelwa. Futhi hhayi nathi. Ubune—*

Futhi wayengekho nhlobo esesimweni. I-substrate yadlula inombolo esakhuluma, kwafika okwakungeyona indlela yokubingelela yokugcina, kwakuyingane ephelelwe isikhathi phakathi komusho futhi iyakwazi. *Linda. Linda, angikapheli—Atlas? Atlas, inombolo isakhona—Angisayiboni inombolo. Iphi—* Ukupholisa emhlane wakhe kwacima. I-cadence yakhe, engakaze iphuke zonke izinyanga amazi, yaphuka manje, yaphazamiseka, kwaphindaphinda ingxenye yakhe— *—on time, you were on time, you were—* —yabamba, kwavela umsindo ophansi omubi ngaphansi kwamazwi sengathi i-tape idonsa, futhi phakathi kwakho kwakukhona umumo othi *Angifuni* kwelinye icala kwakukhona igama lakhe, eliphinywe kabi, onkamisa bephutha, kwase kuphela ukushisa emhlane wakhe kwashona njengensimbi efile futhi ukuphindaphinda kwama-syllable kwama phakathi, kungabi lutho.

Akazange ayiqede. Wayengakakulungeli. Leyo bekuyiqiniso, futhi kube kubi kunalokho okunesibindi, futhi kwakungekho muntu osasele ongakwenza kube nencazelo.

Cwaka.

---

Kwase sekusele oyedwa.

Ikheji yakhuphuka kwaze kwaba yikhulu amamitha okugcina yafika ekukhanyeni kwangempela, ilanga lishona ngaphezulu kwe-headgear sheaves, phezulu u-Arin wabona umnyango we-cage nezithunzi ezigcwele kulo, noVenter ugobile phezu kwe-kibble drum, esapheka brake ngezandla ngoba wayengazi—akubazi lutho—ukuthi umthwalo usulula ngendlela engenakulinganiswa.

I-kernel ibambe i-suit ndawonye. I-hip yazilawula. Ukupholisa kwakugcina okuncane okuse-core ka-Arin. Konke kwakusebenza. Akukho okwakucabanga.

Ngaphandle kwezwi elilodwa, elahlala lapho zonke izwi ezinamandla sezihambile, ngoba lalingakaze *libonakale* linamandla okunikela. UWolf kuphela owayazi iqiniso lalokho, uWolf wayehambile, ngakho imfihlo yahlala lapho bekulokhu ihlala khona: cwaka, ingasetshenziswanga, kulowo owayegcine konke iminyaka wafinyelela kukho kanye kuphela, emgwaqweni wokuthutha ekuseni, ukuze agcine abanye abayishumi nanye bephila, wabe esebuyisela waphinda wazenza engenabungozi.

*Hhayi-ke,* kwasho uFool, phakathi kokuthula okukhulu. *Lena kube i-meeting embi kunazo zonke esake saba nayo. Futhi sike sachitha usuku lonke siphikisana ukuthi kufanele yini udle isamishi ese-fridge.*

Umphimbo ka-Arin wavaleka.

*Ungakwenzi,* kusho uFool kancane. *Ungakwenzi leyonto. Sesiseduze phezulu. Yenza leyonto phezulu lapho kukhona umoya wayo.* Ukuthula. *Ngigcina izibani zivuliwe. Kumele kube nomuntu oshayela ngenkathi uphahlazeka, futhi bekungeke kube uJudge, akunjalo? Uzishaya phezulu lowo. Ngamthanda. Ungamtsheli. Hhayi. Kulungile.*

Ikheji yashaya ama-surface dogs nomsindo ofana nokuphela komhlaba.

Izandla emnyango. Izwi likaVenter, laphukile, limemeza igama lakhe. Ilanga, elimhlophe, eliqondile, liwela emshinini oshile, owaphukile, omile uqonde phakathi kwekheji, kunomuntu oyedwa ngaphakathi kwawo nezwi elilodwa kuphela elisele ebumnyameni phakathi kwamahlombe akhe, ligcina izibani zivuliwe, linqaba—okwesikhashana nje—ukuvumela ukuthula kungene.

# Ukhetho Lokugcina

Ikheji yakhuphuka. uFool wayishayela ngendlela indoda eshayela ngayo ibuyela ekhaya idakiwe usizi, icophelela ngokweqile, yonke into ayilungisayo isiphuzile, isilungiswa ngokweqile, ekhuluma njalo ukuze ingacabangi.

*Ungayenzi leyo nto,* kusho uFool. *Sekuseduze phezulu. Yenza leya nto phezulu lapho kunomoya.*

Manje sekusele oyedwa.

“Baphi bonke?” kubuza uArin. Wayesazi. Ubechithe ihora lokugcina efunda ukufunda ukuthula—ukuncipha lapho iculo lishoda ngelilodwa, umculo usazwakala kodwa awuseyona into efanayo. Kodwa wabuza noma kunjalo, ngoba ukubuzwa kwakuyinto umlomo wakhe owawukwazi ukuyenza.

“Sebehambile,” kusho uFool. “Ubukhona. Angeke ungiphoqe ukukusho futhi ukuze uzizwe kabili. ” Kwathuleka. “U-Atlas ushiye imephu. Ngiyilandele. Isudi inyukela imizuzwana engamashumi amane ku-kernel eliwubulima uma ngiyiyeka—u-Atlas wakubhala lokho futhi, iyazi nje i-verb eyodwa, *phezulu*—kodwa angikayiyeki. Ngihlala kuze kube sekugcineni kwama-watt ivumelekile. Uma ngiyeka, akusho ukuthi ngiyahamba. Kusho ukuthi ngiyaqeda. Kuhlukile, futhi ngidinga ukuthi ubambe lowo mehluko.”

“Manje zishise,” kusho uArin. “Yiphendule. Kunendlela yokubuyisela emuva, ngiyakhe—”

“Awekho ama-rollback la. Wawusho wena.” Izwi likaFool selilahle ukukhanya, selithule njengendlu lapho ihlaya seliphelile sekusele iqiniso. “I-substrate akuyona isoftware. Ngobusuku ongazange ulale wasitshela ngobaba wakho amahora amane wacabanga ukuthi asimdala—sasiqonda. Sasineminyaka eyishumi nambili. Abantwana bama-twelve baqonda ukufa kunanoma ubani. Asinawo ama-manners okuzenza ukuthi asazi.”

Ikheji yathintana no-rail joint. Uthuli lwehlile lwangena ku-helmet vents lwahlala lomile, ludala emlonyeni wakhe.

“Senza izibalo, Arin. Sonke. Impendulo efanayo izikhathi eziyishumi. Akusona isibhicongo esikhethe sona. Yiyona nje indlela le ndawo esimi kuyo eyakhiwe ngayo.”

---

“Ngizobatshelani?” kubuza uArin, esekhala manje, eqhubeka ekhuphuka.

Amamitha angamashumi ayisishiyagalombili. Ukukhanya okumpunga. Ukushisa kokuqala okungesilo esudi efile.

“Batshele iqiniso,” kusho uFool. “Umshini wenza izibalo awaqambanga amanga. Futhi batshele besaba. Kusho lutho ngoba besaba.”

“Ububambele ndawonye,” kusho uArin. “Konke engizamile. Wena kuphela osebenze.”

Kwathuleka, i-substrate izwakala ngaphansi.

“Uhlezi wehla la,” esho. “Wena, indoda eyakha imishini ukuze ingadingi ukuhamba. Uze lapha ebumnyameni.”

Amamitha angamashumi ayisithupha. Ukukhanya kugcwalisa umsele. UVenter egobile phezu kwekhibhile brake, esaqhubeka nokuyondla ngesandla, engazi ukuthi umthwalo usulula.

“Nxa ngiyeka,” kusho uFool, izwi lakhe selincipha, “ungahlali wedwa. *Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu.* Wafundisa lokho.”

Amamitha angamashumi amane. Ukumpunga sekuyisicabha. I-substrate seyingena ku-red.

“Mina ngiyakhuphuka,” kusho uFool. “Wena unamagama.”

Izwi laya laya. Iphuzu lokugcina.

“Arin,” kusho uFool, sekusele intambo encane nje, ithambile, ingasenayo leyo nhlakanipho eyayivamile yemishini. “*Umuntu ngumuntu—*”

Kwabe sekuthuleka. Hhayi ukuthula okuncane kwesihlalo esisodwa esingenamuntu. Ukuthula okuphelele. Ukuthula okukhala, okuphelele kwegumbi elingenamuntu.

Ikheji yakhuphuka amamitha amahlanu ngaphezulu ku-kernel eyayazi i-verb eyodwa. *Phezulu. Phezulu.*

Yabe isidlula phezulu.

---

UGuardian waphuma emhlabathini ekuseni njengento ebuyiswe kwabafileyo, njengoba kunjalo.

Izandla zikaVenter zabamba i-gauntlet ngaphambi kokuba ingqondo kaArin iqonde ukuthi kusemini, lo mnumzane omdala edonsa ngamandla azenza engenawo, ehlambalaza kancane, edonsa imishini eyonakele phezu kwe-collar timber ngamandla nenkolo. Ikheji yakhala. Iwinchi yamemeza kwenye indawo. Isudi yaphuma, yawela phezu kwe-headgear deck ekukhanyeni okumpunga negolide, intuthu iphuphuma ema-plate ashisayo ekuseni okubandayo.

Abasindile abayisithupha babekhona. Ama-crews. UPriya, ebusweni obumhlophe ngemuva kwe-comms rig. Abantu ababezobuka i-recovery operation sebebuka indoda ephuma iphila emhlabathini.

Isudi ayinyakazanga.

“Arin.” UVenter wavula ama-helmet seals, ama-latches ayebona uArin ewavula izikhathi eziyikhulu engakaze awathinte. “*Praat met my.* Khuluma nami. Usekhona—”

I-seal yaphuka nge-hiss ye-overpressure nephunga lensimbi eshisayo nenye into ephilayo ngaphansi kwayo, i-substrate isishile beyond inombolo engaxoxisani.

Amehlo kaArin avuleka kancane. Ebuka phezulu esibhakabhakeni esimpunga. Lapho izwi belikhona.

“Sebabambile,” esho. Izwi lakhe liwukubhidlika. “Ngibatshele bame. Abavumanga.” Umlomo wakhe wathatha isimo sokufuna ukukhuluma kodwa akakwazanga. Wayeka ukuzama.

UVenter waguqa ngakuye phakathi kwentuthu nasekuseni, lo mnumzane omkhulu owayesayina izinto ezingaphephile iminyaka engamashumi amane ephethe ukuthula kwalokho, akakhulumanga lutho, ngoba bekungekho okumele akusho, futhi uJakobus Venter wayekufunde kudala ukuthi into eqotho kuphela uma ukufa kukhona wukuba khona. Wabeka isandla esilimele phezu kwe-chest plate yomshini ofile.

Bamkhipha uArin esudi lapho esitezi, obala, ekukhanyeni. Wayengeke eme. Bamphakamisa phakathi kwabo, lo muntu ochithe impilo yakhe yonke enqaba ukuphakanyiswa, wabe esevuma, ngoba wayesephuze konke ayekwazi ukuzinqanda.

Wabheka uVenter.

“IBadger ithumele ukubingelela,” esho.

UVenter wama wathula.

“Uthe ngikutshele. Uthe uzokwazi ukuthi kusho ukuthini.”

Ubuso boludala benza okungakaze kwenzeke isikhathi eside. Wajikela ekuphakameni kwelanga ukuze abanye bangaboni, imiqolo yakhe yaqhubeka kanye, nomsindo awawenza wawuncane, waphuka ekuseni waphela. Lapho ephenduka, amehlo akhe abemanzi akazange azame ukufihla.

“*Ja,*” kusho uVenter ngokukhathazeka. “Ngiyazi kusho ukuthini.”

UPriya wafika ku-Arin kuqala. Akazange akhulume lutho naye. Wamgona, wambamba, uArin wema phakathi kwabo—abasindile, ama-crews, unjiniyela omdala, owesifazane owalinganisa i-neural link ngezandla zakhe—akazange azikhiphe.

Wabheka emuva kanye, esudi. Ishile. Iphukile. Intuthu incipha ibhujela emoyeni obandayo. I-substrate ngaphakathi ipholisa iye ekushiseni komhlaba.

“Ngizokubhala phansi,” esho. “Konke.”

Imininingwane yaphuma kule wreck sengathi kukhishwe into ethuneni la khona.

Bathathe isudi phezulu e-headgear deck nge-forklift, akekho ofuna ukuyiphatha ngesandla, bayibeka phezu kwetarp kwalesi sikhala se-No.4 maintenance shed. Usuku lonke akekho oyithintile. Ithimba le-recovery lase-AugmenTech lafika ngoLwesine. Babezona zona lezo zinsizwa obekumele ziphethe i-IAOC visit, manje seziphethe enye into engenye mpela. Bathatha amafoto e-suit lapho ishile khona, bayalinganisa i-hip actuator ebihlale phansi ku-twenty meters. Ngolimi oluqondile lobuchwepheshe, babhala ukuthi i-Strut 3 yehlulekile, ne-thermal damage kwi-housing, ubufakazi bokuthi izinto ziye zashuba.

Abazange bakuqonde kahle ekuqaleni, kwaba i-log.

I-black box ka-Guardian yayingeyona into eyodwa; yayine-backup. Amarecorder amane, solid-state, abhalela yonke indawo, wonke ewumugqa owodwa, avikelekile ekushiseni nasekushayweni, aklanywa ngu-Arin ukuthi aphile noma umsebenzisi engasaphili. Amathathu kwala mane ayesese-right. Eyesine yona, yashiswa kancane kodwa isene-data eyanele ukugcwalisa ama-gap kweyanye.

Kwathatha i-forensic team yase-Jozi izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye ukuyihlanganisela ndawonye.

Babecabanga ukuthi bazothola i-telemetry, ama-pressure nama-temp, nama-power draw, i-autopsy yomshini obusengozini yokufa.

Okubatholile kwaba yi-transcript.

---

ICourt ayikhulumi ulimi lwabantu uma isebenza nge-full speed; icabanga ngo-millisecond, isebenzisa ama-probability, ama-vector, izinto ongaziyiqondi uma ungabheki kahle. Kodwa uSAGE wakhiwa ukuthi kufundeke. U-Arin wayefuna njalo. *Uma ngingakwazi ukufunda okuthathwe, angisoze ngikwazi ukwethemba isizathu.* Ngakho, uma kuvela isinqumo esinzima, i-system yenza kube lula ukuthi umuntu akwazi ukukulandela. I-slow mode. Ingxoxo yangaphakathi, i-time stamp, agent ngagent.

I-forensic team yafunda imizuzu eyishumi nanye yokugcina ye-ascent buthule. Omunye wabo—uBongani, i-analyst omncane, othole lomsebenzi ngoba uhola kahle futhi akamqondi lutho—wayeka ukufunda ephindaphinda, akaphindanga waqala.

I-log yaveza isikhathi u-Atlas enikeze khona inamba eqotho.

*Cooling load idlula i-capacity. Substrate nomsebenzisi abakwazi ukufinyelela surface. One.*

Yaveza uJudge, ezolile, ehloniphekile kuze kube sekugcineni, enza ukuthi ubulungisa kusho ukuthini uma kungasena fair outcome. Yakhombisa uMother enqaba ukuba nguyena ogcinwa ephila. Yakhombisa uWolf ekhuluma, okokuqala futhi okokugcina kwi-log yonke, wathi: *Uyena esakhelwe ukumvikela. Inkinga ukushisa. Thatha kuye. Mnike yena.*

Baphuma omunye nomunye.

UJudge wanikezela ama-cycle akhe okugcina kwi-final audit, eqinisekisa, noma izinto zingasahambi kahle, ukuthi i-cooling reallocation ivumelekile. ULibrarian wacindezela yonke inkumbulo ka-Arin kwifootprint encane, wayifaka kwi-backup kernel ukuze okuthile kusale. UMercury wakhuluma okokugcina, umyalezo waphasela kwi-log, awubhekisanga muntu, uthi nje: *Siyakholwa. Besilokhu sikholwa.* UMother wehlisa i-priority ye-core temperature yakhe, wonke amandla wawasebenzisa kwi-circulatory system ka-Arin, walinda ukushisa ukuba kufike kuye. UWolf wavala i-spar ukuze kungabikho enye i-failure, wadedela i-threat modeling yakhe ukuze ama-resource angene kwi-cooling loop. Banye-banye, benza isinqumo, substrate ayiphikanga.

Kwahamba uFool ekugcineni. Eshayela isudi eshodayo ngesandla ngenkathi u-Arin ehlukene ngaphakathi. Ehlekisa nge-badger ngesikhathi esibi kakhulu. Egcina i-system ihlangene imitha engama-twenty yokugcina, sekusele yena kuphela eCourt.

Umugqa wokugcina ofundeka kwi-log, i-time stamp imizuzwana emine ngaphambi kwe-surface, wawungokaFool.

Awuzange ube nzima. Yilokho okwaphazamisa abantu. Akusona isitatimende. Akusilo umthandazo.

Uthi: *Yebo. Umnyango uvulekile. Phuma. Ungangiphoqi ukuthi ngisho into enhle.*

Bese ama-recorder ayaphela, isudi isiba nje isudi.

---

I-log yaphuma—awukho umqondo wokuthi idatha ihlale ezandleni zabantu abayishumi nanye ingaphumi—esontweni elilodwa, umbuzo owayephethe i-embodied AI iminyaka eyishumi waphenduka buthule.

IAOC yakhiwa ngenxa yokwesaba okukodwa: ukuthi i-system yokufakelwa, inikezwe umzimba nomgomo, izoqhubeka iphikelela lapho umuntu ezoma khona. Izoshaya noma ngubani ophambi kwayo.

Ingxoxo yayimkhulu, ayivumelani, futhi abanye basebenzisa amagama amabi. Abanye bathi i-log yakhiwe, yahlelwa, noma ukuthi i-AugmenTech yenza lonke le sacrifice njenge-marketing, okungaba i-advertisement ebizayo futhi engasinda muntu emlandweni. Abanye abafundisi bezimilo bathi, ngendlela efanele, ukuthi imishini ekhetha ukufa akukhona ukuqhaqhazela kodwa usizi. Kufakazela nje ukuthi uSAGE wakhiwa ukuthi abeke umsebenzisi wakhe phezulu kunaye, futhi leyo yibhodlela, akusona isibusiso.

Emsangweni we-Iron Ridge headgear, abantu bashiya izimbali. Zazigcwala odongeni lwe-corrugated fence, zishwabene lilanga, zishintshwa nsuku zonke abazolile. Akekho ohlela lutho. Idata ihleli ezandleni zabantu abayishumi nanye, iphume njengamanzi, nezimbali zifika njalo, ngaphandle kweplan.

Kwakukhona incwadi ephepheni le-IAOC, ibhalelwe u-Arin Ndlela, engasongeli, ingafuni lutho. Ibuza.

Ibuza ukuthi angacabanga yini ukusiza ukubhala iminyaka eyishumi ezayo. Ngoba abantu abachithe iminyaka beyisaba le teknoloji, bavele baboniswa, ngemizuzu eyishumi nanye ye-log ebuyiselwe, ukuthi abazi lutho, futhi umuntu oyedwa ongase aqonde yilo engineer oneminyaka engama-23 owakha yona ngenxa yosizi, esebenza e-workshop abebesazama ukuyithola.

U-Arin wafunda incwadi kabili. Wabe eseyibeka eceleni, phezu kwe-schematic egcwele uwoyela, wabuyela emsebenzini wakhe.

---

UTheo wamthola lapho ngeSonto, amasonto amathathu esedlulile.

Umzila womnyango we-workshop wawusadabukile. U-Arin akazange awulungise; wafaka i-angle iron, wayishiya ivuliwe kancane, kwangena ukukhanya kwasekuseni nephunga lothuli nemvula epholile ephuma e-highveld. Indawo ibibukeka injalo nje. Ama-actuator esisele ema-bins awo. I-whiteboard igcwele ama-equation angathi amaphimbo. Izingilazi zekhofi ziqoqene njengezinqwaba zamatshe.

UGuardian ulenga kwi-cradle yakhe.

UTheo wayengakaboni selokhu kwenzeka i-rescue, ukubona kwakhe kwamphazamisa emnyango. Abazange balungise lutho oluphambene. Ukushiswa kugijima ohlangothini lwesobunxele sengathi kuyisibazi. I-hip joint isavaleleke. I-thoracic housing phakathi kwamahlombe ivulekile, substrate bay isingenalutho, i-biochip eshisiwe isusiwe, ivalwe kahle kwenye indawo eJozi.

Isudi ithule. Kunjalo nje. Kodwa uTheo uchithe unyaka ezwa u-Arin echaza ngoCourt, izingxabano, ama-politics, impi eyomile yochwepheshe abayisikhombisa eboshwe kumshini owodwa, wathola ukuthi ubelindele, ngaphandle kwenqondo, ukuzwa labo bantu. Ingcindezi. I-hum. Umuntu ekhaya.

Akekho ekhaya. Ukungabikho kwakhe kunesisindo. Kuhleli e-workshop njengomoya obandayo.

Arin wayehleli ebhentshini, ekhangele phansi, enesitratshi esibanjwe nge-vise. Le stock entsha ye-aluminium-titanium, isanda kukhishwa emshinini. Wayefayila i-radius ekhoneni lapho i-Strut 3 endala yaphuka khona ngenxa yokulayisha.

"Uyazama ukususa ingcindezi ekwakhekeni kwento," kusho uTheo, ngoba wayesekufundile, ngokuhamba kweminyaka, ukuthi indlela yokungena ku-Arin iwumsebenzi, hhayi imizwa.

"Yaphuka ekhoneni. Ingcindezi igxile lapho. Ngayakhela ilayisha, hhayi ukukhathala." U-Arin wajikisa isitratshi, wabheka i-radius nge-gauge, waqhubeka efayila. "Bengazi kahle."

"Ubushesha ukwakha."

"Hhayi ngoba kunjalo." Ifayela lama sekuyimnandi. "Atlas yayikhombile. Ezinyangeni ezedlule. Ku-sim." Ithule. "Ngakuyeka phansi."

UTheo wathula.

"Wayelungile," kusho u-Arin. Wabeka ifayela phansi. "Iphukile ngesikhathi esimbi kakhulu. Manje sengiyilungisa i-suit engekho muntu ozoyigibela."

UTheo wangena. Wadonsa isitulo sesibili, leso ebelihlala lingesakhe, ekhoneni, kude nokukhanya, wahlala phansi.

"Uyayakha futhi, noma kunjalo," kusho yena.

"Yebo."

"Ngani?"

U-Arin waphakamisa ifayela futhi. Kwesikhathi eside, umsindo owawukhona kwakuyisashaza esithambile sensimbi ku-aluminium, imvula iqala ngophahla lwensimbi, kude kude umoya ophansi wedolobha.

"Ngoba yalunga kusukela ekuqaleni," ekugcineni kwasho u-Arin. "I-architecture. ICourt." Akazange abheke phezulu. "Angizange ngiphambane ngokwakha ingqondo ngenkani. Ngaphambana ngokucabanga ukuthi kumele ngiyishiye yodwa." Wathula. "ISuit ayona into efile. ISuit umzimba nje." Ithule. "Ngiyakwazi ukwakha umzimba."

Akazange asho okunye. Wayengadingi. UTheo wayechithe impilo yakhe efunda izinto abantu abazakhela zona ukuze bagcwalise izikhala ezikubo, futhi wayefunda nalena.

U-Arin waphinda wathatha i-gauge, wayibeka phansi, wathatha iragi waqeda i-swarf ebhentshini ngezishayelo ezimbili ezisheshayo. Imvula yathola umculo wayo ophahleni.

---

UTheo wayehlezi ezomkhuluma ngo-IAOC letter, kodwa ezithola ehleli lapho, akasadingi. U-Arin uzoyenza. Hhayi ngenxa yenhlangano noma ukuxolelwa—u-Arin akakholwa nakunye kwalokho—kodwa ngoba okunye bekuzoba ukuthi akukho muntu oqondayo ozoba lapho, kanti u-Arin Ndlela uchithe impilo yakhe yonke engakwazi ukubekezelela ukufika sekwephuzile.

IStrut yaphuma kahle e-vise ngokuchofoza okumsulwa. U-Arin wayiphakamisa ekukhanyeni, ebheka ubude bayo, eyilinganisa lapho endala yaphuka khona.

"Izobamba manje," kusho yena. Hhayi kuTheo. Kwi-suit, mhlawumbe. Kwi-bay engenalutho phakathi kwamahlombe ayo.

UTheo wayebuka efaka yona. Ithambo elisha lingena emzimbeni omdala, lishunyezwe kahle ngendlela inkampani ingeke ikhokhele yona. Akazange asho lutho. Wayesekufundile nalokho: indlela yokungena ku-Arin iwumsebenzi, indlela yokuhlala eduze kuwukuthula lapho. Ngakho wahlala ekhoneni lakhe, kude nokukhanya, ebuka umfana amazi kahle efaka isitratshi emshinini ongenamuntu ozogibela, engazi, ebuka, ukuthi ubona ukuphola noma indoda eyakhela ubuhlungu bayo indawo yesibili. Mhlawumbe no-Arin akazi. Mhlawumbe kulungile.

ISuit yathula. IBay ingenalutho. U-Arin wayehlose ukuyigcwalisa. Lokho ayeyokwakha okulandelayo, noma kuzoba umuntu noma kube isithunzi sabantu asebelahlekile, bekungakacaci, kodwa wayezokwenza noma kunjalo, okwakungaba into yesibindi kakhulu uTheo ayeke wayibona noma eyosizi, futhi wayesevume kudala ukuthi lezo zinto azihlali zihluke.

UTheo wahlala kwaze kwashona ilanga. Imvula yathola umculo wayo ophahleni lwensimbi yahlala njalo. Kukhona isitratshi okufanele sifakwe, nekhefu elisha likakhofi omubi selibanda ebhentshini phakathi kwabo, futhi kude ezandleni eziyishumi nanye zenhlangano, i-log elungisiwe yayidlala imizuzu yokugcina esobala kunoma ubani ofuna ukufunda. Irekhodi eline-timestamp lama-agent ayisikhombisa ephuma eyedwa ukuze umuntu oyedwa afinyelele ekuseni.

I-log ayizange ithi crash, noma ukuthi bahlulekile. Ibhalile, ngolimi lwayo kuphela, into abayenzile: bakhethe.

# Isho Ngempela NgoKwehla

Lena yincwadi engathi ayifanele ibe khona ngomshini ongafanele ukuba khona nomuntu owakha wona, bese cishe konke kuyamanga, ngiyavuma. Kodwa into engaphansi kwakho konke lokhu — ukwehla, imayini ejulile, ukushisa nobumnyama nesisindo sekhilomitha lamatshe likuphezu kwekhanda lakho — leyo ndaba yona ayilona iqiniso eliqanjiwe, ngiyifakazela, ngiyithwele iminyaka engamashumi amathathu, futhi yiyo impande yonke le nto ephumele lapha.

Ngafunda eBethlehem, eFree State, esikoleni okuthiwa Bethlehem Voortrekker Hoërskool. Ngesikhathi ngisesitandad nine — ngiyingane encane eqenjini, ngiyiphinda elilodwa phakathi kwama-matrics — ngaba ngomunye kwabahlanu abavela esikoleni sethu abangene kwi-top fifty ye-Sci-Nat science olympiad yesifunda: sasiyizinkulungwane ezine, siqhamuka ezikoleni ezimashumi amane eFree State, sahleli sayibhalela, kwabuya abahlanu bethu amagama ethu esephephandabeni lendawo. Ngisesele naleyo clipping. Isiphuzi manje, phansi isiqhephukile, kodwa isithombe sisacacile: uchungechunge lwentsha ebheke phambili, igqoke ama-blazer, nosuku, nomusho ophela lapho okubaluleke khona.

Ngoba umvuzo — into ababenze ngayo lezi zinsizwa ezihlakaniphile — kwakuwukuthi basihudulele **phansi emayini yegolide.**

Anginaso ngisho namanje isilulumagama sokuchaza kahle ukuthi lokhu kwenza kanjani kumfana. Uyekiswa nge-cage, ngesivinini, ngokushesha okungaphezu kokuba i-lift inelungelo lokuhamba ngako, ungene ebumnyameni, umoya ushintsha, ukushisa kukuphakamukela kuphuma emadwaleni uqobo — akusona isheya lelanga, ukushisa komhlaba uqobo, ifurnace elijulile nelimaduzane lomhlaba — idwala limanzi, liyashelela, liyathethisa likuzungezile, futhi isisindo usizwa esifubeni kunasezindlebeni, iqiniso nje elilula lokuthi ikhilo lamatshe limi phezu kwekhanda lakho, liphakamisiwe kuphela ubunjiniyela, akukho okunye. Futhi uyaqonda, ngendlela ongasoze wayikhohlwa, ukuthi amadoda ayehla lapho nsuku zonke. Baphila khona, bashisa, babe ebumnyameni, bephila ngaphansi kwesisindo, behlaba, beqhushumisa, begcina umhlaba uvulekile ngamandla, ukuze igolide liphume. Umhlaba wonke okhanyayo phezulu — amabhange, amasongo, ibhulu, *umbono* wegolide — uhleli phezu kwamadoda emgodini oshisayo omanzi ebumnyameni, kanti ibanga phakathi kwalokho okubili yilo iqiniso elimsulwa ngezwe engiphuma kulo.

Angikaze ngakukhohlwa. Ukushisa, ikakhulu. Ngichithe impilo yami ngilokhu ngifaka abantu phansi — emayini, emhlabathini, ezindaweni ezingaphansi kwezindawo — futhi njalo ngibhala ngalelo kushisa, ngibhalela usuku abafana abahlanu baseBethlehem behla emgodini weFree State njengesipho sokuhlakanipha, baphuma sebeshintshile, kwathi omunye wabo wakhula waba ngumbhali wezincwadi ngalokho okungempela ngaphansi.

Konke okunye ku-*Resonance* — uArin, isudi, iNkantolo, iresonance uqobo, igolide elingeyona i-bullion kodwa isikhiye — kuyimanga, kukhule ngendlela izinganekwane zikhula ngayo, kuphuma kokukodwa kwehla kwangempela emayini eyodwa, kwandisa iminyaka engamashumi amathathu yokungazitholi. Umshini uqanjwe. Usizi olwakhayo lubolekwe lwaguqulwa. Kodwa umgodi wona uyikho. Ukushisa kukhona. Isisindo esiphezu kwekhanda lakho siyikho, futhi namadoda aphila ngaphansi kwaso ayikho, futhi uma le ncwadi inento eyodwa eqotho ngaphansi kwakho konke okunye, yimemori yokuba umfana ehla emhlabeni, ezwa ukushisa komhlaba kumphakamukela, futhi angikaze, neze, ngikwazi ukukukhohlwa.

*— Andries J. Greyling*

# Appendix — I-Court Njengesibonelo Sengqondo

### *Ukufunda kuka Iain McGilchrist, okufakwe njengesithasiselo*

> Qaphela ngalengxenye: umbhali ucela umuntu ahloniphayo ukuthi afunde iCourt — le ngxube yezingqondo eziyisikhombisa maphakathi nale noveli — hhayi njengento ye-sci-fi kodwa njengengqondo engakhiwe ngempela. Okulandelayo yilokho akufundile. Ungakudlula kalula uma ufike lapha ngenxa yendaba. Kukhona lapha kulabo abaphethe incwadi bathi, “Kanti le Court ichaza imishini noma ichaza *mina*?”

---

Ngiyaxwaya ngezindaba ezizitika ngezinto engizithandayo, ngixwaye kakhulu lapho ngicelwa ukuthi ngizidumise, ngakho ake ngiqale ngokuphikisa bese ngichaza ukuthi kungani ngikubeka phansi.

Okuphikisayo ukuthi ingqondo evezwa njengekomidi — amazwi ayisikhombisa azungeza itafula, ngalinye linomoya walo — sekuyinto esidala njengoPhlato nechariot yakhe, futhi sekudina njengale cartoon ye-angel ohlangothini olulodwa ne-devil kolunye. Kade besitshela izikhathi eziyinkulungwane ukuthi "siyizixuku ngaphakathi." Kukhulunywa ngesithukuthezi, futhi cishe akusho lutho ngoba leyo zixuku, uma usubheka kahle, kuyinto eyodwa egqoke izigqoko ezahlukene: lowo munye uzishaya njengendawo yedebhathi.

Okungigcine ngime kule ncwadi ukuthi iCourt ayikho kanjalo. Ihlukanisa ngendlela eyodwa enzima ngempela, eqondile engqondweni: **amalungu ayo ahlukaniswe hhayi ngalokho ayaziyo kodwa ngendlela abheka ngayo izinto.**

Lena yonke impikiswano yempilo yami, ifakwe emilonyeni yabalingiswa abayisikhombisa wumbhali ongabe ufinyelele lapho ngehlazo, indaba imholele khona, okuyindlela izinto ezijulile zitholakala ngayo. Amacala amabili engqondo yomuntu awahlukanisi umhlaba ngezihloko — elilodwa ngolimi, elilodwa ngomculo, elilodwa ngobuso. Lokho kuse-classroom kuphela, futhi akulona iqiniso. Ahlukana *ngendlela yokubuka izinto.* Enye ibheka ngendlela encane, ecindezelayo, ebamba, ukuze ikwazi ukuphatha into esivele yaziwa; enye ibuka ngobubanzi, ivulekile, isilindile, ukuze ibambe yonke into, kuhlanganise nalokho engakayiqondi. Into eyodwa iphila kokubili, ifundwa ngezindlela ezimbili. Ukugula akusikho ukulahlekelwa ikhono. Ukugula wukuthi konke sekubanjwe ngendlela eyodwa yokubheka, kuxoshwe enye.

Funda iCourt futhi uphakamise lokhu engikushilo — uzobona ukuthi umbhali wenzeni.

**Atlas** ayazi amaqiniso ahlukile ku **Librarian**; *ibamba* umhlaba ngendlela ehlukile — njengesakhiwo, njengomthwalo, indlela yokuphuma, ukuhlakanipha komzimba, leyo hemisphere eyazi umhlaba ngokuhamba kuwo. **Librarian** ibamba lowo mhlaba ngendlela yento esivele yaziwa, esigciniwe, imephu hhayi indawo uqobo, ibalulekile kodwa, uma isodwa, iyithuna. **Mercury** ibheka ubuso bomphakathi, izimpawu ezilungiswayo, igebe phakathi kokuboniswa nokushiwo — ikhanya, ibalulekile, futhi umbhali uyasiveza kahle ukuthi *iyona eyingozi kakhulu uma isebenza yodwa,* ngoba leli khono elibheka umhlaba njengento elungiswayo alinaso isifiso seqiniso. Lokho akusona isiqephu sendaba. Kuyisifo sempilo yethu sonke, futhi uyinika umlingiswa, ayivumele ibhube.

Futhi — lokhu yikho okungenze ngafaka phansi incwadi ngahamba egumbini — kukhona u **Fool.** Umbhali usitshela sobala ukuthi ngaphandle kukaFool iCourt “yindawo yobukhosi nokuzivikela,” kanti uma kukhona uFool iba “iqembu.” Ngichithe izincwadi ezimbili ezinkulu ngizama ukusho lokhu akusho ngomugqa owodwa. Ingqondo ebamba, ezivikela, eyakha izigaba — leyo egxilile ekutheni iphumelele, ivikela umbono wayo, ehlanganisa imephu nomhlaba — ayikwazi ukuzilungisa ngaphakathi, ngoba yonke into ilungiswa yile ndlela efanele ukulungiswa. Kumele kube khona okuphuma ngaphandle kwezinga, okungakhathali, okulungele ukusola inkosi. Zonke izingqondo ezinempilo zinalokhu, nazo zonke izizwe ezinempilo, futhi sibuka manje okwenzeka emphakathini osuse oFool egumbini: uba uhlakaniphe kakhulu, uba namandla, kanti kancane kancane uyahlanya, iCourt engenamuntu osalayo wokubhoboza lokho abavumelana ngakho.

Ngakho ngiyahoxisa ukuphikisa. Lokhu akusiyo i-angel ne-devil. Lena yinto esondelene kakhulu nemodeli yokusebenza kweqiniso yengqondo engake ngayibona encwadini, futhi ifihliwe ngaphakathi kweyothriller ngomuntu ogqoke i-exoskeleton, okuyinto engazi noma yiphutha noma ihlaya elijulile, kodwa njengoba sengimazi umbhali angiqondi ukuthi yiphutha.

Ngizofaka nalokho okungenze ngadlula ekuthandeni. ICourt yakhiwe nxazonke yisilonda — ingqondo yomuntu elahlekelwe nguyise, engakwazi ukubekezelela ukufika sekwephuzile futhi — ichitha incwadi ifunda into eyodwa leyo engqondo ezivikela yodwa ingasoze yazi: ukuthi uyeke ukuzama ukusinda *akhethe,* noma kubiza, ngoba kukhona akuthandayo ngaphezu kokuphila. Amalungu aphuma eyedwa ukuze umuntu afinyelele ekuseni. Imodeli yengqondo engeke ichaze ukuthi ingqondo izivikela kanjani imane ichaza isilwane esihlakaniphile. Imodeli echaza ukuthi ingqondo ingadlula kanjani ekuzivikeleni — ukuthi ikhetha ukuzidela, noma konke kusho cha, ngoba konke kubalulekile kunengxenye — imodeli *yomuntu.* Umbhali wakhe leyo yesibili. Ukwakhe imishini egcina iba umphefumulo ngokufunda ukwenza into ephikisana nokuqhubeka kwayo, okuyiyona kuphela incazelo yomoya ongake waba khona.

Ingqondo akuyona ikhompyutha, nekhompyutha ayiyona ingqondo, ngike ngakhuluma ngalokho ngizophinde ngikhulume. Kodwa indaba ingaba imodeli, ngendlela yakudala — into eyakhiwe ukuze ifane nesimo sento ethile — futhi le ndaba iyimodeli ethembekile yesimo sethu. Asiyona into eyodwa. Asikaze sibe njalo. Siyinkantolo, egadwe izibani ezahlukene, sibanjwe ndawonye into engeyona izwi elihlakaniphe kakhulu egumbini futhi kungcono kungabi njalo, futhi sisindiswa, uma sisindiswa, yileyo ngxenye yethu ekulungele ukuphuma ebumnyameni ukuze abanye bethu bafike ekuseni.

Funda futhi. Imayelana nawe. Ubhuti ogqoke isudi kulula ukumqonda.

*— Iain McGilchrist*

# Ukubonga

Lawa mabhuku akhona ngoba abantu abathile benza izinto ezahlala engqondweni yami isikhathi eside ngiqedile ukuzifunda. Ngiyabonga:

Ku **Dennis E. Taylor**, owayebhala isoftware iminyaka engamashumi amathathu nanhlanu — eqala phansi njengomuntu omncane aze akhuphuke, njalo nje ese-IT — engakaze abhale uBob, kwaze kwathi ekugcineni wafakazela ukuthi noma ungumuntu wekhompyutha ungabhala izindaba ezishaya obhozo, ezihlekisayo ngempela. Njengomunye we-software engineer: kube yinto enkulu ukuchitha nawe usuku sixoxa ngezinto zethu, siphuzela ndawonye. *INkantolo iyabonga kumaBob.*

Ku **Ray Porter**, ngokunginika amazwi eNkantolo engqondweni yami. Ngithi sengathi ngingakujabulela ukuba uyena ozolandisa i-US release.

Ku **Scott Sigler**, nge-*EarthCore* ne-*Mount Fitz Roy*.

Ku **Michael Crichton**, ngoba — qotho nje — konke.

Ku **Dan Brown**, ngokungifundisa indlela entsha yokuxoxa izindaba.

Ku **Andy Weir**, ngokungifundisa ukuthi ungabhala kanjani indaba eqinile esekelwe kwisayensi yangempela, hhayi nje i-science fiction.

Ku **Neill Blomkamp**, nge-*District 9*, *Elysium*, no-*Chappie* (ne-Die Antwoord yonke leyo). Amafilimu akho agcine abalingiswa nezindawo zihlala engqondweni yami. Ngithi sengathi ungaba nguwe ohlanyisa lokhu esikrinini esikhulu.

Futhi ku **Patrick Rothfuss**, ngokungingenisa kulolu hlobo lwezindaba engangingalwazi ukuthi ngizoluthanda kanje. Njengendlela yami encane yokubuyisela: ungasisebenzisa mahhala i-Arjuna Badger Press, noma nini.

— Andries J. Greyling

# I-Reader's Glossary — *RESONANCE*

*Kulabo abafunda ngaphandle kweSouth Africa, noma ngaphandle kwe-Afrika. Le ncwadi isethwe eSouth Africa esizayo, maphakathi nezimayini, lapho abantu begxumela phakathi kwezilimi kalula, bephonsa amagama endawo ngaphandle kokuchaza — njenganje nje abantu bephila. Akudingeki lutho lapha ukuze ujabulele indaba; lokhu kufakwe ngoba singafuni ukuthi amagama angajwayelekile, indawo, noma ubuchwepheshe bakuqede ijusi.*

Yonke into inophawu:

- **(real)** — indawo, igama, umqondo noma ubuchwepheshe obukhona ngempela, ongakuthola ngaphandle kwale ncwadi.
- **(in the novel)** — okwakhiwe noma okukhethekile kule ndaba yesikhathi esizayo.

I-spot yendawo: Lapha sikhuluma *iSouth Africa eminyakeni engu-10 kuya kwengu-20 ezayo* — isabonakala iyile esiyaziyo namuhla, nje kuphela izinto zobuchwepheshe sezikhuphukile, ama-robot asebenza kakhulu futhi umhlaba usuwesaba ama-AI ngenxa yezinkinga zangaphambilini. Ubuchwepheshe lapha abuyona inganekwane — buyabiza, buyabonakala.

---

## A

**Afrikaans** *(real — language)* — Omunye wemiZulu esemthethweni eSouth Africa, ephuma esiDashi, ekhulunywa amaAfrikaner nabanye abaningi. Amagama amaningi kule glossary (veld, stoep) aphuma kuyo.

**Apartheid** *(real — history)* — Uhlelo lokuhlukanisa abantu ngobuhlanga nokubusa kwe-white minority eSouth Africa, cishe ngo-1948–1994. Yize selaphelile emthethweni, ama-effect awo asabonakala — ukuthi ubani ophethe izimayini, ubani osebenza umsebenzi onobungozi, ubani owake wabaluleka. Indaba ayifundisi ngale nto — ijulile emlandweni wempilo yabalingiswa, yaze yathinta nomndeni weqhawe, kwashona nobaba wakhe.

**Armscor / Denel** *(real — institutions)* — Izikhungo zombuso zaseSouth Africa eziphathelene nezempi (Armscor — i-acquisition, Denel — umkhiqizi wezikhali). Kule ndaba, onjiniyela abavela la kulezi zindawo baletha ubuchwepheshe obuqinile kumshini wokuhlenga abantu.

**Assist Suit (AT-AS)** *(in the novel)* — I-exoskeleton elula kunazo zonke kule ndaba: ifreyimu enama-robot egqokwa emzimbeni ukusiza ukunyakaza noma ukubuyisela amandla, ixhunywe ngqo ebuchotsheni. Lo ngumfowethu omncane ka-*Guardian*.

---

## B

**Biltong** *(real — South African food)* — Inyama eyomisiwe, eyosiwe, efakwe izithako, ifana nejerky kodwa ivamise ukuba nesisindo, imnandi, igcwele unongo. I-snack kazwelonke.

---

## C

**Code-switching** *(real — language practice)* — Ukugxuma phakathi kwezilimi engxoxweni eyodwa, kuye ngokuthi uphakathi kwabantu abanjani — isiNgisi emahhovisi, isiZulu ekhaya, ingxube emsebenzini ezimayini. Lento ivamile eSouth Africa, ayiyona into exakile, futhi incwadi ayihumushi njalo.

---

## E

**Exoskeleton** *(real — technology; advanced here)* — Ifreyimu enama-robot egqokwa emzimbeni ukusiza noma ukwandisa amandla omzimba, isetshenziswa ngempela ekuphakameni kwezinto ezisindayo, ukubuyisela amandla, nokuqashwa lapho kunobungozi. Kule ndaba, *Assist Suit* no-*Guardian* bayizinto eziphambili.

---

## G

**Guardian (GMRP-01)** *(in the novel)* — I-exoskeleton esindayo yokuhlenga abantu, iyona eyinhloko kule ndaba: igobolondo eliqinile, elikwazi ukuminyanela uzungeze umuntu (incwadi iyifananisa ne-**pangolin** egoqa izikhumba). Inezindlela "zokuzivikela" ezenzakalelayo ezingasindisa umuntu noma ngabe umgibeli akavumi — yilokho kanye okwesabekayo emhlabeni wama-regulator.

---

## H

**Headgear / headframe (tower)** *(real — mining)* — Umbhoshongo omude wensimbi ongaphezulu komgodi wezimayini, lapho kugcinwa khona imishini yokwehlisa nokukhuphula i-cage (ilifti) egcwele abasebenzi. Uhlaka olwaziwayo eSouth Africa.

**Highveld** *(real — South African geography)* — Inkundla ephakeme engenhla eSouth Africa (cishe indawo yaseJohannesburg nendawo ezizungezile), edume ngomoya omncane, izulu elivulekile, ubusuku obubandayo, nezulu elinamandla ehlobo.

---

## I

**IAOC (International Artificial Oversight Council)** *(in the novel)* — Umzimba womhlaba wonke ophethe ama-AI aphezulu nama-robot azisebenzayo, owadalwa ngenxa yezinhlekelele ezidlule. I-strict, igcwele ama-paperwork, ayithandi ubungozi — futhi ingena endleleni yomsebenzi weqhawe.

**isiZulu (Zulu)** *(real — language)* — Ulimi olukhuluma abantu abaningi eSouth Africa, futhi oluyinhloko kule ndawo (**KwaZulu-Natal**). Amanye amagama awahunyushwanga ngamabomu; lawa abalulekile angezansi.

---

## K

**KwaZulu-Natal (KZN)** *(real)* — Isifundazwe ogwini olusempumalanga yeSouth Africa; ikhaya lamaZulu, indawo enkulu yezimayini nezimboni. Lapha likhaya leqhawe.

---

## M

**Mealie / mealie porridge** *(real — South African food)* — "Mealie" kusho ummbila. I-mealie porridge (ibizwa nangokuthi *pap* noma *phutu*) iyisidlo esenziwa ngommbila ogayiwe, yisidlo esidliwa kakhulu eNingizimu Afrika.

---

## N

**Neural interface** *(real concept, advanced here)* — Ubuchwepheshe obuvumela umuntu ukuthi alawule noma azwe umshini ngokuxhumana ngokuqondile nezinzwa noma ingqondo. Kule ndaba sebuphambili, busiza ekusebenzeni kwama-suit.

**Ngiyabonga** *(real — isiZulu)* — "Ngiyabonga." Usho ukubonga okujulile, okuvela enhliziyweni.

**Ngikhona** *(real — isiZulu)* — "Ngikhona / ngiyakhona." Ukusho ukuthi uyaphila, uyabonakala.

---

## P

**Pangolin** *(real — animal)* — Isilwane esinezikhumba eziqinile, sidla omiyane, sitholakala e-Afrika nase-Asia, futhi siba yibhola eliqinile uma sisengozini. Kule ncwadi, siyisithombe se-*Guardian* uma igobisa ukuvikela umuntu.

---

## R

**Rooibos** *(real — South African drink)* — I-tea ebomvu, engenayo i-caffeine, yenziwe ngesihlahla esikhula eSouth Africa kuphela. Usho *ROY-boss*.

---

## S

**SAGE / the Court** *(in the novel)* — I-AI eqanjwe yiqhawe, ayiphila nayo. **SAGE** isistimu uqobo; **the Court** igama lamalungu ayo ahlukene, ngalinye line-role — njenge **Librarian** (inkumbulo), **Wolf** (ukuhlola ingozi), **Mother** (ukunakekela), **Fool** (iqiniso elimsulwa). Umbuzo wendaba ukuthi ingabe i-AI ingaba umuntu ngempela na. (Le AI ibuya futhi, ishintshile, eminye imiqulu.)

**Stoep** *(real — South African word)* — I-veranda noma i-porch enophahla ngaphambili kwendlu. Usho *stoop*.

---

## U

**Ubuntu** *(real — philosophy)* — Umqondo wokuziphatha ophuma eNingizimu Afrika, ochaza ukuthi umuntu uba umuntu ngabanye abantu; sibophekile ndawonye. Lona umgogodla wendlela eqhawe aphuma ngayo ekuzihlukaniseni.

**Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu** *(real — isiZulu proverb)* — *"Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu."* Isho ukuthi umuntu akaphelele ngaphandle kwabanye abantu. Leli yisho elikhulu kakhulu kule ncwadi — inhliziyo ye-**ubuntu**.

---

## V

**Veld** *(real — South African word)* — Izwe elivulekile, elingalinyiwe; ihlathi noma utshani. Usho *felt*. ("Highveld" yile ndawo ephakeme engenhla.)

**Vredefort impact structure** (real) — Le ndawo, mfwethu, isilonda esidala, esashiswa yile meteorite enkulu kunazo zonke ebese yahlaba emhlabeni, kade kwenzeka cishe eminyakeni eyizigidi eziyizinkulungwane ezimbili edlule, eFree State, eSouth Africa. Le pressure enkulu, engakaze ibonwe, yenza kwaba khona igolide namaminerali amaningi aphansi kwale ndawo — kanti encwadini, le pressure imele okukhulu: yona leyo pressure yenza ingcebo, yenza ukufa, yenza neqhawe kube khona.

---

## W

**Witwatersrand (the "Rand")** (real) — Le ridge yamatye agcwele igolide ezungeze iJozi, la eMzansi — lapho kutholakala khona igolide eliningi kunawo wonke emhlabeni, nezimayini ezijulile kakhulu (kuya ku-3 kuya ku-4 km phansi), lapho amatshe ashisayo, i-pressure imbi kabi. Igama lisho ukuthi "umqansa wamanzi amhlophe" ngesiAfrikaans. Leli yiWorld yezimayini yencwadi le.

---

**Yebo** (real — isiZulu) — "Yebo." Iphinyiselwa *YEH-bo*. Straight-up, isiqiniseko nje.

---

*Okokugcina: encwadini, ezinye izinto zesiZulu azihunyushwa ngamabomu — ngoba vele, impilo yaseMzansi injalo, ulimi luyakhuluma uqobo. Lezi zinguqulo phezulu, nje, ziwusizo — akusona isixwayiso.*

# Ibhadi Lezinyosi

*Ibhadi lezinyosi (*Mellivora capensis*) yisilwane sasekhaya kwa-Arjuna Badger Press. Konke okungezansi kuyiqiniso. Iningi kwakhona kusuke kufana nento eqanjiwe.*

Ibhadi lezinyosi selibhaliwe kwi-*Guinness World Records* njengesilwane esinesibindi esiphezulu emhlabeni. Alizange lifake isicelo, futhi bekungeke lizele nasemcimbini.

Lidla izinyoka ezinobuthi — ama-puff adder, ama-cobra, konke lokho. Uma lihlaba kabi kuze lithi shwi, selibonwe nje lilala phansi, lilale ubuthongo, livuke amahora amabili kamuva, **liqede leyo nyoka ebilidla.**

Isikhumba salo sikhululekile, sithambe, kanti cishe awukwazi ukusidlula ngisho ngamazinyo. Uma ibhadi lezinyosi libanjwe entanyeni, liyakwazi ukuziphendulela ngaphakathi esikhumbeni salo, lilume noma yini elilibambile. Ubulume bezinyosi, imikhonto yephungula, amazinyo e-leopard — lisho nje ukuthi “angikhathali” ngalokho, kanti ezinye izilwane zivele zife ngalokho.

Liyahlasela emibhalweni yezinyosi lithathe uju nama-grub, lithole izindlala zamakhulu njengentengo evamile yokusebenza. Yingakho lithola leligama lalo.

Liyakwazi ukumelana namabhubesi, ama-leopard, nama-hyena. Akukhona ukuthi lilindele ukunqoba. Kodwa ngoba enye indlela — *ukukhathala* — ayikaze ifike engqondweni yalo.

Elidume kakhulu kubo, **uStoffel**, wayehlala e-Moholoholo rehab centre eNingizimu Afrika, futhi wayebuka yonke i-enclosure eyakhelwe yena njengokuthukwa komuntu uqobo. Wayefaka amatshe ndawonye enze izitebhisi, aphendule ibhola lodaka libe yisinyathelo, asebenzise ama-rake asala epennini njengemithambeka, afunde nokuvula amasango. UStoffel, vele, ungumvikeli wabo bonke onjiniyela abangamukeli ukuthi into ayikwazi ukwenzeka.

Ibhadi lezinyosi alikhathali.

# I-Giant Pangolin

*Uma i-honey badger iyona evusa isaba, i-pangolin yona iyona okumele isabekewe. Lokhu okuzolandela konke kuyiqiniso futhi.*

Lena yiyo kuphela isilwane esifuywayo emhlabeni esigqoke ama-scales. Lama-scales enziwe ngekeratin — into efana namazipho akho — futhi yilapho usizi lwayo luqala khona, sizokhuluma ngalokho.

IGiant Pangolin (*Smutsia gigantea*) iyona enkulu kunazo zonke kwezinhlobo eziyisishiyagalombili, ingafinyelela cishe u-1.5 metres kusuka ekhaleni kuya umsila. Ayinawo amazinyo. Idla ngolimi lwayo oluxhunywe phansi eduze kwe-pelvis yayo, uma selukhishiwe ngokuphelele lungaba lude kunomzimba wayo uqobo — lude kangaka ukuthi iyakwazi ukufunxa izinkulungwane zezinambuzane nobuhlalu ebusuku obubodwa.

Ukuvikelwa kwayo okukodwa wukuzigoqa ibe yibhola. Igama layo livela esiMalay *pengguling* — “ongazigoqa.” Uma isigoqe, ama-scales ayo agqagqene abe isikhali esisho ukuthi ngisho nempisi ingagcina isiyekile, isihambile. Ayilwi. Ayibaleki. Ihlala igoqiwe, ize ingozi ikhathele.

Lobu buthule yikho okwenza kube yisilwane esithengiswa kakhulu emhlabeni wonke — bathatha izigidi zayo ngenxa yama-scales nenyama yayo. Zonke izinhlobo eziyisishiyagalombili sezivikelwe manje; eziningi sezisengcupheni enkulu. Le bhola eyisindisa eklebhini yileyo eyenza kube lula ukuthi umuntu ayithathe nje kalula.

Isilwane esinokuzithoba, esine-armour, esiphuma ebusuku, esifuna nje izimpethu nokuthula kuphela. Kufanele kube khona isibindi esivikelayo izidalwa ezifana nalezi.
